# Godot Diorama Client Implementation Plan (sub-project 4)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The game the player opens: a god's-eye diorama of their world that catches up on launch, keeps living while open, and lets them read any part of its history and *why* it happened, through the four designed screens (morning digest, map HUD, character page, why chain), pages for families, realms, artifacts and the full chronicle, and war drawn like a strategy map with marching banners.

**Architecture:** `sim/FantasyWorld.Client` is an engine-free class library holding everything testable: the session over a world file (catch-up on a worker thread behind one lock, live stepping, saving, view models), the terrain as continuous fields and mesh arrays, scattering of trees and houses, marker and border layout, label decluttering, the orbit camera, the router and the palette. `game/` is a Godot 4.3 .NET project whose nodes only turn those into meshes and controls. A headless check scene drives every screen on a fresh world and exits 0/1 (CI runs it); a showcase scene renders a full-size world for visual review. Two simulation changes ride along: armies used to trade places forever instead of meeting (Task 9), and nobody was ever knighted, so battles touched only their commanders (Task 12).

**Tech Stack:** .NET 8 / C# 12, xUnit (unchanged); new: Godot 4.3 .NET (`Godot.NET.Sdk/4.3.0`) for `game/`; fonts Instrument Serif and Geist (SIL Open Font License), downloaded from the Google Fonts repository.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-godot-diorama-client-design.md`. **Builds on:** the sim core (Plans 1–6) and the chronicle views (sub-project 2).

## Global Constraints

Everything in the earlier plans' Global Constraints still applies. In addition:

- **Godot nodes hold no game logic.** Anything that can be tested without Godot lives in `FantasyWorld.Client` with xUnit tests; nodes build meshes and controls from it.
- **The simulation never runs on the main thread:** catch-up, live steps, the digest (which may ask the LLM to narrate) and the heavy meshes are built on workers; Godot objects are made on the main thread from their results. Closing cancels a catch-up and keeps the days already simulated.
- **Every visible name is a link** and every link opens a page; Back always returns.
- **Colours are the design's tokens** (`Palette`), given in sRGB and converted to linear for vertex and instance colours.
- `game/.godot/` and `*.import` are generated by Godot and ignored by git.
- Commit messages end with: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`

## Decisions made while drafting (review these)

| Topic | Drafted choice | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Rendering of markers | Godot-free layout (`Markers`, `Scatter`) + Godot `MultiMesh` for trees and houses | Thousands of instances at 60 fps; layout testable in xUnit |
| Picking | Nearest projected marker within 16 px, no physics | Simple, deterministic, testable headless |
| Terrain look | Continuous fields blurred from the tile grid, ridged relief for mountains (visual only) | The tile grid read as blocks; the simulation's elevation is untouched |
| Borders | Tile edges chained, simplified (Douglas–Peucker) and rounded (Chaikin), drawn as constant-screen-width dashes with a halo | Staircase borders were ugly up close and invisible from afar |
| Labels | Greedy declutter by importance (news, then cities, towns, villages), reserving the cards | Tags piled up at region zoom |
| Pages vs diorama | Documents (character, why, family, realm, artifact, chronicle) cover the diorama, which stops drawing | Legibility, and no GPU cost while reading |
| Live play | One step every 2 s on a worker, at most one in flight; diorama refreshed each world day; events of importance ≥ 40 announced | Spec: 1 real day = 1 world year; the CLI's live pin threshold |
| Sim fix | Armies engage an enemy host of the same war in their lands before retaking regions; wars cite the realms' latest grievance; a fallen realm's remaining land passes to its occupier or neighbour | Found through the character page; 59 liberations in one war with no battles |
| Knights | Each realm knights up to two martial adults a year (one per two settlements, at most eight); battles name them, wound them and give glory | No code knighted anyone, so hosts had no knights |
| War style | Strategy-map marks: tapered arrows (attackers red, defenders blue), toothed fronts, hatched occupation, waving banners that march, crossed swords | Requested reference: a strategy map with arrows, front teeth and hatching |
| Plan size | 13 tasks (the spec outlined 9) | The production look, the sim fixes and the war layer earned their own tasks |

**Verification:** each task's xUnit tests fail first and pass after its implementation, and from Task 4 on the headless Godot checks pass with the counts given (the code was replayed task by task in this order). The look was reviewed from screenshots and a film of a full-size world. An independent review of the prototype found that blocking LLM calls and live steps ran on Godot's main thread (a deadlock with Claude on), that closing during catch-up let the world run ahead of real time, that backups rotated before a world was known to load, that clicks on linked text never reached rows and cards, and that hosts chased armies of other wars; all are fixed in the code below and covered by tests or checks.

---

### Task 1: The client session

Spec §2: the game never touches the simulation directly. A `Session` opens (rotating backups) or creates a world with its prehistory, catches up on a worker thread with monthly progress, steps live while open, saves, and builds every screen's view model. One lock guards the simulation: the worker holds it while simulating, and the UI's `TryRead` returns null instead of waiting, so a frame never stalls. Backups move from the CLI into the engine library so the game rotates them too, and the digest gains `ForReader` (everything since the reader last looked, not yet marked seen).

**Files:**
- Create: `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/FantasyWorld.Client.csproj`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Session.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Persist/Backups.cs`
- Modify: `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/AnthropicProvider.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/OpenRouterProvider.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Engine/CatchUp.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.sln`
- Delete (moved): `sim/FantasyWorld.Cli/Backups.cs`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/SessionTests.cs`
- Test (modify): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.csproj`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `WorldGenerator`, `Prehistory`, `CatchUp`, `LiveHost`, `WorldStore`, `LlmGateway`, the LLM deciders (Plans 1–6); the view models (sub-project 2).
- Produces: project `sim/FantasyWorld.Client` (net8 class library; in the solution and referenced by the tests); `sealed class Session : IDisposable { Create(path, seed, size, clock, prehistoryYears?, progress?); Open(path, clock, providers?); DaysBehind; Busy; CatchUpAsync(progress?, ct); Step(); NextDayAt; Read(view); TryRead(view); Digest(); MarkSeen(); Map(year?); Character(id); Family(id); Why(eventId, depth); Chronicle(from?, to?, only?, page); Realm(id); Artifact(id); IsFollowed(entity); Follow(entity, follow) }`; `Backups` moved to `FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist`; `DigestScreen.ForReader(world, log, followed, daysBehind, narrator)`.

`sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/SessionTests.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Support;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class SessionTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset T0 = new(2026, 10, 6, 8, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    private sealed class Clock
    {
        public DateTimeOffset Now { get; set; } = T0;
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ASession_CatchesUpOnAWorker_ThenLivesAndSaves()
    {
        using var file = new TempFile();
        var clock = new Clock();
        using (var created = Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => clock.Now)) { }

        clock.Now = T0.AddHours(8);
        using (var session = Session.Open(file.Path, () => clock.Now))
        {
            Assert.Equal(120, session.DaysBehind);
            var months = new List<WorldDate>();
            var result = await session.CatchUpAsync(new Progress<WorldDate>(months.Add));
            Assert.Equal(120, result.DaysAdvanced);
            Assert.Equal(0, session.DaysBehind);
            Assert.False(session.Busy);

            clock.Now = clock.Now.AddMinutes(4);
            Assert.Equal(1, session.Step().Days);
            Assert.Equal(0, session.Step().Days);
            Assert.Equal(clock.Now.AddMinutes(4), session.NextDayAt);
            Assert.NotNull(session.TryRead((w, _) => w.Name));
        }
        Assert.True(File.Exists(Backups.PathOf(file.Path, 1)));
        using var reopened = WorldStore.Open(file.Path);
        Assert.Equal(121, reopened.Load().World.Date.Day);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheDigest_IsReadOnce_AndFollowingLeadsIt()
    {
        using var file = new TempFile();
        var clock = new Clock();
        using var session = Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => clock.Now, prehistoryYears: 5);

        var digest = session.Digest();
        Assert.NotNull(digest.Headline);
        Assert.Equal("Caught up · Year 6, day 1", digest.Status);
        session.MarkSeen();
        Assert.Null(session.Digest().Headline);

        var lord = session.Read((w, _) => w.Settlements.Values.First(s => s.LordId != 0).LordId);
        session.Follow(new EntityRef("char", lord));
        Assert.True(session.Character(lord).Followed);
        Assert.NotEmpty(session.Map().Settlements);
        Assert.NotEmpty(session.Map(year: 2).Regions);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OpeningAMissingWorld_IsAClearError()
    {
        Assert.Throws<FileNotFoundException>(() => Session.Open("/nowhere/none.fw.db", () => T0));
        using var file = new TempFile();
        using (Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => T0)) { }
        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => T0));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ACancelledCatchUp_KeepsTheWorldClockInStepWithTheDaysSimulated()
    {
        using var file = new TempFile();
        var clock = new Clock();
        using (Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => clock.Now)) { }
        clock.Now = T0.AddDays(20); // 20 real days: 20 world years owed
        using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
        using (var session = Session.Open(file.Path, () => clock.Now))
        {
            var owed = session.DaysBehind;
            var before = session.Read((w, _) => w.Date.Day);
            var progress = new Progress<WorldDate>(_ => cts.Cancel());
            await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(() => session.CatchUpAsync(progress, cts.Token));
            var simulated = session.Read((w, _) => w.Date.Day) - before;
            Assert.InRange(simulated, 1, owed - 1);
            Assert.Equal(owed - simulated, session.DaysBehind);
        }
        using var reopened = Session.Open(file.Path, () => clock.Now);
        Assert.True(reopened.DaysBehind > 0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWorldThatFailsToLoad_DoesNotPushOutTheBackups()
    {
        using var file = new TempFile();
        var clock = new Clock();
        using (Session.Create(file.Path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => clock.Now)) { }
        using (Session.Open(file.Path, () => clock.Now)) { }
        var backup = Backups.PathOf(file.Path, 1);
        Assert.True(File.Exists(backup));
        var good = File.ReadAllBytes(backup);
        File.WriteAllText(file.Path, "not a world");
        Assert.ThrowsAny<Exception>(() => Session.Open(file.Path, () => clock.Now));
        Assert.Equal(good, File.ReadAllBytes(backup));
        Assert.False(File.Exists(Backups.PathOf(file.Path, 2)));
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Session.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>
/// One open world for the game client (sub-project 4 §2): catch-up on launch on a worker thread, live stepping while
/// open, saving, and the screens' view models. The simulation is guarded by one lock: the worker holds it while it
/// simulates, the UI uses <see cref="TryRead{T}"/> so a frame never waits on the simulation.
/// </summary>
public sealed class Session : IDisposable
{
    private readonly object _gate = new();
    private readonly WorldStore _store;
    private readonly Simulator _sim;
    private readonly LlmGateway _gateway;
    private readonly LiveHost _live;
    private readonly Func<DateTimeOffset> _clock;
    private bool _disposed;

    private Session(string path, WorldStore store, Simulator sim, LlmGateway gateway, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock)
    {
        Path = path;
        _store = store;
        _sim = sim;
        _gateway = gateway;
        _live = new LiveHost(sim);
        _clock = clock;
    }

    public string Path { get; }

    /// <summary>Makes a new world file with its prehistory (default by size) and opens it.</summary>
    public static Session Create(string path, ulong seed, WorldSize size, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock, int? prehistoryYears = null,
        IProgress<WorldDate>? progress = null)
    {
        if (File.Exists(path)) throw new InvalidOperationException($"{path} already exists.");
        var world = WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(seed, size), clock());
        var history = Prehistory.Run(world, prehistoryYears ?? Prehistory.DefaultYearsFor(size), progress);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(System.IO.Path.GetDirectoryName(System.IO.Path.GetFullPath(path))!);
        // Written beside the target and moved into place, so a launch killed mid-way never leaves a half-made world.
        var partial = path + ".creating";
        if (File.Exists(partial)) File.Delete(partial);
        using (var store = WorldStore.Open(partial)) store.Save(world, history.Log, history.Queue);
        File.Move(partial, path);
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (File.Exists(partial + suffix)) File.Move(partial + suffix, path + suffix);
        return Open(path, clock, rotate: false);
    }

    /// <summary>Opens a world (rotating its backups first) with LLM agency when its settings and credentials allow.</summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// The world is loaded before the backups rotate, so a file that fails to load never pushes the last good copies
    /// out; a world just created has nothing to back up yet.
    /// </remarks>
    public static Session Open(string path, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?>? providers = null, bool rotate = true)
    {
        if (!File.Exists(path)) throw new FileNotFoundException($"No world file at {path}.", path);
        var store = WorldStore.Open(path);
        try
        {
            var (world, log, queue) = store.Load();
            var settings = store.LoadLlmSettings();
            var gateway = new LlmGateway(settings.Enabled ? (providers ?? (s => LlmProviders.Create(s)))(settings) : null, settings, store.LoadJournal());
            if (rotate) Backups.Rotate(store, path);
            var story = new StoryContext(world, log, gateway);
            var sim = new Simulator(world, log, queue, DefaultSystems.Create(new LlmDecider(story), new LlmArbiter(story), new LlmInventor(story)));
            return new Session(path, store, sim, gateway, clock);
        }
        catch
        {
            store.Dispose();
            throw;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>World days owed to real time (what catch-up will simulate, at most 30 years at a time).</summary>
    public long DaysBehind => Read((w, _) => Math.Max(0, TimeScale.WholeWorldDays(_clock() - w.LastRealUtc)));

    public bool Busy { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>Simulates the real time owed on a worker thread and saves; progress reports each new month.</summary>
    public Task<CatchUpResult> CatchUpAsync(IProgress<WorldDate>? progress = null, CancellationToken ct = default) =>
        Task.Run(() =>
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                Busy = true;
                try
                {
                    var result = CatchUp.Run(_sim, _clock(), progress, ct);
                    Save();
                    return result;
                }
                finally
                {
                    Busy = false;
                }
            }
        }, ct);

    /// <summary>One live step (call every few seconds): simulates any day now due and saves if one was.</summary>
    public LiveTick Step()
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var tick = _live.Step(_clock());
            if (tick.Days > 0) Save();
            return tick;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>When the next world day falls due.</summary>
    public DateTimeOffset NextDayAt => Read((_, _) => _live.NextDayAt);

    /// <summary>Builds a view under the lock, waiting for the simulation if it is mid-step.</summary>
    public T Read<T>(Func<World, EventLog, T> view)
    {
        lock (_gate) return view(_sim.World, _sim.Log);
    }

    /// <summary>Builds a view only if the simulation is idle; null while it is busy (keep showing the last one).</summary>
    public T? TryRead<T>(Func<World, EventLog, T> view) where T : class
    {
        if (!Monitor.TryEnter(_gate)) return null;
        try
        {
            return view(_sim.World, _sim.Log);
        }
        finally
        {
            Monitor.Exit(_gate);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The digest for the returning reader. Narration may call the LLM: build it off the UI thread.</summary>
    public DigestScreen Digest() => Read((w, l) => DigestScreen.ForReader(w, l, _store.LoadFollowed(), DaysOwed(w), new LlmNarrator(new StoryContext(w, l, _gateway))));

    /// <summary>The reader has seen everything so far.</summary>
    public void MarkSeen()
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _sim.World.LastSeenEventId = _sim.Log.NextId - 1;
            Save();
        }
    }

    public MapScreen Map(int? year = null) =>
        Read((w, l) => MapScreen.Build(w, l, year is { } y ? new WorldDate((long)y * WorldDate.DaysPerYear - 1) : null));

    public CharacterScreen Character(long id) => Read((w, l) => CharacterScreen.Build(w, l, id, _store.LoadFollowed().Contains(new EntityRef(Sim.Model.Character.Kind, id))));

    public FamilyTree Family(long id) => Read((w, _) => FamilyTree.Build(w, id));

    public WhyScreen Why(long eventId, int depth = 4) => Read((w, l) => WhyScreen.Build(w, l, eventId, depth));

    public ChronicleScreen Chronicle(int? fromYear = null, int? toYear = null, Category? only = null, int page = 1) =>
        Read((w, l) => ChronicleScreen.Build(w, l, fromYear, toYear, only, 0, page));

    public RealmScreen Realm(long id) => Read((w, l) => RealmScreen.Build(w, l, id));

    public ArtifactScreen Artifact(long id) => Read((w, l) => ArtifactScreen.Build(w, l, id));

    public bool IsFollowed(EntityRef entity)
    {
        lock (_gate) return _store.LoadFollowed().Contains(entity);
    }

    public void Follow(EntityRef entity, bool follow = true)
    {
        lock (_gate) _store.Follow(entity, follow);
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (_disposed) return;
        lock (_gate)
        {
            Save();
            _store.Dispose();
            _disposed = true;
        }
    }

    private long DaysOwed(World world) => Math.Max(0, TimeScale.WholeWorldDays(_clock() - world.LastRealUtc));

    private void Save() => _store.Save(_sim.World, _sim.Log, _sim.Queue, _gateway.Journal);
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/AnthropicProvider.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using Anthropic;
using Anthropic.Models.Messages;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>
/// Claude through the official Anthropic C# SDK (spec §8.5). Credentials are resolved by the SDK from the
/// <c>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</c> environment variable. The world bible is the cached system prefix; answers use
/// structured outputs (JSON schema). Thinking is always on for Claude Opus 5.5, so effort sets the depth:
/// low for story beats, medium for narration.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AnthropicProvider(AnthropicClient? client = null) : ILlmProvider
{
    /// <summary>Thinking shares the output budget, so the answer's own budget gets generous headroom.</summary>
    public const int ThinkingHeadroom = 8000;

    private AnthropicClient? _client = client;

    public string Name => "anthropic";

    public static MessageCreateParams BuildParams(LlmRequest request, string model) => new()
    {
        Model = model,
        MaxTokens = request.MaxTokens + ThinkingHeadroom,
        System = new List<TextBlockParam> { new() { Text = request.System, CacheControl = new CacheControlEphemeral() } },
        OutputConfig = new OutputConfig
        {
            Effort = request.Tier == ModelTier.Narrate ? Effort.Medium : Effort.Low,
            Format = new JsonOutputFormat { Schema = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, JsonElement>>(request.Schema)! },
        },
        Messages = [new() { Role = Role.User, Content = request.Prompt }],
    };

    /// <summary>A game must not stall on one story beat: one retry, a minute at most per attempt.</summary>
    public static AnthropicClient DefaultClient() => new() { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60), MaxRetries = 1 };

    public async Task<LlmResponse> CompleteJsonAsync(LlmRequest request, string model, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        LlmResponse response;
        bool refused;
        try
        {
            _client ??= DefaultClient();
            var message = await _client.Messages.Create(BuildParams(request, model), ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
            refused = message.StopReason == StopReason.Refusal;
            var json = string.Concat(message.Content.Select(b => b.Value).OfType<TextBlock>().Select(t => t.Text));
            var usage = message.Usage;
            var (cacheRead, cacheWrite) = (usage.CacheReadInputTokens ?? 0, usage.CacheCreationInputTokens ?? 0);
            response = new LlmResponse(json, Name, model, usage.InputTokens, usage.OutputTokens, cacheRead, cacheWrite,
                Price.For(model).Cost(usage.InputTokens, usage.OutputTokens, cacheRead, cacheWrite));
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            // Network, auth, timeouts, rate limits, malformed responses: all mean "not now".
            throw new LlmUnavailableException($"Claude is unavailable: {e.Message}", e);
        }
        if (refused) throw new LlmRefusedException("Claude declined this request.", response);
        return response;
    }
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/OpenRouterProvider.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>
/// Any model behind OpenRouter's chat-completions HTTP API (spec §8.5), with JSON-schema structured output.
/// The key comes from <c>OPENROUTER_API_KEY</c>. Cost is OpenRouter's reported cost when given, else the price table.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OpenRouterProvider(string apiKey, HttpClient? http = null) : ILlmProvider
{
    public const string Endpoint = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions";

    private readonly HttpClient _http = http ?? new HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2) };

    public string Name => "openrouter";

    /// <summary>OpenRouter names models by vendor; a bare Claude model name means Anthropic's.</summary>
    public static string Route(string model) => model.Contains('/', StringComparison.Ordinal) ? model : "anthropic/" + model;

    public static JsonObject BuildBody(LlmRequest request, string model) => new()
    {
        ["model"] = Route(model),
        ["max_tokens"] = request.MaxTokens + AnthropicProvider.ThinkingHeadroom,
        ["messages"] = new JsonArray
        {
            new JsonObject { ["role"] = "system", ["content"] = request.System },
            new JsonObject { ["role"] = "user", ["content"] = request.Prompt },
        },
        ["response_format"] = new JsonObject
        {
            ["type"] = "json_schema",
            ["json_schema"] = new JsonObject { ["name"] = request.Tier.ToString().ToLowerInvariant(), ["strict"] = true, ["schema"] = JsonNode.Parse(request.Schema) },
        },
        ["usage"] = new JsonObject { ["include"] = true },
    };

    public async Task<LlmResponse> CompleteJsonAsync(LlmRequest request, string model, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        using var message = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, Endpoint)
        {
            Content = new StringContent(BuildBody(request, model).ToJsonString(), Encoding.UTF8, "application/json"),
        };
        message.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", apiKey);
        try
        {
            using var response = await _http.SendAsync(message, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode) throw new LlmUnavailableException($"OpenRouter answered {(int)response.StatusCode}.");
            using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(body);
            var root = doc.RootElement;
            var choice = root.GetProperty("choices")[0];
            var json = choice.GetProperty("message").GetProperty("content").GetString() ?? "";
            var usage = root.TryGetProperty("usage", out var u) ? u : default;
            long Tokens(string name) => usage.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && usage.TryGetProperty(name, out var v) ? v.GetInt64() : 0;
            var (input, output) = (Tokens("prompt_tokens"), Tokens("completion_tokens"));
            var cost = usage.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && usage.TryGetProperty("cost", out var c) && c.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Number
                ? c.GetDecimal()
                : Price.For(model).Cost(input, output, 0, 0);
            var result = new LlmResponse(json, Name, Route(model), input, output, 0, 0, cost);
            if (choice.TryGetProperty("finish_reason", out var finish) && finish.GetString() is "content_filter")
                throw new LlmRefusedException("The model declined this request.", result);
            return result;
        }
        catch (LlmUnavailableException)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            throw new LlmUnavailableException($"OpenRouter is unavailable: {e.Message}", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Engine/CatchUp.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;

public sealed record CatchUpResult(long DaysAdvanced, long DaysRemaining, WorldDate From, WorldDate To);

/// <summary>Fast-forwards the world by the real time that passed since it last ran.</summary>
public static class CatchUp
{
    public static CatchUpResult Run(
        Simulator sim, DateTimeOffset nowUtc, IProgress<WorldDate>? progress = null, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var world = sim.World;
        var from = world.Date;
        var owed = TimeScale.WholeWorldDays(nowUtc - world.LastRealUtc);
        if (owed <= 0) return new CatchUpResult(0, 0, from, from);

        var days = Math.Min(owed, TimeScale.MaxCatchUpDays);
        try
        {
            sim.AdvanceDays(days, progress, ct);
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            // Stopped part-way (the game was closed): the clock keeps pace with the days actually simulated, so saving
            // now never makes the world run ahead of real time.
            world.LastRealUtc += TimeSpan.FromTicks(TimeScale.RealTimePerWorldDay.Ticks * (world.Date.Day - from.Day));
            throw;
        }
        world.LastRealUtc += TimeSpan.FromTicks(TimeScale.RealTimePerWorldDay.Ticks * days);
        return new CatchUpResult(days, owed - days, from, world.Date);
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Persist/Backups.cs`:
```csharp
namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;

/// <summary>
/// Rolling backups of the last three sessions (spec §9): before a command changes the world, the file is copied to
/// <c>.bak1</c>, after shifting older copies to <c>.bak2</c> and <c>.bak3</c>.
/// </summary>
public static class Backups
{
    public const int Keep = 3;

    public static string PathOf(string world, int n) => $"{world}.bak{n}";

    public static void Rotate(WorldStore store, string world)
    {
        File.Delete(PathOf(world, Keep));
        for (var n = Keep - 1; n >= 1; n--)
            if (File.Exists(PathOf(world, n))) File.Move(PathOf(world, n), PathOf(world, n + 1));
        store.BackupTo(PathOf(world, 1));
    }
}
```

### Task 2: Block mesh and projection

Spec §3: the world as a floating block. The terrain grid becomes plain mesh arrays (positions, normals, colours, indices) that any renderer copies: the top surface coloured by biome, a sea plane, and four walls cut through strata. A projection maps view-model positions (0–1 across and down) to points on the block and back, so markers and pins stand on the surface. (Task 8 replaces the shading with the production look.)

**Files:**
- Create: `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/BlockMesh.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Projection.cs`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/BlockMeshTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TerrainGrid`, `Biome`, `MapPoint` (Plans 1–2, sub-project 2).
- Produces: `record MeshData(Positions, Normals, Colors, Indices) { Triangles }`; `static class BlockMesh { HeightScale; BaseDepth; Strata; Hex(rgb); ColourOf(biome); HeightAt(grid, u, v); Top(grid, resolution); Sides(grid, resolution); … }`; `static class Projection { MarkerLift; ToBlock(grid, point, lift); ToMap(onBlock) }`.

```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Geo;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class BlockMeshTests
{
    private static readonly TerrainGrid Grid =
        WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(3, WorldSize.Tiny), DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch).Geography!.Terrain;

    [Fact]
    public void TheTop_IsAHeightfieldColouredByBiome()
    {
        var top = BlockMesh.Top(Grid, 32);
        Assert.Equal(33 * 33, top.Positions.Length);
        Assert.Equal(32 * 32 * 2, top.Triangles);
        Assert.All(top.Positions, p => Assert.True(p.X is >= -1 and <= 1 && p.Z is >= -1 and <= 1));
        Assert.Contains(top.Positions, p => p.Y < 0);
        Assert.Contains(top.Positions, p => p.Y > 0.05f);
        Assert.All(top.Normals, n => Assert.InRange(n.Length(), 0.999f, 1.001f));
        Assert.True(top.Normals.Count(n => n.Y > 0.5f) > top.Normals.Length * 0.9);
        Assert.Contains(BlockMesh.ColourOf(Biome.Mountain), top.Colors);
        Assert.All(top.Indices, i => Assert.InRange(i, 0, top.Positions.Length - 1));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheSides_CutThroughThreeStrata_DownToTheBase()
    {
        var sides = BlockMesh.Sides(Grid, 16);
        Assert.Equal(4 * 16 * BlockMesh.Strata.Length * 4, sides.Positions.Length);
        Assert.Equal(-BlockMesh.BaseDepth, sides.Positions.Min(p => p.Y));
        Assert.All(BlockMesh.Strata, s => Assert.Contains(s.Colour, sides.Colors));
        Assert.All(sides.Normals, n => Assert.Equal(0, n.Y));
        Assert.Equal(2, BlockMesh.WaterPlane().Triangles);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MapPoints_LandOnTheSurface_AndComeBack()
    {
        var point = new MapPoint(0.25, 0.75);
        var onBlock = Projection.ToBlock(Grid, point);
        Assert.Equal(-0.5f, onBlock.X, 5);
        Assert.Equal(0.5f, onBlock.Z, 5);
        Assert.True(onBlock.Y >= Projection.MarkerLift);
        var back = Projection.ToMap(onBlock);
        Assert.Equal(point.X, back.X, 5);
        Assert.Equal(point.Y, back.Y, 5);
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/BlockMesh.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Geo;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>Plain mesh arrays (positions, normals, colours, triangle indices) a renderer copies into its own mesh type.</summary>
public sealed record MeshData(Vector3[] Positions, Vector3[] Normals, Vector3[] Colors, int[] Indices)
{
    public int Triangles => Indices.Length / 3;
}

/// <summary>
/// The diorama's floating world block (sub-project 4 §3): the terrain grid as a heightfield coloured by biome, a flat
/// water plane at sea level, and four side walls cut through grass, soil and rock like the design's isometric block.
/// The block spans x and z in [-1, 1]; sea level is y = 0.
/// </summary>
public static class BlockMesh
{
    public const float HeightScale = 0.22f;
    public const float SeaFloor = -0.04f;
    public const float BaseDepth = 0.32f;

    private static readonly Dictionary<Biome, Vector3> BiomeColours = new()
    {
        [Biome.Ocean] = Hex(0x6fa9b8), [Biome.Tundra] = Hex(0xe4e8e1), [Biome.Taiga] = Hex(0x7f9f7a), [Biome.Grassland] = Hex(0xb7d39a),
        [Biome.Forest] = Hex(0x6b9655), [Biome.Swamp] = Hex(0x8a9a6b), [Biome.Desert] = Hex(0xe3cf98), [Biome.Savanna] = Hex(0xcdbf7f),
        [Biome.Jungle] = Hex(0x5e8a4a), [Biome.Hills] = Hex(0xa7b48a), [Biome.Mountain] = Hex(0x9aa096),
    };

    public static readonly Vector3 River = Hex(0x7fc4d4);
    public static readonly Vector3 Water = Hex(0x7fc4d4);
    public static readonly (float Depth, Vector3 Colour)[] Strata = { (0.02f, Hex(0x7f9f5e)), (0.14f, Hex(0xbf9468)), (BaseDepth, Hex(0x8c7765)) };

    public static Vector3 Hex(int rgb) => new(((rgb >> 16) & 0xff) / 255f, ((rgb >> 8) & 0xff) / 255f, (rgb & 0xff) / 255f);

    public static Vector3 ColourOf(Biome biome) => BiomeColours[biome];

    /// <summary>Height of the surface at map position (u, v) in [0, 1]: land above sea level, sea floor below it.</summary>
    public static float HeightAt(TerrainGrid grid, double u, double v)
    {
        var x = (int)Math.Clamp(Math.Round(u * (grid.Width - 1)), 0, grid.Width - 1);
        var y = (int)Math.Clamp(Math.Round(v * (grid.Height - 1)), 0, grid.Height - 1);
        return TileHeight(grid, grid.Index(x, y));
    }

    public static float TileHeight(TerrainGrid grid, int index)
    {
        if (!grid.IsLand(index)) return SeaFloor;
        var above = Math.Max(0, grid.Elevation[index] - grid.SeaLevel) / (float)Math.Max(1, 255 - grid.SeaLevel);
        return 0.004f + above * HeightScale;
    }

    /// <summary>The top surface, sampled on a resolution × resolution grid of quads.</summary>
    public static MeshData Top(TerrainGrid grid, int resolution = 128)
    {
        var n = resolution + 1;
        var positions = new Vector3[n * n];
        var colours = new Vector3[n * n];
        for (var j = 0; j < n; j++)
            for (var i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                var (u, v) = (i / (double)resolution, j / (double)resolution);
                var tx = (int)Math.Clamp(Math.Round(u * (grid.Width - 1)), 0, grid.Width - 1);
                var ty = (int)Math.Clamp(Math.Round(v * (grid.Height - 1)), 0, grid.Height - 1);
                var index = grid.Index(tx, ty);
                positions[j * n + i] = new Vector3((float)(u * 2 - 1), TileHeight(grid, index), (float)(v * 2 - 1));
                colours[j * n + i] = grid.Has(index, TileFlags.River) && grid.IsLand(index) ? River : ColourOf(grid.BiomeAt(index));
            }
        var indices = new List<int>(resolution * resolution * 6);
        for (var j = 0; j < resolution; j++)
            for (var i = 0; i < resolution; i++)
            {
                var a = j * n + i;
                indices.AddRange(new[] { a, a + n, a + 1, a + 1, a + n, a + n + 1 });
            }
        var normals = new Vector3[n * n];
        for (var j = 0; j < n; j++)
            for (var i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                var l = positions[j * n + Math.Max(0, i - 1)];
                var r = positions[j * n + Math.Min(n - 1, i + 1)];
                var d = positions[Math.Max(0, j - 1) * n + i];
                var u = positions[Math.Min(n - 1, j + 1) * n + i];
                normals[j * n + i] = Vector3.Normalize(Vector3.Cross(u - d, r - l));
            }
        return new MeshData(positions, normals, colours, indices.ToArray());
    }

    /// <summary>The flat sea, covering the whole block just above the sea floor.</summary>
    public static MeshData WaterPlane() =>
        new(new[] { new Vector3(-1, 0, -1), new Vector3(1, 0, -1), new Vector3(-1, 0, 1), new Vector3(1, 0, 1) },
            Enumerable.Repeat(Vector3.UnitY, 4).ToArray(), Enumerable.Repeat(Water, 4).ToArray(), new[] { 0, 2, 1, 1, 2, 3 });

    /// <summary>The four walls, each a strip of strata bands following the surface edge down to the block's base.</summary>
    public static MeshData Sides(TerrainGrid grid, int resolution = 128)
    {
        var positions = new List<Vector3>();
        var normals = new List<Vector3>();
        var colours = new List<Vector3>();
        var indices = new List<int>();
        var edges = new (Func<double, (double U, double V)> At, Vector3 Normal)[]
        {
            (t => (t, 1), Vector3.UnitZ), (t => (1 - t, 0), -Vector3.UnitZ), (t => (1, 1 - t), Vector3.UnitX), (t => (0, t), -Vector3.UnitX),
        };
        foreach (var (at, normal) in edges)
            for (var k = 0; k < resolution; k++)
            {
                var (u0, v0) = at(k / (double)resolution);
                var (u1, v1) = at((k + 1) / (double)resolution);
                var top0 = Math.Max(0, HeightAt(grid, u0, v0));
                var top1 = Math.Max(0, HeightAt(grid, u1, v1));
                var (prev0, prev1) = (top0, top1);
                foreach (var (depth, colour) in Strata)
                {
                    var (low0, low1) = (depth == BaseDepth ? -BaseDepth : top0 - depth, depth == BaseDepth ? -BaseDepth : top1 - depth);
                    var start = positions.Count;
                    positions.AddRange(new[]
                    {
                        new Vector3((float)(u0 * 2 - 1), prev0, (float)(v0 * 2 - 1)), new Vector3((float)(u1 * 2 - 1), prev1, (float)(v1 * 2 - 1)),
                        new Vector3((float)(u0 * 2 - 1), low0, (float)(v0 * 2 - 1)), new Vector3((float)(u1 * 2 - 1), low1, (float)(v1 * 2 - 1)),
                    });
                    normals.AddRange(Enumerable.Repeat(normal, 4));
                    colours.AddRange(Enumerable.Repeat(colour, 4));
                    indices.AddRange(new[] { start, start + 2, start + 1, start + 1, start + 2, start + 3 });
                    (prev0, prev1) = (low0, low1);
                }
            }
        return new MeshData(positions.ToArray(), normals.ToArray(), colours.ToArray(), indices.ToArray());
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Projection.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Geo;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>Map positions (0–1 across and down) to points on the block's surface, and back.</summary>
public static class Projection
{
    public const float MarkerLift = 0.02f;

    public static Vector3 ToBlock(TerrainGrid grid, MapPoint point, float lift = MarkerLift) =>
        new((float)(point.X * 2 - 1), Math.Max(0, BlockMesh.HeightAt(grid, point.X, point.Y)) + lift, (float)(point.Y * 2 - 1));

    public static MapPoint ToMap(Vector3 onBlock) =>
        new(Math.Clamp((onBlock.X + 1) / 2.0, 0, 1), Math.Clamp((onBlock.Z + 1) / 2.0, 0, 1));
}
```

### Task 3: Camera rig, router and palette

Spec §3–§5, the parts of the client that are pure logic. The orbit rig turns drags into yaw and pitch, scrolls into distance, presets into Realm/Region/Town zoom, and eases toward its goal each frame within limits (never below ~15°, never through the block). The router keeps the current screen and a back stack, and maps any linked entity to the page it opens (people → character page; places → the map, flying to them; realms and artifacts → their pages; wars → the war chronicle). The palette holds the design tokens and the colours of categories and heraldry.

**Files:**
- Create: `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/OrbitRig.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Palette.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/ScreenRouter.cs`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/NavigationTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EntityRef`, `Category` (Plans 1–6, sub-project 2).

```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class NavigationTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void TheCamera_OrbitsWithinLimits_AndEasesToItsGoal()
    {
        var rig = new OrbitRig();
        rig.Drag(0, 10_000);
        Assert.Equal(OrbitRig.MaxPitch, rig.GoalPitch);
        rig.Drag(0, -20_000);
        Assert.Equal(OrbitRig.MinPitch, rig.GoalPitch);
        rig.Scroll(100);
        Assert.Equal(OrbitRig.MinDistance, rig.GoalDistance);
        rig.Preset(Zoom.Region);
        rig.Focus(new Vector3(5, 0.1f, -0.3f));
        Assert.Equal(new Vector3(1, 0.1f, -0.3f), rig.GoalTarget);
        for (var i = 0; i < 120; i++) rig.Update(1 / 60f);
        Assert.Equal(OrbitRig.DistanceFor(Zoom.Region), rig.Distance, 2);
        Assert.Equal(Zoom.Region, rig.NearestZoom);
        Assert.True(rig.Eye.Y > rig.Target.Y);
        Assert.Equal(rig.Distance, Vector3.Distance(rig.Eye, rig.Target), 3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LinksOpenTheirPages_AndBackReturns()
    {
        var router = new ScreenRouter();
        var changes = new List<Screen>();
        router.Changed += changes.Add;
        router.Explain(42);
        router.Open(new EntityRef("char", 7));
        router.Open(new EntityRef("house", 3));
        Assert.Equal(new Screen(ScreenKind.Character, 7), router.Current);
        Assert.True(router.Back());
        Assert.Equal(new Screen(ScreenKind.Why, 42), router.Current);
        router.Home();
        Assert.False(router.CanGoBack);
        Assert.Equal(4, changes.Count);
        Assert.Equal(new Screen(ScreenKind.Map, Focus: new EntityRef("settlement", 1)), ScreenRouter.For(new EntityRef("settlement", 1)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePalette_FollowsTheDesign()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new Vector3(0x1a / 255f, 0x1a / 255f, 0x18 / 255f), Palette.Ink);
        Assert.Equal(Palette.Conflict, Palette.For(Category.War));
        Assert.Equal(Palette.Realm, Palette.Heraldic("azure"));
        Assert.Equal(Palette.Muted, Palette.Heraldic("plaid"));
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/OrbitRig.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Numerics;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>The three zoom presets of the map HUD.</summary>
public enum Zoom { Realm, Region, Town }

/// <summary>
/// The god's-eye camera (sub-project 4 §3): orbits a target on the block (drag), descends and rises (scroll) between
/// limits, jumps to zoom presets or a point of interest, and eases toward its goal each frame. Angles in radians.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OrbitRig
{
    public const float MinPitch = 0.26f;   // ~15°: never skims the surface
    public const float MaxPitch = 1.40f;   // ~80°: almost straight down
    public const float MinDistance = 0.35f;
    public const float MaxDistance = 4.5f;
    public const float DragSpeed = 0.005f; // radians per pixel: a full turn takes a long drag, so the world never whirls
    public const float Ease = 5f;          // per second: settles in about half a second

    public static float DistanceFor(Zoom zoom) => zoom switch { Zoom.Realm => 3.4f, Zoom.Region => 1.4f, _ => 0.6f };

    public float Yaw { get; private set; } = 0.785f;
    public float Pitch { get; private set; } = 0.62f;
    public float Distance { get; private set; } = DistanceFor(Zoom.Realm);
    public Vector3 Target { get; private set; } = Vector3.Zero;

    public float GoalYaw { get; private set; } = 0.785f;
    public float GoalPitch { get; private set; } = 0.62f;
    public float GoalDistance { get; private set; } = DistanceFor(Zoom.Realm);
    public Vector3 GoalTarget { get; private set; } = Vector3.Zero;

    public void Drag(float dxPixels, float dyPixels)
    {
        GoalYaw -= dxPixels * DragSpeed;
        GoalPitch = Math.Clamp(GoalPitch + dyPixels * DragSpeed, MinPitch, MaxPitch);
    }

    /// <summary>Scroll steps: positive descends (closer), negative rises.</summary>
    public void Scroll(float steps) => GoalDistance = Math.Clamp(GoalDistance * MathF.Pow(0.85f, steps), MinDistance, MaxDistance);

    public void Preset(Zoom zoom) => GoalDistance = DistanceFor(zoom);

    public void Focus(Vector3 point, Zoom? zoom = null)
    {
        GoalTarget = new Vector3(Math.Clamp(point.X, -1, 1), point.Y, Math.Clamp(point.Z, -1, 1));
        if (zoom is { } z) Preset(z);
    }

    public void Reset()
    {
        (GoalYaw, GoalPitch, GoalTarget) = (0.785f, 0.62f, Vector3.Zero);
        Preset(Zoom.Realm);
    }

    /// <summary>Eases toward the goal; call once per frame with the frame time.</summary>
    public void Update(float seconds)
    {
        var t = 1 - MathF.Exp(-Ease * Math.Max(0, seconds));
        Yaw += (GoalYaw - Yaw) * t;
        Pitch += (GoalPitch - Pitch) * t;
        Distance += (GoalDistance - Distance) * t;
        Target += (GoalTarget - Target) * t;
    }

    /// <summary>Where the camera sits, looking at <see cref="Target"/>.</summary>
    public Vector3 Eye => Target + Distance * new Vector3(MathF.Cos(Pitch) * MathF.Sin(Yaw), MathF.Sin(Pitch), MathF.Cos(Pitch) * MathF.Cos(Yaw));

    /// <summary>The zoom preset nearest the current distance (highlights the HUD's Realm/Region/Town buttons).</summary>
    public Zoom NearestZoom => Enum.GetValues<Zoom>().MinBy(z => Math.Abs(MathF.Log(DistanceFor(z) / Distance)));
}
```

```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>The design's tokens (sub-project 4 §5) as linear-ish RGB in 0–1, plus colours for categories and realms.</summary>
public static class Palette
{
    public static readonly Vector3 Ground = BlockMesh.Hex(0xecebe7);
    public static readonly Vector3 Card = BlockMesh.Hex(0xf7f6f3);
    public static readonly Vector3 Border = BlockMesh.Hex(0xdcdad4);
    public static readonly Vector3 Rule = BlockMesh.Hex(0xe3e1db);
    public static readonly Vector3 Ink = BlockMesh.Hex(0x1a1a18);
    public static readonly Vector3 Body = BlockMesh.Hex(0x4a4842);
    public static readonly Vector3 Muted = BlockMesh.Hex(0x66645d);
    public static readonly Vector3 Conflict = BlockMesh.Hex(0xc4621f);
    public static readonly Vector3 Realm = BlockMesh.Hex(0x3b6fb6);

    public const string DisplayFont = "Instrument Serif";
    public const string UiFont = "Geist";

    public static Vector3 For(Category category) => category switch
    {
        Category.War => Conflict,
        Category.Politics => Realm,
        Category.Economy => BlockMesh.Hex(0xb58a3c),
        Category.FaithAndMagic => BlockMesh.Hex(0x7b5ea7),
        Category.Structures => Ink,
        Category.Nature => BlockMesh.Hex(0x5e8a4a),
        Category.Chronicle => Muted,
        _ => Body,
    };

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, Vector3> Heraldry = new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        ["crimson"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0xa8323a), ["azure"] = Realm, ["gold"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0xc9a227), ["silver"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0x9aa0a6),
        ["sable"] = Ink, ["vert"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0x3f7d4e), ["purpure"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0x6d4c8f), ["orange"] = Conflict,
        ["white"] = Card, ["russet"] = BlockMesh.Hex(0x8b4a2b), ["grey"] = Muted,
    };

    /// <summary>A realm's heraldic colour name (e.g. "azure") as a colour; unknown names are muted.</summary>
    public static Vector3 Heraldic(string name) => Heraldry.TryGetValue(name, out var colour) ? colour : Muted;
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/ScreenRouter.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

public enum ScreenKind { Digest, Map, Character, Why, Chronicle, Realm, Artifact, Family }

/// <summary>Which screen is showing and for what: an entity or event id, the map's year, and the place a map flies to.</summary>
public sealed record Screen(ScreenKind Kind, long Id = 0, int? Year = null, EntityRef? Focus = null);

/// <summary>
/// Navigation (sub-project 4 §4): every linked name opens its page (characters → character page, places → the map
/// focused there, realms, wars and artifacts → their pages), "why" opens a chain, and Back returns.
/// </summary>
public sealed class ScreenRouter
{
    private readonly Stack<Screen> _back = new();

    public Screen Current { get; private set; } = new(ScreenKind.Digest);

    public bool CanGoBack => _back.Count > 0;

    public event Action<Screen>? Changed;

    public void Go(Screen screen)
    {
        if (screen == Current) return;
        _back.Push(Current);
        Current = screen;
        Changed?.Invoke(screen);
    }

    public bool Back()
    {
        if (_back.Count == 0) return false;
        Current = _back.Pop();
        Changed?.Invoke(Current);
        return true;
    }

    public void Home()
    {
        _back.Clear();
        Current = new Screen(ScreenKind.Digest);
        Changed?.Invoke(Current);
    }

    /// <summary>The screen a linked entity opens, or null when it has none (e.g. a house).</summary>
    public static Screen? For(EntityRef entity) => entity.Kind switch
    {
        "char" => new Screen(ScreenKind.Character, entity.Id),
        "settlement" or "region" or "structure" => new Screen(ScreenKind.Map, Focus: entity),
        "realm" => new Screen(ScreenKind.Realm, entity.Id),
        "artifact" => new Screen(ScreenKind.Artifact, entity.Id),
        "war" => new Screen(ScreenKind.Chronicle),
        _ => null,
    };

    public void Open(EntityRef entity)
    {
        if (For(entity) is { } screen) Go(screen);
    }

    public void Explain(long eventId) => Go(new Screen(ScreenKind.Why, eventId));
}
```

### Task 4: The Godot project and headless checks

Spec §2 and §7. A Godot 4.3 .NET project (`game/`) that references the client library: the design's theme (Instrument Serif and Geist, both under the SIL Open Font License, shipped with their licences), small builders for the controls every screen uses, and `Main`, which opens or creates the world (`-- --world <path>`, else one in Godot's user folder), catches up on a worker thread with progress in the status line, then steps live. A headless check scene creates a tiny world, runs the real `Main` and exits 0 or 1, so CI can drive every screen without a display; with `--shots <dir>` and a display it also saves screenshots for review.

**Files:**
- Create: `game/.gitignore`, `game/FantasyWorld.Game.csproj`, `game/fonts/Geist-OFL.txt`, `game/fonts/Geist[wght].ttf`, `game/fonts/InstrumentSerif-Italic.ttf`, `game/fonts/InstrumentSerif-OFL.txt`, `game/fonts/InstrumentSerif-Regular.ttf`, `game/project.godot`, `game/scenes/Main.tscn`, `game/scripts/Main.cs`, `game/scripts/Ui.cs`, `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs`, `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–3.
- Produces: `game/project.godot` (main scene `res://scenes/Main.tscn`, 1440×900), `game/FantasyWorld.Game.csproj` (`Godot.NET.Sdk/4.3.0`, references `FantasyWorld.Client`); `static class Ui { CreateTheme(); C(rgb, alpha); Card; Kicker; Title; Body; Button; Pill; Column; Row; Clear }`; `partial class Main : Control { Use(session); Session; Router; Status; CaughtUp; OnCaughtUp(); UpdateStatus() }`; `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn` (prints `HEADLESS CHECKS PASSED (n)`); fonts in `game/fonts/`.

```csharp
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The root of the game (sub-project 4 §2): opens or creates the world, catches up on a worker thread with progress,
/// then keeps it live, and hosts the diorama and the screens. The world file comes from `-- --world &lt;path&gt;`, else
/// user://worlds/world.fw.db (created as a medium world with a century of prehistory on first launch).
/// </summary>
public partial class Main : Control
{
    public const double LiveInterval = 2.0;

    private Session? _session;
    private double _sinceStep;

    public Session? Session => _session;

    public ScreenRouter Router { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>One line of state: creating, catching up, caught up.</summary>
    public Label Status { get; private set; } = null!;

    public bool Ready { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>Lets a test (or another host) supply an open session before the node enters the tree.</summary>
    public void Use(Session session) => _session = session;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Main";
        Theme = Ui.CreateTheme();
        SetAnchorsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        var ground = new ColorRect { Name = "Ground", Color = Ui.C(Palette.Ground), MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        ground.SetAnchorsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(ground);
        Status = Ui.Body("Opening…", "Status", Palette.Body, 12);
        Status.SetAnchorsPreset(LayoutPreset.BottomRight);
        Status.Position = new Vector2(-320, -44);
        AddChild(Status);
        _ = StartAsync();
    }

    private async Task StartAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_session is null)
            {
                var path = WorldPath();
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(path))
                {
                    Status.Text = "Creating your world…";
                    _session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, (ulong)DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.UtcTicks, WorldSize.Medium, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
                }
                else _session = Session.Open(path, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
            }
            var progress = new Progress<WorldDate>(date => Status.Text = $"Catching up… {date}");
            await _session.CatchUpAsync(progress);
            OnCaughtUp();
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            Status.Text = $"Could not open the world: {e.Message}";
            GD.PushError(e.ToString());
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Called once the world is current: subclasses and later tasks show the screens here.</summary>
    protected virtual void OnCaughtUp()
    {
        Ready = true;
        UpdateStatus();
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (!Ready || _session is null) return;
        _sinceStep += delta;
        if (_sinceStep < LiveInterval) return;
        _sinceStep = 0;
        if (_session.Step().Days > 0) UpdateStatus();
    }

    public void UpdateStatus()
    {
        if (_session?.TryRead((w, _) => w.Date.ToString()) is { } date)
            Status.Text = _session.DaysBehind > 0 ? $"Catching up · {_session.DaysBehind} days behind" : $"Caught up · {date}";
    }

    public override void _ExitTree() => _session?.Dispose();

    private static string WorldPath()
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = Array.IndexOf(args, "--world");
        return at >= 0 && at + 1 < args.Length
            ? args[at + 1]
            : System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "worlds", "world.fw.db");
    }
}
```

`game/scripts/Ui.cs`:
```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>The design's look (sub-project 4 §5) and small builders for the controls every screen uses.</summary>
public static class Ui
{
    private static readonly FontFile Display = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Italic.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile DisplayUpright = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Regular.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile Sans = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/Geist[wght].ttf");

    public static Color C(Rgb rgb, float alpha = 1) => new(rgb.X, rgb.Y, rgb.Z, alpha);

    public static Theme CreateTheme()
    {
        var theme = new Theme { DefaultFont = Sans, DefaultFontSize = 13 };
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Label", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Button", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_hover_color", "Button", C(Palette.Body));
        theme.SetStylebox("normal", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("hover", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("pressed", "Button", Pill(filled: true));
        theme.SetStylebox("focus", "Button", new StyleBoxEmpty());
        theme.SetColor("font_pressed_color", "Button", Colors.White);
        return theme;
    }

    public static StyleBoxFlat Pill(bool filled) => new()
    {
        BgColor = filled ? C(Palette.Ink) : new Color(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BorderColor = C(Palette.Ink),
        BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
        CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 999, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 999, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 999, CornerRadiusTopRight = 999,
        ContentMarginLeft = 14, ContentMarginRight = 14, ContentMarginTop = 7, ContentMarginBottom = 7,
    };

    /// <summary>The floating card: warm white, hairline border, soft shadow, 16 px corners.</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Card(string name, float width)
    {
        var card = new PanelContainer { Name = name, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(width, 0) };
        card.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = C(Palette.Card), BorderColor = C(Palette.Border),
            BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
            CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 16, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 16, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 16, CornerRadiusTopRight = 16,
            ShadowColor = new Color(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.09f, 0.06f), ShadowSize = 20, ShadowOffset = new Godot.Vector2(0, 12),
            ContentMarginLeft = 22, ContentMarginRight = 22, ContentMarginTop = 22, ContentMarginBottom = 22,
        });
        return card;
    }

    /// <summary>The small uppercase label above sections ("OVERNIGHT", "TIES").</summary>
    public static Label Kicker(string text, string name = "Kicker")
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text.ToUpperInvariant() };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", 11);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(Palette.Muted));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>Italic serif display text ("Year 312.", names on pages).</summary>
    public static Label Title(string text, int size, string name = "Title", bool italic = true)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontOverride("font", italic ? Display : DisplayUpright);
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        return label;
    }

    public static Label Body(string text, string name = "Body", Rgb? colour = null, int size = 13)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(colour ?? Palette.Body));
        return label;
    }

    public static Button Button(string text, string name, bool primary = false)
    {
        var button = new Button { Name = name, Text = text, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(0, 34) };
        if (primary)
        {
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("normal", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("hover", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", Colors.White);
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", Colors.White);
        }
        return button;
    }

    public static VBoxContainer Column(string name, int gap)
    {
        var column = new VBoxContainer { Name = name };
        column.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return column;
    }

    public static HBoxContainer Row(string name, int gap)
    {
        var row = new HBoxContainer { Name = name };
        row.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return row;
    }

    public static void Clear(Node node)
    {
        foreach (var child in node.GetChildren())
        {
            node.RemoveChild(child);
            child.QueueFree();
        }
    }
}
```

`game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Runs the real scenes headless against a fresh tiny world and checks what they show (sub-project 4 §7). Exits 0 when
/// every check passes, 1 otherwise; CI runs it with `godot --headless --path game res://tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn`.
/// </summary>
public partial class HeadlessChecks : Node
{
    private readonly List<string> _failures = new();
    private int _checks;

    public override void _Ready() => _ = RunAsync();

    private async Task RunAsync()
    {
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "checks", $"check-{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.fw.db");
        try
        {
            var session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => System.DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, prehistoryYears: 8));
            var main = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://scenes/Main.tscn").Instantiate<Main>();
            main.Use(session);
            AddChild(main);
            await Until(() => main.Ready, "the world catches up");
            await RunChecks(main);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Fail($"unexpected {e}");
        }
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "", ".bak1", ".bak2", ".bak3", "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path + suffix)) System.IO.File.Delete(path + suffix);
        GD.Print(_failures.Count == 0 ? $"HEADLESS CHECKS PASSED ({_checks})" : $"HEADLESS CHECKS FAILED: {string.Join("; ", _failures)}");
        GetTree().Quit(_failures.Count == 0 ? 0 : 1);
    }

    /// <summary>The checks for each screen; later tasks add theirs.</summary>
    private async Task RunChecks(Main main)
    {
        Check(main.Status.Text.StartsWith("Caught up · "), $"status reads caught up (\"{main.Status.Text}\")");
        await Frames(2);
    }

    private void Check(bool ok, string what)
    {
        _checks++;
        if (!ok) Fail(what);
    }

    private void Fail(string what)
    {
        _failures.Add(what);
        GD.PrintErr("FAIL: " + what);
    }

    private async Task Frames(int n)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
    }

    private async Task Until(System.Func<bool> condition, string what, int maxFrames = 600)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < maxFrames && !condition(); i++) await Frames(1);
        Check(condition(), what);
    }
}
```

### Task 5: The diorama scene

Spec §3: the block, the sea, settlements and structures as markers, realm borders and digest pins, seen through the orbit camera (drag to orbit, scroll to descend). Godot-free layout lives in `Markers` (positions, sizes by tier, colours by holder, border segments between differently held regions); the `Diorama` node only builds meshes from it. Clicking near a marker picks it by its projected screen position, so picking needs no physics.

**Files:**
- Create: `game/scripts/Diorama.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Markers.cs`
- Modify: `game/scripts/Main.cs`, `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/MarkersTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–4; `MapScreen`, `DigestScreen` (sub-project 2).
- Produces: `enum MarkerShape`; `record Marker`, `Pin`, `Segment`; `static class Markers { PinLift; SizeOf(tier); For(map, geography); Pins(digest, geography); Borders(map, geography) }`; `partial class Diorama : Node3D { Rig; Camera; Block; MarkerLayer; PinLayer; BorderLines; Markers; Pins; Picked; Build(geography); Show(map); ShowPins(pins); Focus(point, zoom?); Pick(screen); ScreenOf(position); ToGodot(v) }`; `Main.Diorama`.

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class MarkersTests
{
    private static readonly Simulator Aged = Prehistory.Run(WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(3, WorldSize.Tiny), DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch), 40);

    [Fact]
    public void EverySettlementAndStructure_StandsOnTheBlock_SizedByTier()
    {
        var map = MapScreen.Build(Aged.World, Aged.Log);
        var markers = Markers.For(map, Aged.World.Geography!);
        Assert.Equal(map.Settlements.Count + map.Structures.Count, markers.Count);
        Assert.All(markers, m => Assert.True(m.Position.X is >= -1.1f and <= 1.1f && m.Position.Z is >= -1.1f and <= 1.1f && m.Position.Y >= 0));
        var city = map.Settlements.MaxBy(s => Markers.SizeOf(s.Tier))!;
        Assert.Equal(Markers.SizeOf(city.Tier), markers.Single(m => m.Ref == city.Ref).Size);
        Assert.True(Markers.SizeOf("City") > Markers.SizeOf("Hamlet"));
        Assert.All(markers.Where(m => m.Kind == PinMarker.Conflict), m => Assert.Equal(Palette.Conflict, m.Colour));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Borders_RunBetweenDifferentlyHeldRegions()
    {
        var map = MapScreen.Build(Aged.World, Aged.Log);
        var borders = Markers.Borders(map, Aged.World.Geography!);
        Assert.Equal(map.Borders.Count > 0, borders.Count > 0);
        var grid = Aged.World.Geography!.Terrain;
        Assert.All(borders, b => Assert.InRange(Math.Abs(b.From.X - b.To.X) + Math.Abs(b.From.Z - b.To.Z), 0.5f / grid.Width, 2.5f / grid.Width));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DigestPins_FloatAboveTheirPlaces_InOrder()
    {
        var digest = DigestScreen.Build(Aged.World, Aged.Log, FantasyWorld.Sim.Chronicle.Digest.Build(Aged.Log, Aged.World, 0, new HashSet<EntityRef>()), new WorldDate(0));
        var pins = Markers.Pins(digest, Aged.World.Geography!);
        Assert.Equal(digest.Pins.Count, pins.Count);
        Assert.Equal(pins.Select(p => p.Number).Order(), pins.Select(p => p.Number));
        Assert.All(pins, p => Assert.True(p.Position.Y >= Markers.PinLift));
    }
}
```

`game/scripts/Diorama.cs`:
```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The floating world block (sub-project 4 §3): terrain, sea, strata walls, markers, realm borders and digest pins,
/// seen through the orbit camera. Geography is built once; markers and borders are rebuilt when the map changes.
/// Drag to orbit, scroll to descend; clicking near a marker raises <see cref="Picked"/>.
/// </summary>
public partial class Diorama : Node3D
{
    public const float PickRadius = 16f;

    private Geography? _geography;
    private bool _dragging;
    private Vector2 _pressedAt;
    private readonly List<Marker> _markers = new();
    private readonly List<Pin> _pins = new();

    public OrbitRig Rig { get; } = new();
    public Camera3D Camera { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D Block { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D MarkerLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D PinLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D BorderLines { get; private set; } = null!;
    public IReadOnlyList<Marker> Markers => _markers;
    public IReadOnlyList<Pin> Pins => _pins;

    /// <summary>A marker or pin was clicked.</summary>
    public event Action<EntityRef>? Picked;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Diorama";
        Camera = new Camera3D { Name = "Camera", Fov = 32, Near = 0.01f, Far = 50 };
        AddChild(Camera);
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Sun", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-55, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.6f, ShadowEnabled = true });
        // A soft fill from the viewer's side keeps the strata walls readable.
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Fill", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-15, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.45f });
        AddChild(new WorldEnvironment
        {
            Name = "Environment",
            Environment = new Godot.Environment
            {
                BackgroundMode = Godot.Environment.BGMode.Color, BackgroundColor = Ui.C(Palette.Ground),
                AmbientLightSource = Godot.Environment.AmbientSource.Color, AmbientLightColor = Colors.White, AmbientLightEnergy = 0.55f,
            },
        });
        Block = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Block" };
        AddChild(Block);
        BorderLines = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Borders" };
        AddChild(BorderLines);
        MarkerLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Markers" };
        AddChild(MarkerLayer);
        PinLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Pins" };
        AddChild(PinLayer);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    /// <summary>Builds the terrain block, its walls and the sea (once per world).</summary>
    public void Build(Geography geography, int resolution = 128)
    {
        _geography = geography;
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Top(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Sides(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        Block.Mesh = mesh;
        var water = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(water, BlockMesh.WaterPlane(), new StandardMaterial3D
        {
            AlbedoColor = Ui.C(BlockMesh.Water, 0.72f), Transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TransparencyEnum.Alpha, Roughness = 0.3f,
        });
        var sea = GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water") ?? new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Water" };
        sea.Mesh = water;
        if (sea.GetParent() is null) AddChild(sea);
    }

    /// <summary>Places settlements, structures and borders for a map (today's or a past year's).</summary>
    public void Show(MapScreen map)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        Ui.Clear(MarkerLayer);
        _markers.Clear();
        _markers.AddRange(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.For(map, _geography));
        foreach (var marker in _markers) MarkerLayer.AddChild(MarkerNode(marker));
        BorderLines.Mesh = DashedLines(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.Borders(map, _geography), Palette.Ink);
    }

    /// <summary>The digest's numbered pins (empty to clear them).</summary>
    public void ShowPins(IEnumerable<Pin> pins)
    {
        Ui.Clear(PinLayer);
        _pins.Clear();
        _pins.AddRange(pins);
        foreach (var pin in _pins)
        {
            var node = new Node3D { Name = $"Pin{pin.Number}", Position = ToGodot(pin.Position) };
            node.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D
            {
                Name = "Stem", Position = new Vector3(0, -Client.Markers.PinLift / 2, 0),
                Mesh = new CylinderMesh { TopRadius = 0.0015f, BottomRadius = 0.0015f, Height = Client.Markers.PinLift },
                MaterialOverride = Flat(Palette.Ink),
            });
            node.AddChild(new Label3D
            {
                Name = "Number", Text = pin.Number.ToString(), Billboard = BaseMaterial3D.BillboardModeEnum.Enabled, FontSize = 48, PixelSize = 0.0006f,
                Modulate = Colors.White, OutlineModulate = Ui.C(Palette.Ink), OutlineSize = 22, NoDepthTest = true,
            });
            PinLayer.AddChild(node);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Eases the camera to a place on the map.</summary>
    public void Focus(MapPoint point, Zoom? zoom = null)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        var at = Client.Projection.ToBlock(_geography.Terrain, point, 0);
        Rig.Focus(at, zoom);
    }

    /// <summary>The marker or pin drawn nearest a screen point, within <see cref="PickRadius"/> pixels.</summary>
    public EntityRef? Pick(Vector2 screen)
    {
        var candidates = _pins.Select(p => (p.Place, p.Position)).Concat(_markers.Select(m => (m.Ref, m.Position)));
        EntityRef? best = null;
        var bestDistance = PickRadius;
        foreach (var (entity, position) in candidates)
        {
            var world = ToGodot(position);
            if (Camera.IsPositionBehind(world)) continue;
            var distance = Camera.UnprojectPosition(world).DistanceTo(screen);
            if (distance < bestDistance) (best, bestDistance) = (entity, distance);
        }
        return best;
    }

    /// <summary>Where a block-space point appears on screen.</summary>
    public Vector2 ScreenOf(Rgb position) => Camera.UnprojectPosition(ToGodot(position));

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        Rig.Update((float)delta);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    public override void _UnhandledInput(InputEvent input)
    {
        switch (input)
        {
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left } button:
                if (button.Pressed) (_dragging, _pressedAt) = (true, button.Position);
                else
                {
                    _dragging = false;
                    if (button.Position.DistanceTo(_pressedAt) < 4 && Pick(button.Position) is { } picked) Picked?.Invoke(picked);
                }
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelUp, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelDown, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(-1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseMotion motion when _dragging:
                Rig.Drag(motion.Relative.X, motion.Relative.Y);
                break;
        }
    }

    private void UpdateCamera()
    {
        if (Camera is null) return;
        Camera.Position = ToGodot(Rig.Eye);
        Camera.LookAt(ToGodot(Rig.Target), Vector3.Up);
    }

    private static Node3D MarkerNode(Marker marker)
    {
        var node = new Node3D { Name = $"{marker.Ref.Kind}{marker.Ref.Id}", Position = ToGodot(marker.Position) };
        var size = marker.Size;
        switch (marker.Shape)
        {
            case MarkerShape.Settlement:
                node.AddChild(Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size * 0.8f, size) }, marker.Colour, size * 0.4f));
                break;
            case MarkerShape.Labyrinth:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 1.4f, Palette.Card, size));
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 0.8f, marker.Colour, size * 1.05f));
                break;
            default:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size, marker.Colour, size));
                break;
        }
        return node;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Diamond(float size, Rgb colour, float lift)
    {
        var diamond = Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size, size) }, colour, lift);
        diamond.RotationDegrees = new Vector3(45, 0, 45);
        return diamond;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Solid(Mesh mesh, Rgb colour, float lift) =>
        new() { Mesh = mesh, Position = new Vector3(0, lift, 0), MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), Roughness = 0.8f } };

    private static StandardMaterial3D Flat(Rgb colour) =>
        new() { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded };

    private static StandardMaterial3D VertexColours() => new() { VertexColorUseAsAlbedo = true, Roughness = 0.95f };

    /// <summary>Every other segment, so borders read as the design's dashed lines.</summary>
    private static ArrayMesh DashedLines(IReadOnlyList<Segment> segments, Rgb colour)
    {
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        var points = new List<Vector3>();
        for (var i = 0; i < segments.Count; i += 2)
        {
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].From));
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].To));
        }
        if (points.Count == 0) return mesh;
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = points.ToArray();
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Lines, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(0, Flat(colour));
        return mesh;
    }

    private static void AddSurface(ArrayMesh mesh, MeshData data, Material material)
    {
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = data.Positions.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Normal] = data.Normals.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Color] = data.Colors.Select(c => Ui.C(c).SrgbToLinear()).ToArray();
        // Godot treats clockwise triangles as front faces; the client's indices are counter-clockwise.
        var indices = new int[data.Indices.Length];
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.Length; i += 3) (indices[i], indices[i + 1], indices[i + 2]) = (data.Indices[i], data.Indices[i + 2], data.Indices[i + 1]);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Index] = indices;
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Triangles, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(mesh.GetSurfaceCount() - 1, material);
    }

    public static Vector3 ToGodot(Rgb v) => new(v.X, v.Y, v.Z);
}
```
