```csharp
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The root of the game (sub-project 4 §2): opens or creates the world, catches up on a worker thread with progress,
/// then keeps it live, and hosts the diorama and the screens. The world file comes from `-- --world &lt;path&gt;`, else
/// user://worlds/world.fw.db (created as a medium world with a century of prehistory on first launch).
/// </summary>
public partial class Main : Control
{
    public const double LiveInterval = 2.0;

    private Session? _session;
    private double _sinceStep;

    public Session? Session => _session;

    public ScreenRouter Router { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>One line of state: creating, catching up, caught up.</summary>
    public Label Status { get; private set; } = null!;

    public bool CaughtUp { get; private set; }

    public Diorama Diorama { get; private set; } = null!;

    /// <summary>Lets a test (or another host) supply an open session before the node enters the tree.</summary>
    public void Use(Session session) => _session = session;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Main";
        Theme = Ui.CreateTheme();
        SetAnchorsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        // The diorama draws behind every card; the root lets clicks it does not use fall through to it.
        MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        Diorama = new Diorama();
        AddChild(Diorama);
        Status = Ui.Body("Opening…", "Status", Palette.Body, 12);
        Status.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        Status.SetAnchorsPreset(LayoutPreset.BottomLeft);
        Status.Position = new Vector2(28, -40);
        AddChild(Status);
        _ = StartAsync();
    }

    private async Task StartAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_session is null)
            {
                var path = WorldPath();
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(path))
                {
                    Status.Text = "Creating your world…";
                    _session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, (ulong)DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.UtcTicks, WorldSize.Medium, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
                }
                else _session = Session.Open(path, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
            }
            var progress = new Progress<WorldDate>(date => Status.Text = $"Catching up… {date}");
            await _session.CatchUpAsync(progress);
            OnCaughtUp();
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            Status.Text = $"Could not open the world: {e.Message}";
            GD.PushError(e.ToString());
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Called once the world is current: subclasses and later tasks show the screens here.</summary>
    protected virtual void OnCaughtUp()
    {
        if (_session!.Read((w, _) => w.Geography) is { } geography)
        {
            Diorama.Build(geography);
            Diorama.Show(_session.Map());
        }
        CaughtUp = true;
        UpdateStatus();
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (!CaughtUp || _session is null) return;
        _sinceStep += delta;
        if (_sinceStep < LiveInterval) return;
        _sinceStep = 0;
        if (_session.Step().Days > 0) UpdateStatus();
    }

    public void UpdateStatus()
    {
        if (_session?.TryRead((w, _) => w.Date.ToString()) is { } date)
            Status.Text = _session.DaysBehind > 0 ? $"Catching up · {_session.DaysBehind} days behind" : $"Caught up · {date}";
    }

    public override void _ExitTree() => _session?.Dispose();

    private static string WorldPath()
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = Array.IndexOf(args, "--world");
        return at >= 0 && at + 1 < args.Length
            ? args[at + 1]
            : System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "worlds", "world.fw.db");
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Linq;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Runs the real scenes headless against a fresh tiny world and checks what they show (sub-project 4 §7). Exits 0 when
/// every check passes, 1 otherwise; CI runs it with `godot --headless --path game res://tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn`.
/// </summary>
public partial class HeadlessChecks : Node
{
    private readonly List<string> _failures = new();
    private int _checks;

    public override void _Ready() => _ = RunAsync();

    private async Task RunAsync()
    {
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "checks", $"check-{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.fw.db");
        try
        {
            var session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => System.DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, prehistoryYears: 8));
            var main = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://scenes/Main.tscn").Instantiate<Main>();
            main.Use(session);
            AddChild(main);
            await Until(() => main.CaughtUp, "the world catches up");
            await RunChecks(main);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Fail($"unexpected {e}");
        }
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "", ".bak1", ".bak2", ".bak3", "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path + suffix)) System.IO.File.Delete(path + suffix);
        GD.Print(_failures.Count == 0 ? $"HEADLESS CHECKS PASSED ({_checks})" : $"HEADLESS CHECKS FAILED: {string.Join("; ", _failures)}");
        GetTree().Quit(_failures.Count == 0 ? 0 : 1);
    }

    /// <summary>The checks for each screen; later tasks add theirs.</summary>
    private async Task RunChecks(Main main)
    {
        Check(main.Status.Text.StartsWith("Caught up · "), $"status reads caught up (\"{main.Status.Text}\")");
        await Frames(2);
        await DioramaChecks(main);
    }

    private async Task DioramaChecks(Main main)
    {
        var diorama = main.Diorama;
        var map = main.Session!.Map();
        Check(diorama.Block.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } block && block.GetSurfaceCount() == 2, "the block has a top and walls");
        Check(diorama.GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water")?.Mesh is not null, "the sea is drawn");
        Check(diorama.MarkerLayer.GetChildCount() == map.Settlements.Count(s => s.Position is not null) + map.Structures.Count(s => s.Position is not null),
            $"one marker per settlement and structure ({diorama.MarkerLayer.GetChildCount()})");
        Check(map.Borders.Count == 0 || diorama.BorderLines.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } lines && lines.GetSurfaceCount() == 1, "realm borders are drawn");

        diorama.Rig.Reset();
        await Frames(90);
        await Shot("diorama");
        Check(diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Eye)) < 0.01f, "the camera follows the rig");
        var target = diorama.Markers[0];
        var onScreen = diorama.ScreenOf(target.Position);
        Check(diorama.Pick(onScreen) is { } picked && (picked == target.Ref || diorama.Markers.Any(m => m.Ref == picked && diorama.ScreenOf(m.Position).DistanceTo(onScreen) < 1)),
            "clicking a marker picks it");
        Check(diorama.Pick(new Godot.Vector2(-500, -500)) is null, "clicking empty space picks nothing");

        var settlement = map.Settlements.First(s => s.Position is not null);
        diorama.Focus(settlement.Position!, Zoom.Town);
        await Frames(120);
        await Shot("diorama-town");
        Check(diorama.Rig.NearestZoom == Zoom.Town && diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Target)) < 1f, "focusing descends to the town");
    }

    /// <summary>With `-- --shots &lt;dir&gt;` (and a real display), saves what the screen shows, for visual review.</summary>
    private async Task Shot(string name)
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = System.Array.IndexOf(args, "--shots");
        if (at < 0 || at + 1 >= args.Length || DisplayServer.GetName() == "headless") return;
        await Frames(4);
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(args[at + 1]);
        GetViewport().GetTexture().GetImage().SavePng(System.IO.Path.Combine(args[at + 1], name + ".png"));
    }

    private void Check(bool ok, string what)
    {
        _checks++;
        if (!ok) Fail(what);
    }

    private void Fail(string what)
    {
        _failures.Add(what);
        GD.PrintErr("FAIL: " + what);
    }

    private async Task Frames(int n)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
    }

    private async Task Until(System.Func<bool> condition, string what, int maxFrames = 600)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < maxFrames && !condition(); i++) await Frames(1);
        Check(condition(), what);
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Markers.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

public enum MarkerShape { Settlement, Landmark, Labyrinth, Ruin }

/// <summary>One thing standing on the block: where, what shape, how big and in what colour.</summary>
public sealed record Marker(EntityRef Ref, string Name, MarkerShape Shape, Vector3 Position, float Size, Vector3 Colour, PinMarker Kind);

/// <summary>A numbered digest pin floating above its place.</summary>
public sealed record Pin(int Number, EntityRef Place, string Label, Vector3 Position, long EventId);

/// <summary>A straight piece of a realm border on the block's surface.</summary>
public sealed record Segment(Vector3 From, Vector3 To);

/// <summary>
/// Lays the map screen out on the diorama (sub-project 4 §3): settlement blocks sized by tier and coloured by their
/// holder (orange when occupied), diamonds for landmarks, nested diamonds for labyrinths, numbered pins above digest
/// places, and realm borders along the tile edges where two differently held regions meet.
/// </summary>
public static class Markers
{
    public const float PinLift = 0.14f;

    public static float SizeOf(string tier) => tier switch
    {
        nameof(SettlementTier.City) => 0.045f,
        nameof(SettlementTier.Town) => 0.034f,
        nameof(SettlementTier.Village) => 0.024f,
        _ => 0.016f,
    };

    public static IReadOnlyList<Marker> For(MapScreen map, Geography geography)
    {
        var grid = geography.Terrain;
        var colourOf = map.Regions.ToDictionary(r => r.Ref.Id, r => r.Colour);
        var markers = new List<Marker>();
        foreach (var s in map.Settlements.Where(s => s.Position is not null))
        {
            var region = RegionAt(geography, s.Position!);
            var colour = s.Marker == PinMarker.Conflict ? Palette.Conflict
                : colourOf.TryGetValue(region, out var name) && name != "none" ? Palette.Heraldic(name) : Palette.Muted;
            markers.Add(new Marker(s.Ref, s.Name, MarkerShape.Settlement, Projection.ToBlock(grid, s.Position!, 0), SizeOf(s.Tier), colour, s.Marker));
        }
        foreach (var st in map.Structures.Where(st => st.Position is not null))
        {
            var shape = st.State == nameof(StructureState.Ruined) ? MarkerShape.Ruin
                : st.Kind == nameof(StructureKind.Labyrinth) ? MarkerShape.Labyrinth : MarkerShape.Landmark;
            var colour = shape == MarkerShape.Ruin ? Palette.Muted : shape == MarkerShape.Labyrinth ? Palette.For(Category.FaithAndMagic) : Palette.Ink;
            // Structures stand beside their settlement, not inside it.
            var at = Projection.ToBlock(grid, st.Position!, 0) + new Vector3(0.03f, 0, 0.03f);
            markers.Add(new Marker(st.Ref, st.Name, shape, at with { Y = Math.Max(0, BlockMesh.HeightAt(grid, (at.X + 1) / 2, (at.Z + 1) / 2)) },
                0.022f, colour, PinMarker.Plain));
        }
        return markers;
    }

    /// <summary>The digest's numbered pins, lifted above the block so they read from any angle.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<Pin> Pins(DigestScreen digest, Geography geography) =>
        digest.Pins.Select(p => new Pin(p.Number, p.Place, p.Label, Projection.ToBlock(geography.Terrain, p.Position, PinLift), p.EventId)).ToList();

    /// <summary>Tile edges between land regions whose holders differ, slightly above the surface.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<Segment> Borders(MapScreen map, Geography geography, float lift = 0.006f)
    {
        var grid = geography.Terrain;
        var owner = map.Regions.ToDictionary(r => r.Ref.Id, r => r.Owner?.Id ?? 0);
        long Owner(int region) => owner.GetValueOrDefault(region);
        var (w, h) = (grid.Width, grid.Height);
        Vector3 At(double u, double v) => new((float)(u * 2 - 1), Math.Max(0, BlockMesh.HeightAt(grid, u, v)) + lift, (float)(v * 2 - 1));
        var segments = new List<Segment>();
        for (var y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (var x = 0; x < w; x++)
            {
                var here = geography.RegionOf[grid.Index(x, y)];
                if (here == 0) continue;
                if (x + 1 < w && geography.RegionOf[grid.Index(x + 1, y)] is var right and not 0 && right != here && Owner(right) != Owner(here))
                    segments.Add(new Segment(At((x + 1.0) / w, y / (double)h), At((x + 1.0) / w, (y + 1.0) / h)));
                if (y + 1 < h && geography.RegionOf[grid.Index(x, y + 1)] is var down and not 0 && down != here && Owner(down) != Owner(here))
                    segments.Add(new Segment(At(x / (double)w, (y + 1.0) / h), At((x + 1.0) / w, (y + 1.0) / h)));
            }
        return segments;
    }

    private static long RegionAt(Geography geography, MapPoint point)
    {
        var grid = geography.Terrain;
        var x = (int)Math.Clamp(point.X * grid.Width, 0, grid.Width - 1);
        var y = (int)Math.Clamp(point.Y * grid.Height, 0, grid.Height - 1);
        return geography.RegionOf[grid.Index(x, y)];
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class MarkupTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void LinkedNames_BecomeUrls_AndBracketsAreEscaped()
    {
        var text = new LinkedText("Maren [the Bold] rode to Esk.", new[]
        {
            new TextLink(25, 3, new EntityRef("settlement", 4)),
            new TextLink(0, 16, new EntityRef("char", 12)),
        });
        Assert.Equal("[url=char#12]Maren [lb]the Bold][/url] rode to [url=settlement#4]Esk[/url].", Markup.Bbcode(text));
        Assert.Equal("No links [lb]here].", Markup.Bbcode(LinkedText.Plain("No links [here].")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TimeAway_ReadsInItsLargestUnit()
    {
        Assert.Equal("now", Markup.Elapsed(0));
        Assert.Equal("+1 day", Markup.Elapsed(1));
        Assert.Equal("+4 months", Markup.Elapsed(4 * WorldDate.DaysPerMonth + 7));
        Assert.Equal("+2 years", Markup.Elapsed(2 * WorldDate.DaysPerYear + 40));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheStatusLine_SaysCaughtUpOrHowFarBehind()
    {
        Assert.Equal("Caught up · Year 312, day 196", DigestScreen.StatusLine(WorldDate.FromYmd(312, 7, 16), 0));
        Assert.Equal("Catching up · 1,200 days behind", DigestScreen.StatusLine(new WorldDate(0), 1200));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The floating world block (sub-project 4 §3): terrain, sea, strata walls, markers, realm borders and digest pins,
/// seen through the orbit camera. Geography is built once; markers and borders are rebuilt when the map changes.
/// Drag to orbit, scroll to descend; clicking near a marker raises <see cref="Picked"/>.
/// </summary>
public partial class Diorama : Node3D
{
    public const float PickRadius = 16f;

    private Geography? _geography;
    private bool _dragging;
    private Vector2 _pressedAt;
    private readonly List<Marker> _markers = new();
    private readonly List<Pin> _pins = new();

    public OrbitRig Rig { get; } = new();
    public Camera3D Camera { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D Block { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D MarkerLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D PinLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D BorderLines { get; private set; } = null!;
    public IReadOnlyList<Marker> Markers => _markers;
    public IReadOnlyList<Pin> Pins => _pins;

    /// <summary>A marker or pin was clicked.</summary>
    public event Action<EntityRef>? Picked;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Diorama";
        Camera = new Camera3D { Name = "Camera", Fov = 32, Near = 0.01f, Far = 50 };
        AddChild(Camera);
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Sun", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-55, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.6f, ShadowEnabled = true });
        // A soft fill from the viewer's side keeps the strata walls readable.
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Fill", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-15, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.45f });
        AddChild(new WorldEnvironment
        {
            Name = "Environment",
            Environment = new Godot.Environment
            {
                BackgroundMode = Godot.Environment.BGMode.Color, BackgroundColor = Ui.C(Palette.Ground),
                AmbientLightSource = Godot.Environment.AmbientSource.Color, AmbientLightColor = Colors.White, AmbientLightEnergy = 0.55f,
            },
        });
        Block = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Block" };
        AddChild(Block);
        BorderLines = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Borders" };
        AddChild(BorderLines);
        MarkerLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Markers" };
        AddChild(MarkerLayer);
        PinLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Pins" };
        AddChild(PinLayer);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    /// <summary>Builds the terrain block, its walls and the sea (once per world).</summary>
    public void Build(Geography geography, int resolution = 128)
    {
        _geography = geography;
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Top(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Sides(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        Block.Mesh = mesh;
        var water = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(water, BlockMesh.WaterPlane(), new StandardMaterial3D
        {
            AlbedoColor = Ui.C(BlockMesh.Water, 0.72f), Transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TransparencyEnum.Alpha, Roughness = 0.3f,
        });
        var sea = GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water") ?? new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Water" };
        sea.Mesh = water;
        if (sea.GetParent() is null) AddChild(sea);
    }

    /// <summary>Places settlements, structures and borders for a map (today's or a past year's).</summary>
    public void Show(MapScreen map)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        Ui.Clear(MarkerLayer);
        _markers.Clear();
        _markers.AddRange(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.For(map, _geography));
        foreach (var marker in _markers) MarkerLayer.AddChild(MarkerNode(marker));
        BorderLines.Mesh = DashedLines(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.Borders(map, _geography), Palette.Ink);
    }

    /// <summary>The digest's pins as stems over their places (the screen draws their numbered labels).</summary>
    public void ShowPins(IEnumerable<Pin> pins)
    {
        Ui.Clear(PinLayer);
        _pins.Clear();
        _pins.AddRange(pins);
        foreach (var pin in _pins)
        {
            var node = new Node3D { Name = $"Pin{pin.Number}", Position = ToGodot(pin.Position) };
            node.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D
            {
                Name = "Stem", Position = new Vector3(0, -Client.Markers.PinLift / 2, 0),
                Mesh = new CylinderMesh { TopRadius = 0.0015f, BottomRadius = 0.0015f, Height = Client.Markers.PinLift },
                MaterialOverride = Flat(Palette.Ink),
            });
            node.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Head", Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = 0.006f, Height = 0.012f }, MaterialOverride = Flat(Palette.Ink) });
            PinLayer.AddChild(node);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Eases the camera to a place on the map.</summary>
    public void Focus(MapPoint point, Zoom? zoom = null)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        var at = Client.Projection.ToBlock(_geography.Terrain, point, 0);
        Rig.Focus(at, zoom);
    }

    /// <summary>The marker or pin drawn nearest a screen point, within <see cref="PickRadius"/> pixels.</summary>
    public EntityRef? Pick(Vector2 screen)
    {
        var candidates = _pins.Select(p => (p.Place, p.Position)).Concat(_markers.Select(m => (m.Ref, m.Position)));
        EntityRef? best = null;
        var bestDistance = PickRadius;
        foreach (var (entity, position) in candidates)
        {
            var world = ToGodot(position);
            if (Camera.IsPositionBehind(world)) continue;
            var distance = Camera.UnprojectPosition(world).DistanceTo(screen);
            if (distance < bestDistance) (best, bestDistance) = (entity, distance);
        }
        return best;
    }

    /// <summary>Where a block-space point appears on screen.</summary>
    public Vector2 ScreenOf(Rgb position) => Camera.UnprojectPosition(ToGodot(position));

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        Rig.Update((float)delta);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    public override void _UnhandledInput(InputEvent input)
    {
        switch (input)
        {
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left } button:
                if (button.Pressed) (_dragging, _pressedAt) = (true, button.Position);
                else
                {
                    _dragging = false;
                    if (button.Position.DistanceTo(_pressedAt) < 4 && Pick(button.Position) is { } picked) Picked?.Invoke(picked);
                }
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelUp, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelDown, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(-1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseMotion motion when _dragging:
                Rig.Drag(motion.Relative.X, motion.Relative.Y);
                break;
        }
    }

    private void UpdateCamera()
    {
        if (Camera is null) return;
        Camera.Position = ToGodot(Rig.Eye);
        Camera.LookAt(ToGodot(Rig.Target), Vector3.Up);
    }

    private static Node3D MarkerNode(Marker marker)
    {
        var node = new Node3D { Name = $"{marker.Ref.Kind}{marker.Ref.Id}", Position = ToGodot(marker.Position) };
        var size = marker.Size;
        switch (marker.Shape)
        {
            case MarkerShape.Settlement:
                node.AddChild(Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size * 0.8f, size) }, marker.Colour, size * 0.4f));
                break;
            case MarkerShape.Labyrinth:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 1.4f, Palette.Card, size));
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 0.8f, marker.Colour, size * 1.05f));
                break;
            default:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size, marker.Colour, size));
                break;
        }
        return node;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Diamond(float size, Rgb colour, float lift)
    {
        var diamond = Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size, size) }, colour, lift);
        diamond.RotationDegrees = new Vector3(45, 0, 45);
        return diamond;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Solid(Mesh mesh, Rgb colour, float lift) =>
        new() { Mesh = mesh, Position = new Vector3(0, lift, 0), MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), Roughness = 0.8f } };

    private static StandardMaterial3D Flat(Rgb colour) =>
        new() { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded };

    private static StandardMaterial3D VertexColours() => new() { VertexColorUseAsAlbedo = true, Roughness = 0.95f };

    /// <summary>Every other segment, so borders read as the design's dashed lines.</summary>
    private static ArrayMesh DashedLines(IReadOnlyList<Segment> segments, Rgb colour)
    {
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        var points = new List<Vector3>();
        for (var i = 0; i < segments.Count; i += 2)
        {
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].From));
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].To));
        }
        if (points.Count == 0) return mesh;
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = points.ToArray();
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Lines, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(0, Flat(colour));
        return mesh;
    }

    private static void AddSurface(ArrayMesh mesh, MeshData data, Material material)
    {
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = data.Positions.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Normal] = data.Normals.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Color] = data.Colors.Select(c => Ui.C(c).SrgbToLinear()).ToArray();
        // Godot treats clockwise triangles as front faces; the client's indices are counter-clockwise.
        var indices = new int[data.Indices.Length];
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.Length; i += 3) (indices[i], indices[i + 1], indices[i + 2]) = (data.Indices[i], data.Indices[i + 2], data.Indices[i + 1]);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Index] = indices;
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Triangles, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(mesh.GetSurfaceCount() - 1, material);
    }

    public static Vector3 ToGodot(Rgb v) => new(v.X, v.Y, v.Z);
}
```

Replace `game/scripts/Main.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The root of the game (sub-project 4 §2): opens or creates the world, catches up on a worker thread with progress,
/// then keeps it live, and hosts the diorama and the screens. The world file comes from `-- --world &lt;path&gt;`, else
/// user://worlds/world.fw.db (created as a medium world with a century of prehistory on first launch).
/// </summary>
public partial class Main : Control
{
    public const double LiveInterval = 2.0;

    private Session? _session;
    private double _sinceStep;

    public Session? Session => _session;

    public ScreenRouter Router { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>One line of state: creating, catching up, caught up.</summary>
    public Label Status { get; private set; } = null!;

    public bool CaughtUp { get; private set; }

    public Diorama Diorama { get; private set; } = null!;

    /// <summary>The layer the current screen's controls live in, above the diorama.</summary>
    public Control Screens { get; private set; } = null!;

    public Panel StatusLight { get; private set; } = null!;

    public Geography? Geography { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>The digest built when the world caught up (it is marked seen at once, so it is kept, not rebuilt).</summary>
    public DigestScreen? Digest { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>The control showing the current screen, if that screen has one yet.</summary>
    public Control? Current { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>Lets a test (or another host) supply an open session before the node enters the tree.</summary>
    public void Use(Session session) => _session = session;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Main";
        Theme = Ui.CreateTheme();
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        // The diorama draws behind every card; the root lets clicks it does not use fall through to it.
        MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        Diorama = new Diorama();
        AddChild(Diorama);
        Screens = new Control { Name = "Screens", MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        Screens.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(Screens);
        var pill = Ui.Capsule("StatusPill", Ui.C(Palette.Card), Ui.C(Palette.Border), 14, 8);
        Ui.Place(pill, 1, 1, -40, -40);
        var row = Ui.Row("Row", 8);
        StatusLight = Ui.Dot(Palette.Muted, 8, "Light");
        row.AddChild(StatusLight);
        Status = Ui.Body("Opening…", "Status", Palette.Body, 12);
        Status.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        row.AddChild(Status);
        pill.AddChild(row);
        AddChild(pill);
        _ = StartAsync();
    }

    private async Task StartAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_session is null)
            {
                var path = WorldPath();
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(path))
                {
                    Status.Text = "Creating your world…";
                    _session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, (ulong)DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.UtcTicks, WorldSize.Medium, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));
                }
                else _session = Session.Open(path, () => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
            }
            var progress = new Progress<WorldDate>(date => Status.Text = $"Catching up… {date}");
            await _session.CatchUpAsync(progress);
            OnCaughtUp();
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            Status.Text = $"Could not open the world: {e.Message}";
            GD.PushError(e.ToString());
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Called once the world is current: subclasses and later tasks show the screens here.</summary>
    protected virtual void OnCaughtUp()
    {
        Geography = _session!.Read((w, _) => w.Geography);
        if (Geography is not null)
        {
            Diorama.Build(Geography);
            Diorama.Show(_session.Map());
        }
        Digest = _session.Digest();
        _session.MarkSeen();
        Diorama.Picked += Router.Open;
        Router.Changed += Show;
        CaughtUp = true;
        UpdateStatus();
        Show(Router.Current);
    }

    /// <summary>Replaces the current screen's controls (and the diorama's pins) for a screen.</summary>
    public void Show(Screen screen)
    {
        Ui.Clear(Screens);
        Diorama.ShowPins(screen.Kind == ScreenKind.Digest && Digest is not null && Geography is not null
            ? Markers.Pins(Digest, Geography) : Array.Empty<Pin>());
        Current = screen.Kind switch
        {
            ScreenKind.Digest when Digest is not null => new DigestView { Host = this, Digest = Digest },
            _ => null,
        };
        if (Current is not null) Screens.AddChild(Current);
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (!CaughtUp || _session is null) return;
        _sinceStep += delta;
        if (_sinceStep < LiveInterval) return;
        _sinceStep = 0;
        if (_session.Step().Days > 0) UpdateStatus();
    }

    public void UpdateStatus()
    {
        if (_session?.TryRead((w, _) => DigestScreen.StatusLine(w.Date, _session.DaysBehind)) is { } line)
        {
            Status.Text = line;
            StatusLight.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", Ui.Round(Ui.C(_session.DaysBehind > 0 ? Palette.Conflict : Palette.Realm), null));
        }
    }

    public override void _ExitTree() => _session?.Dispose();

    private static string WorldPath()
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = Array.IndexOf(args, "--world");
        return at >= 0 && at + 1 < args.Length
            ? args[at + 1]
            : System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "worlds", "world.fw.db");
    }
}
```

Replace `game/scripts/Ui.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Chronicle;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>The design's look (sub-project 4 §5) and small builders for the controls every screen uses.</summary>
public static class Ui
{
    private static readonly FontFile Display = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Italic.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile DisplayUpright = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Regular.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile Sans = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/Geist[wght].ttf");

    public static Color C(Rgb rgb, float alpha = 1) => new(rgb.X, rgb.Y, rgb.Z, alpha);

    public static Theme CreateTheme()
    {
        var theme = new Theme { DefaultFont = Sans, DefaultFontSize = 13 };
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Label", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Button", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_hover_color", "Button", C(Palette.Body));
        theme.SetStylebox("normal", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("hover", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("pressed", "Button", Pill(filled: true));
        theme.SetStylebox("focus", "Button", new StyleBoxEmpty());
        theme.SetColor("font_pressed_color", "Button", Colors.White);
        return theme;
    }

    public static StyleBoxFlat Pill(bool filled) => new()
    {
        BgColor = filled ? C(Palette.Ink) : new Color(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BorderColor = C(Palette.Ink),
        BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
        CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 999, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 999, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 999, CornerRadiusTopRight = 999,
        ContentMarginLeft = 14, ContentMarginRight = 14, ContentMarginTop = 7, ContentMarginBottom = 7,
    };

    /// <summary>The floating card: warm white, hairline border, soft shadow, 16 px corners.</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Card(string name, float width)
    {
        var card = new PanelContainer { Name = name, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(width, 0) };
        card.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = C(Palette.Card), BorderColor = C(Palette.Border),
            BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
            CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 16, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 16, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 16, CornerRadiusTopRight = 16,
            ShadowColor = new Color(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.09f, 0.06f), ShadowSize = 20, ShadowOffset = new Godot.Vector2(0, 12),
            ContentMarginLeft = 22, ContentMarginRight = 22, ContentMarginTop = 22, ContentMarginBottom = 22,
        });
        return card;
    }

    /// <summary>The small uppercase label above sections ("OVERNIGHT", "TIES").</summary>
    public static Label Kicker(string text, string name = "Kicker")
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text.ToUpperInvariant() };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", 11);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(Palette.Muted));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>Italic serif display text ("Year 312.", names on pages).</summary>
    public static Label Title(string text, int size, string name = "Title", bool italic = true)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontOverride("font", italic ? Display : DisplayUpright);
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        return label;
    }

    public static Label Body(string text, string name = "Body", Rgb? colour = null, int size = 13)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(colour ?? Palette.Body));
        return label;
    }

    public static Button Button(string text, string name, bool primary = false)
    {
        var button = new Button { Name = name, Text = text, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(0, 34) };
        if (primary)
        {
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("normal", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("hover", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", Colors.White);
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", Colors.White);
        }
        return button;
    }

    public static VBoxContainer Column(string name, int gap)
    {
        var column = new VBoxContainer { Name = name };
        column.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return column;
    }

    public static HBoxContainer Row(string name, int gap)
    {
        var row = new HBoxContainer { Name = name };
        row.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return row;
    }

    /// <summary>Text whose names are links: clicking one calls <paramref name="open"/> with its entity.</summary>
    public static RichTextLabel Linked(LinkedText text, string name, Action<EntityRef> open, int size = 13, Rgb? colour = null, bool serif = false)
    {
        var label = new RichTextLabel
        {
            Name = name, BbcodeEnabled = true, FitContent = true, ScrollActive = false, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart,
            Text = Markup.Bbcode(text), MetaUnderlined = true, SizeFlagsHorizontal = Control.SizeFlags.ExpandFill,
        };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("normal_font_size", size);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("default_color", C(colour ?? Palette.Body));
        if (serif) label.AddThemeFontOverride("normal_font", DisplayUpright);
        label.MetaClicked += meta => open(Importance.ParseRef(meta.AsString()));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>A small filled circle (category dots, the status light).</summary>
    public static Panel Dot(Rgb colour, float size = 8, string name = "Dot")
    {
        var dot = new Panel { Name = name, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(size, size), SizeFlagsVertical = Control.SizeFlags.ShrinkCenter };
        dot.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", Round(C(colour), null));
        return dot;
    }

    /// <summary>A pill-shaped panel (labels on pins, the status pill).</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Capsule(string name, Color fill, Color? border = null, int padX = 10, int padY = 3)
    {
        var pill = new PanelContainer { Name = name };
        var style = Round(fill, border);
        (style.ContentMarginLeft, style.ContentMarginRight, style.ContentMarginTop, style.ContentMarginBottom) = (padX, padX, padY, padY);
        pill.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", style);
        return pill;
    }

    /// <summary>A row with a hairline rule above it, like the digest's list.</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Ruled(string name, Control content, bool ruleBelow = false)
    {
        var row = new PanelContainer { Name = name };
        row.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = new Color(0, 0, 0, 0), BorderColor = C(Palette.Rule), BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthBottom = ruleBelow ? 1 : 0,
            ContentMarginTop = 10, ContentMarginBottom = 10,
        });
        row.AddChild(content);
        return row;
    }

    public static StyleBoxFlat Round(Color fill, Color? border)
    {
        var style = new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = fill, CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 999, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 999, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 999, CornerRadiusTopRight = 999,
        };
        if (border is { } b) (style.BorderColor, style.BorderWidthBottom, style.BorderWidthTop, style.BorderWidthLeft, style.BorderWidthRight) = (b, 1, 1, 1, 1);
        return style;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Pins a control to a corner of its parent: anchors (0 or 1 across and down) plus an offset to its near corner;
    /// a control anchored right or bottom grows left or up as its content grows, so the margin holds.
    /// </summary>
    public static T Place<T>(T control, float anchorX, float anchorY, float x, float y) where T : Control
    {
        (control.AnchorLeft, control.AnchorRight, control.AnchorTop, control.AnchorBottom) = (anchorX, anchorX, anchorY, anchorY);
        (control.OffsetLeft, control.OffsetRight, control.OffsetTop, control.OffsetBottom) = (x, x, y, y);
        control.GrowHorizontal = anchorX > 0 ? Control.GrowDirection.Begin : Control.GrowDirection.End;
        control.GrowVertical = anchorY > 0 ? Control.GrowDirection.Begin : Control.GrowDirection.End;
        return control;
    }

    public static void Clear(Node node)
    {
        foreach (var child in node.GetChildren())
        {
            node.RemoveChild(child);
            child.QueueFree();
        }
    }
}
```

`game/scripts/screens/DigestView.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The *Morning digest* (sub-project 4 §4): the year and time away on the left, the control card on the right with
/// the Digest / Full chronicle toggle, the headline and its "Why did this happen?", the other items, and numbered
/// pins over their places on the diorama. Clicking an item flies to its place; clicking a pin opens its story.
/// </summary>
public partial class DigestView : Control
{
    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;
    public DigestScreen Digest { get; init; } = null!;

    public bool ShowingChronicle { get; private set; }
    public VBoxContainer Content { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Control PinLayer { get; private set; } = null!;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "DigestScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;

        PinLayer = new Control { Name = "Pins", MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        PinLayer.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(PinLayer);
        foreach (var pin in Host.Diorama.Pins) PinLayer.AddChild(PinButton(pin));

        var intro = Ui.Column("Intro", 10);
        Ui.Place(intro, 0, 0, 56, 48);
        intro.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(340, 0);
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Kicker($"{Digest.WorldName} · Morning digest"));
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Title(Digest.Title, 72));
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Body(Digest.Subtitle, "Subtitle", Palette.Muted));
        AddChild(intro);

        var card = Ui.Card("Card", 360);
        Ui.Place(card, 1, 0, -40, 40);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 20);
        card.AddChild(column);
        var top = Ui.Row("Top", 0);
        var overnight = Ui.Kicker("Overnight");
        overnight.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        top.AddChild(overnight);
        top.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(Markup.Elapsed(Digest.ElapsedDays), "Elapsed"));
        column.AddChild(top);
        var toggle = Ui.Row("Toggle", 6);
        var digestButton = Ui.Button("Digest", "DigestButton", primary: true);
        var chronicleButton = Ui.Button("Full chronicle", "ChronicleButton");
        digestButton.Pressed += () => ShowChronicle(false);
        chronicleButton.Pressed += () => ShowChronicle(true);
        toggle.AddChild(digestButton);
        toggle.AddChild(chronicleButton);
        column.AddChild(toggle);
        Content = Ui.Column("Content", 20);
        column.AddChild(Content);
        AddChild(card);

        var hint = Ui.Column("Hint", 2);
        hint.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(300, 0);
        Ui.Place(hint, 0, 1, 56, -44);
        hint.AddChild(Ui.Title("Drag to orbit. Scroll to descend.", 15));
        hint.AddChild(Ui.Body("Click a numbered pin to read its story.", "HintBody", Palette.Muted, 12));
        AddChild(hint);

        ShowChronicle(false);
    }

    /// <summary>Switches the card between the digest and the full chronicle of the last two years.</summary>
    public void ShowChronicle(bool chronicle)
    {
        ShowingChronicle = chronicle;
        Ui.Clear(Content);
        var toggle = GetNode<HBoxContainer>("Card/Column/Toggle");
        Restyle(toggle.GetNode<Button>("DigestButton"), !chronicle);
        Restyle(toggle.GetNode<Button>("ChronicleButton"), chronicle);
        if (chronicle) FillChronicle();
        else FillDigest();
    }

    private void FillDigest()
    {
        if (Digest.Headline is { } headline)
        {
            var block = Ui.Column("Headline", 8);
            var kicker = Ui.Row("KickerRow", 8);
            kicker.AddChild(Ui.Dot(Palette.For(headline.Category)));
            kicker.AddChild(Ui.Kicker($"{headline.Number} · {headline.Kicker}"));
            block.AddChild(kicker);
            block.AddChild(Ui.Linked(headline.Title, "HeadlineTitle", Host.Router.Open, 30, Palette.Ink, serif: true));
            block.AddChild(Ui.Linked(headline.Body, "HeadlineBody", Host.Router.Open));
            if (headline.CanExplain)
            {
                var why = Ui.Button("Why did this happen?", "Why", primary: true);
                why.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ShrinkBegin;
                why.Pressed += () => Host.Router.Explain(headline.EventId);
                block.AddChild(why);
            }
            Content.AddChild(block);
        }
        else Content.AddChild(Ui.Body("Nothing of note happened while you were away.", "Empty", Palette.Muted));

        var items = Ui.Column("Items", 0);
        for (var i = 0; i < Digest.Items.Count; i++)
        {
            var item = Digest.Items[i];
            var row = Ui.Row("Row", 12);
            var number = Ui.Body(item.Number.ToString(), "Number", Palette.Muted);
            number.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(14, 0);
            row.AddChild(number);
            row.AddChild(Ui.Linked(item.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 13, Palette.Ink));
            var ruled = Ui.Ruled($"Item{item.Number}", row, ruleBelow: i == Digest.Items.Count - 1);
            var eventId = item.EventId;
            ruled.GuiInput += input =>
            {
                if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true }) FlyTo(eventId);
            };
            items.AddChild(ruled);
        }
        Content.AddChild(items);
    }

    private void FillChronicle()
    {
        // This year and last, newest first: early in a year this year alone is nearly empty.
        var from = Math.Max(1, Digest.Year - 1);
        var first = Host.Session!.Chronicle(from, Digest.Year);
        var page = first.Pages > 1 ? Host.Session.Chronicle(from, Digest.Year, page: first.Pages) : first;
        var scroll = new ScrollContainer { Name = "Chronicle", CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(0, 520), HorizontalScrollMode = ScrollContainer.ScrollMode.Disabled };
        var list = Ui.Column("List", 12);
        list.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        foreach (var season in page.Seasons.Reverse())
        {
            list.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(season.Label, "Season"));
            foreach (var row in season.Rows.Reverse())
            {
                var line = Ui.Row("Row", 8);
                line.AddChild(Ui.Dot(Palette.For(row.Category), 6));
                line.AddChild(Ui.Linked(row.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 12));
                list.AddChild(line);
            }
        }
        if (page.Total == 0) list.AddChild(Ui.Body("Nothing is recorded for these years yet.", "Empty", Palette.Muted));
        scroll.AddChild(list);
        Content.AddChild(scroll);
    }

    /// <summary>Eases the camera to the place of a digest entry, when it has one.</summary>
    public void FlyTo(long eventId)
    {
        if (Digest.Pins.FirstOrDefault(p => p.EventId == eventId) is { } pin) Host.Diorama.Focus(pin.Position, Zoom.Region);
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        foreach (var child in PinLayer.GetChildren())
            if (child is PinTag tag && Host.Diorama.Pins.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Number == tag.Number) is { } pin)
            {
                tag.Visible = !Host.Diorama.Camera.IsPositionBehind(Diorama.ToGodot(pin.Position));
                tag.Position = Host.Diorama.ScreenOf(pin.Position) - new Vector2(11, 11);
            }
    }

    private PinTag PinButton(Pin pin)
    {
        var tag = new PinTag { Name = $"Pin{pin.Number}", Number = pin.Number, EventId = pin.EventId, MouseDefaultCursorShape = CursorShape.PointingHand };
        tag.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", 6);
        var number = Ui.Capsule("Number", Ui.C(Palette.Ink), padX: 7, padY: 2);
        number.AddChild(Ui.Body(pin.Number.ToString(), "Digit", Palette.Card, 11));
        var label = Ui.Capsule("Label", Ui.C(Palette.Card), Ui.C(Palette.Border));
        label.AddChild(Ui.Body(pin.Label, "Text", Palette.Ink, 12));
        tag.AddChild(number);
        tag.AddChild(label);
        foreach (var text in tag.FindChildren("*", "Label", owned: false)) ((Label)text).AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        foreach (var capsule in new Control[] { number, label }) capsule.MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        tag.GuiInput += input =>
        {
            if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true }) Host.Router.Explain(tag.EventId);
        };
        return tag;
    }

    private static void Restyle(Button button, bool active)
    {
        button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("normal", Ui.Pill(filled: active));
        button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("hover", Ui.Pill(filled: active));
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", active ? Colors.White : Ui.C(Palette.Ink));
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", active ? Colors.White : Ui.C(Palette.Body));
    }
}

/// <summary>A numbered pin's label on screen: the number in a black circle and the place in a pill.</summary>
public partial class PinTag : HBoxContainer
{
    public int Number { get; init; }
    public long EventId { get; init; }
}
```

Replace `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Linq;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Runs the real scenes headless against a fresh tiny world and checks what they show (sub-project 4 §7). Exits 0 when
/// every check passes, 1 otherwise; CI runs it with `godot --headless --path game res://tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn`.
/// </summary>
public partial class HeadlessChecks : Node
{
    private readonly List<string> _failures = new();
    private int _checks;

    public override void _Ready() => _ = RunAsync();

    private async Task RunAsync()
    {
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "checks", $"check-{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.fw.db");
        try
        {
            var session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => System.DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, prehistoryYears: 8));
            var main = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://scenes/Main.tscn").Instantiate<Main>();
            main.Use(session);
            AddChild(main);
            await Until(() => main.CaughtUp, "the world catches up");
            await RunChecks(main);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Fail($"unexpected {e}");
        }
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "", ".bak1", ".bak2", ".bak3", "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path + suffix)) System.IO.File.Delete(path + suffix);
        GD.Print(_failures.Count == 0 ? $"HEADLESS CHECKS PASSED ({_checks})" : $"HEADLESS CHECKS FAILED: {string.Join("; ", _failures)}");
        GetTree().Quit(_failures.Count == 0 ? 0 : 1);
    }

    /// <summary>The checks for each screen; later tasks add theirs.</summary>
    private async Task RunChecks(Main main)
    {
        Check(main.Status.Text.StartsWith("Caught up · "), $"status reads caught up (\"{main.Status.Text}\")");
        await Frames(2);
        await DioramaChecks(main);
        await DigestChecks(main);
    }

    private async Task DigestChecks(Main main)
    {
        main.Router.Home();
        main.Diorama.Rig.Reset();
        await Frames(60);
        var view = main.Current as DigestView;
        Check(view is not null, "the digest is the first screen");
        if (view is null) return;
        var digest = main.Digest!;
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Title").Text == digest.Title, "the digest shows the year");
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Subtitle").Text == digest.Subtitle, "and the time away");
        Check(main.Session!.Digest().Items.Count == 0 && main.Session.Digest().Headline is null, "showing the digest marks it seen");
        Check(digest.Headline is null || view.Content.GetNode<RichTextLabel>("Headline/HeadlineTitle").GetParsedText() == digest.Headline.Title.Text,
            "the headline is shown");
        Check(view.Content.GetNodeOrNull("Items")?.GetChildCount() == digest.Items.Count, $"every item is listed ({digest.Items.Count})");
        Check(view.PinLayer.GetChildCount() == digest.Pins.Count && main.Diorama.Pins.Count == digest.Pins.Count, $"one pin per place ({digest.Pins.Count})");
        await Shot("digest");

        if (digest.Pins.Count > 0)
        {
            var pin = view.PinLayer.GetChild<PinTag>(0);
            var expected = main.Diorama.ScreenOf(main.Diorama.Pins[0].Position) - new Godot.Vector2(11, 11);
            Check(pin.Position.DistanceTo(expected) < 1, "pins follow their places on screen");
            view.FlyTo(digest.Pins[0].EventId);
            await Frames(2);
            Check(main.Diorama.Rig.GoalDistance == OrbitRig.DistanceFor(Zoom.Region), "an item flies the camera to its place");
        }

        view.ShowChronicle(true);
        await Frames(2);
        Check(view.Content.GetNodeOrNull("Chronicle") is not null && view.ShowingChronicle, "the toggle shows the full chronicle");
        await Shot("digest-chronicle");
        view.ShowChronicle(false);

        if (digest.Headline is { CanExplain: true } headline)
        {
            view.Content.GetNode<Button>("Headline/Why").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Check(main.Router.Current == new Screen(ScreenKind.Why, headline.EventId), "\"Why did this happen?\" opens the why chain");
            main.Router.Back();
            await Frames(2);
            Check(main.Current is DigestView, "Back returns to the digest");
        }
    }

    private async Task DioramaChecks(Main main)
    {
        var diorama = main.Diorama;
        var map = main.Session!.Map();
        Check(diorama.Block.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } block && block.GetSurfaceCount() == 2, "the block has a top and walls");
        Check(diorama.GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water")?.Mesh is not null, "the sea is drawn");
        Check(diorama.MarkerLayer.GetChildCount() == map.Settlements.Count(s => s.Position is not null) + map.Structures.Count(s => s.Position is not null),
            $"one marker per settlement and structure ({diorama.MarkerLayer.GetChildCount()})");
        Check(map.Borders.Count == 0 || diorama.BorderLines.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } lines && lines.GetSurfaceCount() == 1, "realm borders are drawn");

        diorama.Rig.Reset();
        await Frames(90);
        await Shot("diorama");
        Check(diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Eye)) < 0.01f, "the camera follows the rig");
        var target = diorama.Markers[0];
        var onScreen = diorama.ScreenOf(target.Position);
        Check(diorama.Pick(onScreen) is { } picked && (picked == target.Ref || diorama.Markers.Any(m => m.Ref == picked && diorama.ScreenOf(m.Position).DistanceTo(onScreen) < 1)),
            "clicking a marker picks it");
        Check(diorama.Pick(new Godot.Vector2(-500, -500)) is null, "clicking empty space picks nothing");

        var settlement = map.Settlements.First(s => s.Position is not null);
        diorama.Focus(settlement.Position!, Zoom.Town);
        await Frames(120);
        await Shot("diorama-town");
        Check(diorama.Rig.NearestZoom == Zoom.Town && diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Target)) < 1f, "focusing descends to the town");
    }

    /// <summary>With `-- --shots &lt;dir&gt;` (and a real display), saves what the screen shows, for visual review.</summary>
    private async Task Shot(string name)
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = System.Array.IndexOf(args, "--shots");
        if (at < 0 || at + 1 >= args.Length || DisplayServer.GetName() == "headless") return;
        await Frames(4);
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(args[at + 1]);
        GetViewport().GetTexture().GetImage().SavePng(System.IO.Path.Combine(args[at + 1], name + ".png"));
    }

    private void Check(bool ok, string what)
    {
        _checks++;
        if (!ok) Fail(what);
    }

    private void Fail(string what)
    {
        _failures.Add(what);
        GD.PrintErr("FAIL: " + what);
    }

    private async Task Frames(int n)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
    }

    private async Task Until(System.Func<bool> condition, string what, int maxFrames = 600)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < maxFrames && !condition(); i++) await Frames(1);
        Check(condition(), what);
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Markup.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Text;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

namespace FantasyWorld.Client;

/// <summary>Text for the screens: linked names as rich-text markup, and short spans of world time.</summary>
public static class Markup
{
    /// <summary>
    /// BBCode for a rich-text label: each linked span becomes [url=kind#id]…[/url] (the label raises the ref when
    /// clicked), and brackets in the prose are escaped so names like "[the Grey]" cannot inject markup.
    /// </summary>
    public static string Bbcode(LinkedText text)
    {
        var builder = new StringBuilder();
        var at = 0;
        foreach (var link in text.Links.OrderBy(l => l.Start))
        {
            if (link.Start < at || link.Start + link.Length > text.Text.Length) continue;
            builder.Append(Escape(text.Text[at..link.Start]));
            builder.Append("[url=").Append(link.Entity).Append(']').Append(Escape(text.Text.Substring(link.Start, link.Length))).Append("[/url]");
            at = link.Start + link.Length;
        }
        return builder.Append(Escape(text.Text[at..])).ToString();
    }

    public static string Escape(string text) => text.Replace("[", "[lb]");

    /// <summary>The control card's "+4 months": world time away in its largest whole unit.</summary>
    public static string Elapsed(long days) => days switch
    {
        <= 0 => "now",
        < WorldDate.DaysPerMonth => $"+{days} day{(days == 1 ? "" : "s")}",
        < WorldDate.DaysPerYear => $"+{days / WorldDate.DaysPerMonth} month{(days / WorldDate.DaysPerMonth == 1 ? "" : "s")}",
        _ => $"+{days / WorldDate.DaysPerYear} year{(days / WorldDate.DaysPerYear == 1 ? "" : "s")}",
    };
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Chronicle;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;

public sealed record DigestPin(int Number, EntityRef Place, string Label, MapPoint Position, long EventId);

public sealed record Headline(int Number, Category Category, string Kicker, LinkedText Title, LinkedText Body, long EventId, bool CanExplain);

public sealed record DigestItem(int Number, Category Category, LinkedText Text, long EventId);

/// <summary>
/// The *Morning digest* screen (sub-project 2 §3.1): the digest's events numbered by importance (1 = the headline),
/// map pins for those with a place, a title and subtitle for the time away, and the catch-up status line.
/// </summary>
public sealed record DigestScreen(
    string WorldName, int Year, string Title, string Subtitle, long ElapsedDays, string Status,
    IReadOnlyList<DigestPin> Pins, Headline? Headline, IReadOnlyList<DigestItem> Items)
{
    private static readonly string[] Numbers = { "no", "one", "two", "three", "four", "five", "six", "seven", "eight", "nine", "ten", "eleven", "twelve" };

    public static DigestScreen Build(World world, EventLog log, DigestView digest, WorldDate since, long daysBehind = 0, INarrator? narrator = null)
    {
        var templates = new TemplateNarrator(world);
        var nameOf = Names.Of(world);
        var name = DisplayName(world);
        var ranked = digest.Entries.OrderByDescending(e => e.Score).ThenBy(e => e.Event.Id).Select((e, i) => (Number: i + 1, e.Event)).ToList();
        LinkedText Link(string text, WorldEvent ev) => LinkedText.Link(text, LinkedText.Mentions(ev), nameOf);

        var pins = ranked.Where(r => r.Event.Place is { } p && MapPoint.Of(world, p) is not null)
            .DistinctBy(r => r.Event.Place!.Value).Take(5)
            .Select(r => new DigestPin(r.Number, r.Event.Place!.Value, nameOf(r.Event.Place!.Value) ?? r.Event.Place!.Value.ToString(),
                MapPoint.Of(world, r.Event.Place!.Value)!, r.Event.Id))
            .ToList();
        Headline? headline = null;
        if (ranked.Count > 0)
        {
            var top = ranked[0].Event;
            var body = narrator is null ? Note(top) : narrator.Narrate(top);
            headline = new Headline(1, Categories.Of(top), Categories.Kicker(top), Link(templates.Sentence(top), top), Link(body, top), top.Id, top.Causes.Count > 0);
        }
        var items = ranked.Skip(1).Select(r => new DigestItem(r.Number, Categories.Of(r.Event), Link(templates.Sentence(r.Event), r.Event), r.Event.Id)).ToList();

        var elapsed = Math.Max(0, world.Date.Day - since.Day);
        var subtitle = elapsed == 0
            ? $"Nothing has passed since you last looked at {name}."
            : $"While you were away, {Span(elapsed)} passed in {name}. {Seasons(since, world.Date)}, and {Count(digest.EventCount)} recorded.";
        return new DigestScreen(name, world.Date.Year, $"Year {world.Date.Year}.", subtitle, elapsed, StatusLine(world.Date, daysBehind), pins, headline, items);
    }

    /// <summary>"Caught up · Year 312, day 196", or "Catching up · 12 days behind" while real time is still owed.</summary>
    public static string StatusLine(WorldDate date, long daysBehind) => daysBehind > 0
        ? $"Catching up · {daysBehind.ToString("N0", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} day{(daysBehind == 1 ? "" : "s")} behind"
        : $"Caught up · Year {date.Year}, day {(date.Month - 1) * WorldDate.DaysPerMonth + date.DayOfMonth}";

    /// <summary>The digest a returning reader sees: everything since they last looked, without marking it seen.</summary>
    public static DigestScreen ForReader(World world, EventLog log, IReadOnlySet<EntityRef> followed, long daysBehind = 0, INarrator? narrator = null)
    {
        var since = log.TryGet(world.LastSeenEventId, out var seen) ? seen.Date : new WorldDate(0);
        return Build(world, log, Digest.Build(log, world, world.LastSeenEventId, followed), since, daysBehind, narrator);
    }

    public static string DisplayName(World world) => world.Name.Length > 0 ? world.Name : "The World";

    /// <summary>What the engine knows of why or how: motive, account or lore, else when it happened.</summary>
    private static string Note(WorldEvent ev) =>
        ev.Payload.GetValueOrDefault("account") ?? ev.Payload.GetValueOrDefault("motive") ?? ev.Payload.GetValueOrDefault("lore")
        ?? $"{Digest.SeasonLabel(ev.Date)}.";

    private static string Word(long n) => n < Numbers.Length ? Numbers[n] : n.ToString("N0", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    private static string Span(long days) =>
        days < WorldDate.DaysPerMonth ? Plural(days, "day")
        : days < WorldDate.DaysPerYear ? Plural(days / WorldDate.DaysPerMonth, "month")
        : Plural(days / WorldDate.DaysPerYear, "year");

    private static string Plural(long n, string unit) => $"{Word(n)} {unit}{(n == 1 ? "" : "s")}";

    private static string Count(int events) => events == 1 ? "one event was" : $"{Word(events)} events were";

    private static string Seasons(WorldDate from, WorldDate to) =>
        to.Day - from.Day >= WorldDate.DaysPerYear || from.Season == to.Season && to.Day - from.Day >= 90 ? "The seasons turned"
        : from.Season == to.Season ? $"It is still {to.Season.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}"
        : $"{from.Season} gave way to {to.Season.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}";
}
```

Replace `game/scripts/Diorama.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The floating world block (sub-project 4 §3): terrain, sea, strata walls, markers, realm borders and digest pins,
/// seen through the orbit camera. Geography is built once; markers and borders are rebuilt when the map changes.
/// Drag to orbit, scroll to descend; clicking near a marker raises <see cref="Picked"/>.
/// </summary>
public partial class Diorama : Node3D
{
    public const float PickRadius = 16f;

    private Geography? _geography;
    private bool _dragging;
    private Vector2 _pressedAt;
    private readonly List<Marker> _markers = new();
    private readonly List<Pin> _pins = new();

    public OrbitRig Rig { get; } = new();
    public Camera3D Camera { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D Block { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D MarkerLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D SettlementLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Node3D StructureLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MapLayers Layers { get; private set; } = MapLayers.Default;
    public Node3D PinLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public MeshInstance3D BorderLines { get; private set; } = null!;
    public IReadOnlyList<Marker> Markers => _markers;
    public IReadOnlyList<Pin> Pins => _pins;

    /// <summary>A marker or pin was clicked.</summary>
    public event Action<EntityRef>? Picked;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "Diorama";
        Camera = new Camera3D { Name = "Camera", Fov = 32, Near = 0.01f, Far = 50 };
        AddChild(Camera);
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Sun", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-55, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.6f, ShadowEnabled = true });
        // A soft fill from the viewer's side keeps the strata walls readable.
        AddChild(new DirectionalLight3D { Name = "Fill", RotationDegrees = new Vector3(-15, 35, 0), LightEnergy = 0.45f });
        AddChild(new WorldEnvironment
        {
            Name = "Environment",
            Environment = new Godot.Environment
            {
                BackgroundMode = Godot.Environment.BGMode.Color, BackgroundColor = Ui.C(Palette.Ground),
                AmbientLightSource = Godot.Environment.AmbientSource.Color, AmbientLightColor = Colors.White, AmbientLightEnergy = 0.55f,
            },
        });
        Block = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Block" };
        AddChild(Block);
        BorderLines = new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Borders" };
        AddChild(BorderLines);
        MarkerLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Markers" };
        AddChild(MarkerLayer);
        SettlementLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Settlements" };
        StructureLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Structures" };
        MarkerLayer.AddChild(SettlementLayer);
        MarkerLayer.AddChild(StructureLayer);
        PinLayer = new Node3D { Name = "Pins" };
        AddChild(PinLayer);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    /// <summary>Builds the terrain block, its walls and the sea (once per world).</summary>
    public void Build(Geography geography, int resolution = 128)
    {
        _geography = geography;
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Top(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        AddSurface(mesh, BlockMesh.Sides(geography.Terrain, resolution), VertexColours());
        Block.Mesh = mesh;
        var water = new ArrayMesh();
        AddSurface(water, BlockMesh.WaterPlane(), new StandardMaterial3D
        {
            AlbedoColor = Ui.C(BlockMesh.Water, 0.72f), Transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TransparencyEnum.Alpha, Roughness = 0.3f,
        });
        var sea = GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water") ?? new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Water" };
        sea.Mesh = water;
        if (sea.GetParent() is null) AddChild(sea);
    }

    /// <summary>Places settlements, structures and borders for a map: today's, or the given past year's.</summary>
    public void Show(MapScreen map, int? pastYear = null)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        Ui.Clear(SettlementLayer);
        Ui.Clear(StructureLayer);
        _markers.Clear();
        _markers.AddRange(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.For(map, _geography));
        foreach (var marker in _markers) (marker.Shape == MarkerShape.Settlement ? SettlementLayer : StructureLayer).AddChild(MarkerNode(marker));
        BorderLines.Mesh = DashedLines(FantasyWorld.Client.Markers.Borders(map, _geography), Palette.Ink);
        ShownYear = pastYear;
    }

    /// <summary>The past year the markers show, or null for today.</summary>
    public int? ShownYear { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>Shows or hides the borders, settlements and structures (news tags are the screen's).</summary>
    public void SetLayers(MapLayers layers)
    {
        Layers = layers;
        BorderLines.Visible = layers.HasFlag(MapLayers.Borders);
        SettlementLayer.Visible = layers.HasFlag(MapLayers.Settlements);
        StructureLayer.Visible = layers.HasFlag(MapLayers.Structures);
    }

    /// <summary>The digest's pins as stems over their places (the screen draws their numbered labels).</summary>
    public void ShowPins(IEnumerable<Pin> pins)
    {
        Ui.Clear(PinLayer);
        _pins.Clear();
        _pins.AddRange(pins);
        foreach (var pin in _pins)
        {
            var node = new Node3D { Name = $"Pin{pin.Number}", Position = ToGodot(pin.Position) };
            node.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D
            {
                Name = "Stem", Position = new Vector3(0, -Client.Markers.PinLift / 2, 0),
                Mesh = new CylinderMesh { TopRadius = 0.0015f, BottomRadius = 0.0015f, Height = Client.Markers.PinLift },
                MaterialOverride = Flat(Palette.Ink),
            });
            node.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D { Name = "Head", Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = 0.006f, Height = 0.012f }, MaterialOverride = Flat(Palette.Ink) });
            PinLayer.AddChild(node);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Eases the camera to a place on the map.</summary>
    public void Focus(MapPoint point, Zoom? zoom = null)
    {
        if (_geography is null) return;
        var at = Client.Projection.ToBlock(_geography.Terrain, point, 0);
        Rig.Focus(at, zoom);
    }

    /// <summary>The marker or pin drawn nearest a screen point, within <see cref="PickRadius"/> pixels.</summary>
    public EntityRef? Pick(Vector2 screen)
    {
        var candidates = _pins.Select(p => (p.Place, p.Position)).Concat(_markers
            .Where(m => m.Shape == MarkerShape.Settlement ? SettlementLayer.Visible : StructureLayer.Visible).Select(m => (m.Ref, m.Position)));
        EntityRef? best = null;
        var bestDistance = PickRadius;
        foreach (var (entity, position) in candidates)
        {
            var world = ToGodot(position);
            if (Camera.IsPositionBehind(world)) continue;
            var distance = Camera.UnprojectPosition(world).DistanceTo(screen);
            if (distance < bestDistance) (best, bestDistance) = (entity, distance);
        }
        return best;
    }

    /// <summary>Where a block-space point appears on screen.</summary>
    public Vector2 ScreenOf(Rgb position) => Camera.UnprojectPosition(ToGodot(position));

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        Rig.Update((float)delta);
        UpdateCamera();
    }

    public override void _UnhandledInput(InputEvent input)
    {
        switch (input)
        {
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left } button:
                if (button.Pressed) (_dragging, _pressedAt) = (true, button.Position);
                else
                {
                    _dragging = false;
                    if (button.Position.DistanceTo(_pressedAt) < 4 && Pick(button.Position) is { } picked) Picked?.Invoke(picked);
                }
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelUp, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.WheelDown, Pressed: true }:
                Rig.Scroll(-1);
                break;
            case InputEventMouseMotion motion when _dragging:
                Rig.Drag(motion.Relative.X, motion.Relative.Y);
                break;
        }
    }

    private void UpdateCamera()
    {
        if (Camera is null) return;
        Camera.Position = ToGodot(Rig.Eye);
        Camera.LookAt(ToGodot(Rig.Target), Vector3.Up);
    }

    private static Node3D MarkerNode(Marker marker)
    {
        var node = new Node3D { Name = $"{marker.Ref.Kind}{marker.Ref.Id}", Position = ToGodot(marker.Position) };
        var size = marker.Size;
        switch (marker.Shape)
        {
            case MarkerShape.Settlement:
                node.AddChild(Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size * 0.8f, size) }, marker.Colour, size * 0.4f));
                break;
            case MarkerShape.Labyrinth:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 1.4f, Palette.Card, size));
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size * 0.8f, marker.Colour, size * 1.05f));
                break;
            default:
                node.AddChild(Diamond(size, marker.Colour, size));
                break;
        }
        return node;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Diamond(float size, Rgb colour, float lift)
    {
        var diamond = Solid(new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(size, size, size) }, colour, lift);
        diamond.RotationDegrees = new Vector3(45, 0, 45);
        return diamond;
    }

    private static MeshInstance3D Solid(Mesh mesh, Rgb colour, float lift) =>
        new() { Mesh = mesh, Position = new Vector3(0, lift, 0), MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), Roughness = 0.8f } };

    private static StandardMaterial3D Flat(Rgb colour) =>
        new() { AlbedoColor = Ui.C(colour), ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded };

    private static StandardMaterial3D VertexColours() => new() { VertexColorUseAsAlbedo = true, Roughness = 0.95f };

    /// <summary>Every other segment, so borders read as the design's dashed lines.</summary>
    private static ArrayMesh DashedLines(IReadOnlyList<Segment> segments, Rgb colour)
    {
        var mesh = new ArrayMesh();
        var points = new List<Vector3>();
        for (var i = 0; i < segments.Count; i += 2)
        {
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].From));
            points.Add(ToGodot(segments[i].To));
        }
        if (points.Count == 0) return mesh;
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = points.ToArray();
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Lines, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(0, Flat(colour));
        return mesh;
    }

    private static void AddSurface(ArrayMesh mesh, MeshData data, Material material)
    {
        var arrays = new Godot.Collections.Array();
        arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = data.Positions.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Normal] = data.Normals.Select(ToGodot).ToArray();
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Color] = data.Colors.Select(c => Ui.C(c).SrgbToLinear()).ToArray();
        // Godot treats clockwise triangles as front faces; the client's indices are counter-clockwise.
        var indices = new int[data.Indices.Length];
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.Length; i += 3) (indices[i], indices[i + 1], indices[i + 2]) = (data.Indices[i], data.Indices[i + 2], data.Indices[i + 1]);
        arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Index] = indices;
        mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Triangles, arrays);
        mesh.SurfaceSetMaterial(mesh.GetSurfaceCount() - 1, material);
    }

    public static Vector3 ToGodot(Rgb v) => new(v.X, v.Y, v.Z);
}
```
