```csharp
using Rgb = System.Numerics.Vector3;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Chronicle;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>The design's look (sub-project 4 §5) and small builders for the controls every screen uses.</summary>
public static class Ui
{
    private static readonly FontFile Display = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Italic.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile DisplayUpright = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/InstrumentSerif-Regular.ttf");
    private static readonly FontFile Sans = GD.Load<FontFile>("res://fonts/Geist[wght].ttf");

    public static Color C(Rgb rgb, float alpha = 1) => new(rgb.X, rgb.Y, rgb.Z, alpha);

    public static Theme CreateTheme()
    {
        var theme = new Theme { DefaultFont = Sans, DefaultFontSize = 13 };
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Label", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "Button", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_hover_color", "Button", C(Palette.Body));
        theme.SetStylebox("normal", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("hover", "Button", Pill(filled: false));
        theme.SetStylebox("pressed", "Button", Pill(filled: true));
        theme.SetStylebox("focus", "Button", new StyleBoxEmpty());
        theme.SetColor("font_pressed_color", "Button", Colors.White);
        theme.SetColor("font_color", "CheckBox", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_hover_color", "CheckBox", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_pressed_color", "CheckBox", C(Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetColor("font_disabled_color", "CheckBox", C(Palette.Muted));
        theme.SetIcon("checked", "CheckBox", Box(14, filled: true));
        theme.SetIcon("unchecked", "CheckBox", Box(14, filled: false));
        theme.SetIcon("checked_disabled", "CheckBox", Box(14, filled: true, Palette.Muted));
        theme.SetIcon("unchecked_disabled", "CheckBox", Box(14, filled: false, Palette.Border));
        foreach (var state in new[] { "normal", "hover", "pressed", "focus", "disabled", "hover_pressed" })
            theme.SetStylebox(state, "CheckBox", new StyleBoxEmpty());
        theme.SetStylebox("slider", "HSlider", new StyleBoxFlat { BgColor = C(Palette.Border), ContentMarginTop = 1.5f, ContentMarginBottom = 1.5f });
        theme.SetStylebox("grabber_area", "HSlider", new StyleBoxFlat { BgColor = C(Palette.Ink), ContentMarginTop = 1.5f, ContentMarginBottom = 1.5f });
        theme.SetStylebox("grabber_area_highlight", "HSlider", new StyleBoxFlat { BgColor = C(Palette.Ink), ContentMarginTop = 1.5f, ContentMarginBottom = 1.5f });
        theme.SetIcon("grabber", "HSlider", Circle(14, Palette.Ink));
        theme.SetIcon("grabber_highlight", "HSlider", Circle(14, Palette.Body));
        return theme;
    }

    /// <summary>A checkbox square: ink-filled with a light inset when checked, a hairline outline when not.</summary>
    public static ImageTexture Box(int size, bool filled, Rgb? colour = null)
    {
        var ink = C(colour ?? Palette.Ink);
        var image = Image.CreateEmpty(size, size, false, Image.Format.Rgba8);
        for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
            for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
            {
                var edge = x < 2 || y < 2 || x >= size - 2 || y >= size - 2;
                var inset = x >= 4 && y >= 4 && x < size - 4 && y < size - 4;
                image.SetPixel(x, y, edge ? ink : filled ? (inset ? C(Palette.Card) : ink) : new Color(0, 0, 0, 0));
            }
        return ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(image);
    }

    /// <summary>A filled disc (slider grabbers).</summary>
    public static ImageTexture Circle(int size, Rgb colour)
    {
        var image = Image.CreateEmpty(size, size, false, Image.Format.Rgba8);
        var r = size / 2f;
        for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
            for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
            {
                var d = new Godot.Vector2(x + 0.5f - r, y + 0.5f - r).Length();
                image.SetPixel(x, y, C(colour, Math.Clamp(r - d, 0, 1)));
            }
        return ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(image);
    }

    public static StyleBoxFlat Pill(bool filled) => new()
    {
        BgColor = filled ? C(Palette.Ink) : new Color(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BorderColor = C(Palette.Ink),
        BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
        CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 999, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 999, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 999, CornerRadiusTopRight = 999,
        ContentMarginLeft = 14, ContentMarginRight = 14, ContentMarginTop = 7, ContentMarginBottom = 7,
    };

    /// <summary>The floating card: warm white, hairline border, soft shadow, 16 px corners.</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Card(string name, float width)
    {
        var card = new PanelContainer { Name = name, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(width, 0) };
        card.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = C(Palette.Card), BorderColor = C(Palette.Border),
            BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
            CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 16, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 16, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 16, CornerRadiusTopRight = 16,
            ShadowColor = new Color(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.09f, 0.06f), ShadowSize = 20, ShadowOffset = new Godot.Vector2(0, 12),
            ContentMarginLeft = 22, ContentMarginRight = 22, ContentMarginTop = 22, ContentMarginBottom = 22,
        });
        return card;
    }

    /// <summary>The small uppercase label above sections ("OVERNIGHT", "TIES").</summary>
    public static Label Kicker(string text, string name = "Kicker")
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text.ToUpperInvariant() };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", 11);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(Palette.Muted));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>Italic serif display text ("Year 312.", names on pages).</summary>
    public static Label Title(string text, int size, string name = "Title", bool italic = true)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontOverride("font", italic ? Display : DisplayUpright);
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>A one-line display title that shrinks its type, never wraps, to fit a width (long world names).</summary>
    public static Label FittedTitle(string text, int size, float maxWidth, string name = "Title")
    {
        var label = Title(text, size, name);
        label.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        var width = Display.GetStringSize(text, HorizontalAlignment.Left, -1, size).X;
        if (width > maxWidth) label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", Math.Max(28, (int)(size * maxWidth / width)));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>A label that never wraps (short labels in rows).</summary>
    public static Label Line(string text, string name, Rgb? colour = null, int size = 13)
    {
        var label = Body(text, name, colour, size);
        label.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>A pill chip: outlined (traits, systems) or filled black (the ambition).</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Chip(string text, bool filled = false, string name = "Chip", Rgb? dot = null)
    {
        var chip = Capsule(name, filled ? C(Palette.Ink) : new Color(0, 0, 0, 0), filled ? null : C(Palette.Ink), 12, 5);
        var row = Row("Row", 6);
        if (dot is { } d) row.AddChild(Dot(d, 8));
        row.AddChild(Line(text, "Text", filled ? Palette.Card : Palette.Ink, 12));
        chip.AddChild(row);
        return chip;
    }

    /// <summary>A whole name as one link.</summary>
    public static RichTextLabel NameLink(Mention mention, string name, Action<EntityRef> open, int size = 13) =>
        Linked(new LinkedText(mention.Name, new[] { new TextLink(0, mention.Name.Length, mention.Ref) }), name, open, size, Palette.Ink);

    /// <summary>"← Back", a quiet text button.</summary>
    public static Button BackLink(Action back)
    {
        var button = new Button { Name = "Back", Text = "← Back", Flat = true, Alignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MouseDefaultCursorShape = Control.CursorShape.PointingHand };
        foreach (var state in new[] { "normal", "hover", "pressed", "focus" }) button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride(state, new StyleBoxEmpty());
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(Palette.Muted));
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", C(Palette.Ink));
        button.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", 12);
        button.SizeFlagsHorizontal = Control.SizeFlags.ShrinkBegin;
        button.Pressed += back;
        return button;
    }

    /// <summary>The page ground: screens that are documents rather than the diorama cover it.</summary>
    public static ColorRect Page()
    {
        var page = new ColorRect { Name = "Page", Color = C(Palette.Ground), MouseFilter = Control.MouseFilterEnum.Stop };
        page.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(Control.LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        return page;
    }

    public static Label Body(string text, string name = "Body", Rgb? colour = null, int size = 13)
    {
        var label = new Label { Name = name, Text = text, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", size);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", C(colour ?? Palette.Body));
        return label;
    }

    public static Button Button(string text, string name, bool primary = false)
    {
        var button = new Button { Name = name, Text = text, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(0, 34) };
        if (primary)
        {
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("normal", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("hover", Pill(filled: true));
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", Colors.White);
            button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", Colors.White);
        }
        return button;
    }

    public static VBoxContainer Column(string name, int gap)
    {
        var column = new VBoxContainer { Name = name };
        column.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return column;
    }

    public static HBoxContainer Row(string name, int gap)
    {
        var row = new HBoxContainer { Name = name };
        row.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", gap);
        return row;
    }

    /// <summary>Text whose names are links: clicking one calls <paramref name="open"/> with its entity.</summary>
    public static RichTextLabel Linked(LinkedText text, string name, Action<EntityRef> open, int size = 13, Rgb? colour = null, bool serif = false)
    {
        var label = new RichTextLabel
        {
            Name = name, BbcodeEnabled = true, FitContent = true, ScrollActive = false, AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.WordSmart,
            Text = Markup.Bbcode(text), MetaUnderlined = true, SizeFlagsHorizontal = Control.SizeFlags.ExpandFill,
        };
        label.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("normal_font_size", size);
        label.AddThemeColorOverride("default_color", C(colour ?? Palette.Body));
        if (serif) label.AddThemeFontOverride("normal_font", DisplayUpright);
        label.MetaClicked += meta => open(Importance.ParseRef(meta.AsString()));
        return label;
    }

    /// <summary>A small filled circle (category dots, the status light).</summary>
    public static Panel Dot(Rgb colour, float size = 8, string name = "Dot")
    {
        var dot = new Panel { Name = name, CustomMinimumSize = new Godot.Vector2(size, size), SizeFlagsVertical = Control.SizeFlags.ShrinkCenter };
        dot.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", Round(C(colour), null));
        return dot;
    }

    /// <summary>A pill-shaped panel (labels on pins, the status pill).</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Capsule(string name, Color fill, Color? border = null, int padX = 10, int padY = 3)
    {
        var pill = new PanelContainer { Name = name };
        var style = Round(fill, border);
        (style.ContentMarginLeft, style.ContentMarginRight, style.ContentMarginTop, style.ContentMarginBottom) = (padX, padX, padY, padY);
        pill.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", style);
        return pill;
    }

    /// <summary>A row with a hairline rule above it, like the digest's list.</summary>
    public static PanelContainer Ruled(string name, Control content, bool ruleBelow = false)
    {
        var row = new PanelContainer { Name = name };
        row.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = new Color(0, 0, 0, 0), BorderColor = C(Palette.Rule), BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthBottom = ruleBelow ? 1 : 0,
            ContentMarginTop = 10, ContentMarginBottom = 10,
        });
        row.AddChild(content);
        return row;
    }

    public static StyleBoxFlat Round(Color fill, Color? border)
    {
        var style = new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = fill, CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 999, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 999, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 999, CornerRadiusTopRight = 999,
        };
        if (border is { } b) (style.BorderColor, style.BorderWidthBottom, style.BorderWidthTop, style.BorderWidthLeft, style.BorderWidthRight) = (b, 1, 1, 1, 1);
        return style;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Pins a control to a corner of its parent: anchors (0 or 1 across and down) plus an offset to its near corner;
    /// a control anchored right or bottom grows left or up as its content grows, so the margin holds.
    /// </summary>
    public static T Place<T>(T control, float anchorX, float anchorY, float x, float y) where T : Control
    {
        (control.AnchorLeft, control.AnchorRight, control.AnchorTop, control.AnchorBottom) = (anchorX, anchorX, anchorY, anchorY);
        (control.OffsetLeft, control.OffsetRight, control.OffsetTop, control.OffsetBottom) = (x, x, y, y);
        control.GrowHorizontal = anchorX > 0 ? Control.GrowDirection.Begin : Control.GrowDirection.End;
        control.GrowVertical = anchorY > 0 ? Control.GrowDirection.Begin : Control.GrowDirection.End;
        return control;
    }

    public static void Clear(Node node)
    {
        foreach (var child in node.GetChildren())
        {
            node.RemoveChild(child);
            child.QueueFree();
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The *Character page* (sub-project 4 §4): name, role line, trait and ambition chips and a painted inset map of
/// where they are on the left; their life and deeds down the middle with the latest great deed in a dark card; ties,
/// Follow and Family tree on the right. Every name is a link.
/// </summary>
public partial class CharacterView : Control
{
    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;
    public CharacterScreen Character { get; init; } = null!;

    public VBoxContainer Timeline { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Button FollowButton { get; private set; } = null!;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "CharacterScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(Ui.Page());

        var left = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Left", 24), 0, 0, 56, 40);
        left.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(380, 0);
        left.AddChild(Ui.BackLink(() => Host.Router.Back()));
        var who = Ui.Column("Who", 10);
        who.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(Character.Kicker));
        who.AddChild(Ui.FittedTitle($"{Character.Name}.", 76, 380));
        who.AddChild(Ui.Body(Character.Line, "Line", Palette.Body));
        left.AddChild(who);
        var chips = new HFlowContainer { Name = "Chips" };
        chips.AddThemeConstantOverride("h_separation", 6);
        chips.AddThemeConstantOverride("v_separation", 6);
        foreach (var trait in Character.Traits) chips.AddChild(Ui.Chip(trait, name: "Trait"));
        if (Character.Ambition.Length > 0)
            chips.AddChild(Ui.Chip(Character.Ambition.StartsWith("Ambition", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? Character.Ambition : $"Ambition: {Character.Ambition}",
                filled: true, name: "Ambition"));
        left.AddChild(chips);
        AddChild(left);
        AddChild(Inset());

        var middle = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Middle", 18), 0, 0, 476, 92);
        middle.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(580, 0);
        middle.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("Life & deeds"));
        var scroll = new ScrollContainer { Name = "Scroll", CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(580, 760), HorizontalScrollMode = ScrollContainer.ScrollMode.Disabled };
        Timeline = Ui.Column("Timeline", 0);
        Timeline.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        if (Character.Earlier > 0)
            Timeline.AddChild(Ui.Body($"{Character.Earlier} earlier deeds are in the full chronicle.", "Earlier", Palette.Muted, 12));
        foreach (var row in Character.Timeline) Timeline.AddChild(row.Highlight ? Highlight(row) : Row(row));
        if (Character.Folded > 0)
            Timeline.AddChild(Ui.Ruled("Folded", Ui.Body($"… and {Character.Folded} smaller moments.", "Text", Palette.Muted, 12), ruleBelow: true));
        scroll.AddChild(Timeline);
        middle.AddChild(scroll);
        AddChild(middle);
        // Lives read upward from the latest deed, as in the design: open at the bottom.
        Callable.From(() => scroll.ScrollVertical = (int)scroll.GetVScrollBar().MaxValue).CallDeferred();

        AddChild(TiesCard());
    }

    private Control Row(TimelineRow row)
    {
        var line = Ui.Row("Row", 0);
        var year = Ui.Line($"Year {row.Year}", "Year", Palette.Muted, 12);
        year.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(76, 0);
        line.AddChild(year);
        line.AddChild(Ui.Linked(row.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 14, Palette.Ink));
        var ruled = Ui.Ruled($"Event{row.EventId}", line);
        ((StyleBoxFlat)ruled.GetThemeStylebox("panel")).ContentMarginTop = 14;
        ((StyleBoxFlat)ruled.GetThemeStylebox("panel")).ContentMarginBottom = 14;
        return ruled;
    }

    /// <summary>The year's most important deed, in the design's dark card with a larger serif line.</summary>
    private Control Highlight(TimelineRow row)
    {
        var card = Ui.Card($"Event{row.EventId}", 0);
        var style = (StyleBoxFlat)card.GetThemeStylebox("panel").Duplicate();
        (style.BgColor, style.BorderColor) = (Ui.C(Palette.Ink), Ui.C(Palette.Ink));
        (style.CornerRadiusBottomLeft, style.CornerRadiusBottomRight, style.CornerRadiusTopLeft, style.CornerRadiusTopRight) = (12, 12, 12, 12);
        (style.ContentMarginTop, style.ContentMarginBottom, style.ContentMarginLeft, style.ContentMarginRight) = (18, 18, 20, 20);
        card.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", style);
        var line = Ui.Row("Row", 0);
        var year = Ui.Line($"Year {row.Year}", "Year", new System.Numerics.Vector3(0.73f, 0.72f, 0.69f), 12);
        year.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(76, 0);
        line.AddChild(year);
        line.AddChild(Ui.Linked(row.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 22, Palette.Card, serif: true));
        card.AddChild(line);
        var spacer = new MarginContainer { Name = "Highlight" };
        spacer.AddThemeConstantOverride("margin_top", 4);
        spacer.AddChild(card);
        return spacer;
    }

    private PanelContainer TiesCard()
    {
        var card = Ui.Place(Ui.Card("Ties", 320), 1, 0, -40, 40);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 16);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("Ties"));
        var list = Ui.Column("List", 0);
        for (var i = 0; i < Character.Ties.Count; i++)
        {
            var tie = Character.Ties[i];
            var entry = Ui.Column("Entry", 2);
            var label = tie.Qualifier.Length > 0 ? $"{tie.Relation} · {tie.Qualifier}" : tie.Relation;
            var kicker = Ui.Body(label, "Relation", tie.Tone == Sim.Views.Tone.Hostile ? Palette.Conflict : Palette.Muted, 11);
            entry.AddChild(kicker);
            entry.AddChild(Ui.NameLink(new Mention(tie.Ref, tie.Name), "Name", Host.Router.Open));
            list.AddChild(Ui.Ruled($"Tie{i}", entry, ruleBelow: i == Character.Ties.Count - 1));
        }
        if (Character.Ties.Count == 0) list.AddChild(Ui.Body("No close ties are recorded.", "None", Palette.Muted, 12));
        column.AddChild(list);
        var actions = Ui.Row("Actions", 6);
        FollowButton = Ui.Button(Character.Followed ? "Following" : "Follow", "Follow", primary: !Character.Followed);
        FollowButton.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        FollowButton.Pressed += ToggleFollow;
        var family = Ui.Button("Family tree", "Family");
        family.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        family.Pressed += () => Host.Router.Go(new Screen(ScreenKind.Family, Character.Ref.Id));
        actions.AddChild(FollowButton);
        actions.AddChild(family);
        column.AddChild(actions);
        card.AddChild(column);
        return card;
    }

    /// <summary>Follows or unfollows: followed people lead the digest and are never compacted from memory.</summary>
    public void ToggleFollow()
    {
        var follow = !Host.Session!.IsFollowed(Character.Ref);
        Host.Session.Follow(Character.Ref, follow);
        FollowButton.Text = follow ? "Following" : "Follow";
        foreach (var (state, filled) in new[] { ("normal", !follow), ("hover", !follow) }) FollowButton.AddThemeStyleboxOverride(state, Ui.Pill(filled));
        FollowButton.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", follow ? Ui.C(Palette.Ink) : Colors.White);
    }

    /// <summary>A painted map of the world with a pin where they are now (or were last seen).</summary>
    private Control Inset()
    {
        var frame = Ui.Place(new Control { Name = "Inset", CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(320, 250), Size = new Vector2(320, 250) }, 0, 1, 56, -290);
        if (Host.Geography is not { } geography) return frame;
        const int size = 192;
        var image = Image.CreateFromData(size, size, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, MiniMap.Pixels(geography.Terrain, size));
        // The world square, framed like the design's cards.
        var border = new Panel { Name = "Frame", Size = new Vector2(252, 252), Position = new Vector2(-1, -1), MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        border.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = Ui.C(Palette.Card), BorderColor = Ui.C(Palette.Border), BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
            CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 12, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 12, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 12, CornerRadiusTopRight = 12,
            ShadowColor = new Color(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.09f, 0.06f), ShadowSize = 16, ShadowOffset = new Vector2(0, 8),
        });
        frame.AddChild(border);
        var map = new TextureRect
        {
            Name = "Map", Texture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(image), ExpandMode = TextureRect.ExpandModeEnum.IgnoreSize,
            StretchMode = TextureRect.StretchModeEnum.Scale, Size = new Vector2(250, 250), MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore,
        };
        frame.AddChild(map);
        if (Character.Location is { Position: { } position } location)
        {
            var at = new Vector2((float)position.X * 250, (float)position.Y * 250);
            var pin = Ui.Row("Pin", 6);
            var dot = Ui.Capsule("Dot", Ui.C(Palette.Realm), Ui.C(Palette.Card), 0, 0);
            dot.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(18, 18);
            dot.SizeFlagsVertical = SizeFlags.ShrinkCenter;
            pin.AddChild(dot);
            var label = Ui.Capsule("Label", Ui.C(Palette.Card), Ui.C(Palette.Border));
            label.AddChild(Ui.Line(location.Label, "Text", Palette.Ink, 12));
            pin.AddChild(label);
            pin.Position = at - new Vector2(9, 12);
            frame.AddChild(pin);
            var open = Ui.Button("Show on map", "ShowOnMap");
            open.Position = new Vector2(0, 258);
            open.Pressed += () => Host.Router.Open(location.Ref);
            frame.AddChild(open);
        }
        return frame;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The *Morning digest* (sub-project 4 §4): the year and time away on the left, the control card on the right with
/// the Digest / Full chronicle toggle, the headline and its "Why did this happen?", the other items, and numbered
/// pins over their places on the diorama. Clicking an item flies to its place; clicking a pin opens its story.
/// </summary>
public partial class DigestView : Control
{
    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;
    public DigestScreen Digest { get; init; } = null!;

    public bool ShowingChronicle { get; private set; }
    public VBoxContainer Content { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Control PinLayer { get; private set; } = null!;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "DigestScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;

        PinLayer = new Control { Name = "Pins", MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        PinLayer.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(PinLayer);
        foreach (var pin in Host.Diorama.Pins) PinLayer.AddChild(PinButton(pin));

        var intro = Ui.Column("Intro", 10);
        Ui.Place(intro, 0, 0, 56, 48);
        intro.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(340, 0);
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Kicker($"{Digest.WorldName} · Morning digest"));
        intro.AddChild(Ui.FittedTitle(Digest.Title, 72, 440));
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Body(Digest.Subtitle, "Subtitle", Palette.Muted));
        AddChild(intro);

        var card = Ui.Card("Card", 360);
        Ui.Place(card, 1, 0, -40, 40);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 20);
        card.AddChild(column);
        var top = Ui.Row("Top", 0);
        var overnight = Ui.Kicker("Overnight");
        overnight.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        top.AddChild(overnight);
        top.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(Markup.Elapsed(Digest.ElapsedDays), "Elapsed"));
        column.AddChild(top);
        var toggle = Ui.Row("Toggle", 6);
        var digestButton = Ui.Button("Digest", "DigestButton", primary: true);
        var chronicleButton = Ui.Button("Full chronicle", "ChronicleButton");
        digestButton.Pressed += () => ShowChronicle(false);
        chronicleButton.Pressed += () => ShowChronicle(true);
        toggle.AddChild(digestButton);
        toggle.AddChild(chronicleButton);
        column.AddChild(toggle);
        Content = Ui.Column("Content", 20);
        column.AddChild(Content);
        AddChild(card);

        var hint = Ui.Column("Hint", 2);
        hint.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(300, 0);
        Ui.Place(hint, 0, 1, 56, -44);
        hint.AddChild(Ui.Title("Drag to orbit. Scroll to descend.", 15));
        hint.AddChild(Ui.Body("Click a numbered pin to read its story.", "HintBody", Palette.Muted, 12));
        AddChild(hint);

        ShowChronicle(false);
    }

    /// <summary>Switches the card between the digest and the full chronicle of the last two years.</summary>
    public void ShowChronicle(bool chronicle)
    {
        ShowingChronicle = chronicle;
        Ui.Clear(Content);
        var toggle = GetNode<HBoxContainer>("Card/Column/Toggle");
        Restyle(toggle.GetNode<Button>("DigestButton"), !chronicle);
        Restyle(toggle.GetNode<Button>("ChronicleButton"), chronicle);
        if (chronicle) FillChronicle();
        else FillDigest();
    }

    private void FillDigest()
    {
        if (Digest.Headline is { } headline)
        {
            var block = Ui.Column("Headline", 8);
            var kicker = Ui.Row("KickerRow", 8);
            kicker.AddChild(Ui.Dot(Palette.For(headline.Category)));
            kicker.AddChild(Ui.Kicker($"{headline.Number} · {headline.Kicker}"));
            block.AddChild(kicker);
            block.AddChild(Ui.Linked(headline.Title, "HeadlineTitle", Host.Router.Open, 30, Palette.Ink, serif: true));
            block.AddChild(Ui.Linked(headline.Body, "HeadlineBody", Host.Router.Open));
            if (headline.CanExplain)
            {
                var why = Ui.Button("Why did this happen?", "Why", primary: true);
                why.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ShrinkBegin;
                why.Pressed += () => Host.Router.Explain(headline.EventId);
                block.AddChild(why);
            }
            Content.AddChild(block);
        }
        else Content.AddChild(Ui.Body("Nothing of note happened while you were away.", "Empty", Palette.Muted));

        var items = Ui.Column("Items", 0);
        for (var i = 0; i < Digest.Items.Count; i++)
        {
            var item = Digest.Items[i];
            var row = Ui.Row("Row", 12);
            var number = Ui.Body(item.Number.ToString(), "Number", Palette.Muted);
            number.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(14, 0);
            row.AddChild(number);
            row.AddChild(Ui.Linked(item.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 13, Palette.Ink));
            var ruled = Ui.Ruled($"Item{item.Number}", row, ruleBelow: i == Digest.Items.Count - 1);
            var eventId = item.EventId;
            ruled.GuiInput += input =>
            {
                if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true }) FlyTo(eventId);
            };
            items.AddChild(ruled);
        }
        Content.AddChild(items);
    }

    private void FillChronicle()
    {
        // This year and last, newest first: early in a year this year alone is nearly empty.
        var from = Math.Max(1, Digest.Year - 1);
        var first = Host.Session!.Chronicle(from, Digest.Year);
        var page = first.Pages > 1 ? Host.Session.Chronicle(from, Digest.Year, page: first.Pages) : first;
        var scroll = new ScrollContainer { Name = "Chronicle", CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(0, 520), HorizontalScrollMode = ScrollContainer.ScrollMode.Disabled };
        var list = Ui.Column("List", 12);
        list.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        foreach (var season in page.Seasons.Reverse())
        {
            list.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(season.Label, "Season"));
            foreach (var row in season.Rows.Reverse())
            {
                var line = Ui.Row("Row", 8);
                line.AddChild(Ui.Dot(Palette.For(row.Category), 6));
                line.AddChild(Ui.Linked(row.Text, "Text", Host.Router.Open, 12));
                list.AddChild(line);
            }
        }
        if (page.Total == 0) list.AddChild(Ui.Body("Nothing is recorded for these years yet.", "Empty", Palette.Muted));
        scroll.AddChild(list);
        Content.AddChild(scroll);
    }

    /// <summary>Eases the camera to the place of a digest entry, when it has one.</summary>
    public void FlyTo(long eventId)
    {
        if (Digest.Pins.FirstOrDefault(p => p.EventId == eventId) is { } pin) Host.Diorama.Focus(pin.Position, Zoom.Region);
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        var tags = PinLayer.GetChildren().OfType<PinTag>().ToList();
        var boxes = new List<LabelBox>(tags.Count);
        foreach (var tag in tags)
        {
            var pin = Host.Diorama.Pins.First(p => p.Number == tag.Number);
            tag.Position = Host.Diorama.ScreenOf(pin.Position) - new Vector2(11, 11);
            boxes.Add(Host.Diorama.Camera.IsPositionBehind(Diorama.ToGodot(pin.Position))
                ? new LabelBox(-1e5f, -1e5f, 0, 0, 0)
                : new LabelBox(tag.Position.X, tag.Position.Y, tag.Size.X, tag.Size.Y, 100 - tag.Number));
        }
        var visible = Declutter.Visible(boxes, GetViewportRect().Size.X, GetViewportRect().Size.Y, MapHudView.Reserved(this, "Card", "Intro"));
        for (var i = 0; i < tags.Count; i++) tags[i].Visible = visible[i];
    }

    private PinTag PinButton(Pin pin)
    {
        var tag = new PinTag { Name = $"Pin{pin.Number}", Number = pin.Number, EventId = pin.EventId, MouseDefaultCursorShape = CursorShape.PointingHand };
        tag.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", 6);
        var number = Ui.Capsule("Number", Ui.C(Palette.Ink), padX: 7, padY: 2);
        number.AddChild(Ui.Body(pin.Number.ToString(), "Digit", Palette.Card, 11));
        var label = Ui.Capsule("Label", Ui.C(Palette.Card), Ui.C(Palette.Border));
        label.AddChild(Ui.Body(pin.Label, "Text", Palette.Ink, 12));
        tag.AddChild(number);
        tag.AddChild(label);
        foreach (var text in tag.FindChildren("*", "Label", owned: false)) ((Label)text).AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        foreach (var capsule in new Control[] { number, label }) capsule.MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        tag.GuiInput += input =>
        {
            if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true }) Host.Router.Explain(tag.EventId);
        };
        return tag;
    }

    private static void Restyle(Button button, bool active)
    {
        button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("normal", Ui.Pill(filled: active));
        button.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("hover", Ui.Pill(filled: active));
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", active ? Colors.White : Ui.C(Palette.Ink));
        button.AddThemeColorOverride("font_hover_color", active ? Colors.White : Ui.C(Palette.Body));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>A character's family tree: generations as rows of linked names, oldest at the top, the person ringed.</summary>
public partial class FamilyView : Control
{
    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;
    public FamilyTree Tree { get; init; } = null!;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "FamilyScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(Ui.Page());
        var column = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Column", 16), 0, 0, 56, 40);
        column.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(1100, 0);
        column.AddChild(Ui.BackLink(() => Host.Router.Back()));
        var root = Tree.Nodes.First(n => n.Ref == Tree.Root);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("Family tree"));
        column.AddChild(Ui.FittedTitle($"The family of {root.Name}.", 56, 1000));
        var generation = Generations();
        foreach (var group in Tree.Nodes.GroupBy(n => generation[n.Ref]).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
        {
            column.AddChild(Ui.Kicker(group.Key switch
            {
                <= -2 => "Grandparents", -1 => "Parents", 0 => "Their generation", 1 => "Children", _ => "Grandchildren",
            }, $"Label{group.Key}"));
            var row = new HFlowContainer { Name = $"Generation{group.Key}" };
            row.AddThemeConstantOverride("h_separation", 10);
            row.AddThemeConstantOverride("v_separation", 10);
            foreach (var node in group.OrderBy(n => n.Born))
            {
                string Year(int y) => y < 1 ? "before Year 1" : $"Year {y}";
                var years = node.Died is { } died ? $"{Year(node.Born)} – {Year(died)}" : $"born {Year(node.Born)}";
                var chip = Ui.Capsule($"Person{node.Ref.Id}", Ui.C(node.Ref == Tree.Root ? Palette.Ink : Palette.Card), Ui.C(Palette.Border), 14, 8);
                var inner = Ui.Column("Inner", 2);
                inner.AddChild(Ui.NameLink(new Mention(node.Ref, node.Name), "Name", Host.Router.Open));
                inner.AddChild(Ui.Line(years, "Years", Palette.Muted, 11));
                if (node.Ref == Tree.Root)
                {
                    inner.GetNode<RichTextLabel>("Name").AddThemeColorOverride("default_color", Ui.C(Palette.Card));
                    inner.GetNode<Label>("Years").AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", new Color(0.73f, 0.72f, 0.69f));
                }
                chip.AddChild(inner);
                chip.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(180, 0);
                row.AddChild(chip);
            }
            column.AddChild(row);
        }
        AddChild(column);
    }

    /// <summary>Generation of each person relative to the root (parents −1, children +1), from the parent edges.</summary>
    private Dictionary<Sim.Events.EntityRef, int> Generations()
    {
        var generation = new Dictionary<Sim.Events.EntityRef, int> { [Tree.Root] = 0 };
        var queue = new Queue<Sim.Events.EntityRef>(new[] { Tree.Root });
        while (queue.Count > 0)
        {
            var at = queue.Dequeue();
            foreach (var edge in Tree.Edges)
            {
                var (other, step) = edge.From == at ? (edge.To, edge.Kind == "parent" ? 1 : 0)
                    : edge.To == at ? (edge.From, edge.Kind == "parent" ? -1 : 0) : (default, 0);
                if (other == default || generation.ContainsKey(other)) continue;
                generation[other] = generation[at] + step;
                queue.Enqueue(other);
            }
        }
        foreach (var node in Tree.Nodes) generation.TryAdd(node.Ref, 0);
        return generation;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The *Diorama map HUD* (sub-project 4 §4): the world's name and summary, name tags over settlements (more as you
/// descend), a dark hover card per settlement, and the control card: zoom presets, layer toggles, the history slider
/// that rebuilds the map at any past year, reset and snapshot. A legend of the realms sits bottom left.
/// </summary>
public partial class MapHudView : Control
{
    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;

    /// <summary>A place to fly to when the map opens (a linked settlement, region or structure).</summary>
    public EntityRef? Focus { get; init; }

    public MapScreen Map { get; private set; } = null!;
    public int Year { get; private set; }
    public int Now { get; private set; }
    public Control TagLayer { get; private set; } = null!;
    public PanelContainer HoverCard { get; private set; } = null!;
    public HSlider History { get; private set; } = null!;

    private Label _kicker = null!;
    private Label _summary = null!;
    private Label _yearLabel = null!;
    private VBoxContainer _legend = null!;
    private Zoom _zoom;

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "MapScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        Map = Host.Session!.Map();
        Now = Map.Year;
        Year = Host.Diorama.ShownYear ?? Now;
        if (Year != Now) Map = Host.Session.Map(Year);
        _zoom = Host.Diorama.Rig.NearestZoom;

        TagLayer = new Control { Name = "Tags", MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        TagLayer.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(TagLayer);

        var intro = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Intro", 10), 0, 0, 56, 48);
        intro.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(300, 0);
        _kicker = Ui.Kicker("", "Kicker");
        intro.AddChild(_kicker);
        intro.AddChild(Ui.FittedTitle($"{Map.WorldName}.", 64, 440));
        _summary = Ui.Body("", "Summary", Palette.Muted);
        intro.AddChild(_summary);
        AddChild(intro);

        AddChild(ControlCard());

        _legend = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Legend", 8), 0, 1, 56, -44);
        AddChild(_legend);

        HoverCard = Ui.Card("HoverCard", 250);
        var dark = (StyleBoxFlat)HoverCard.GetThemeStylebox("panel").Duplicate();
        (dark.BgColor, dark.BorderColor, dark.CornerRadiusBottomLeft, dark.CornerRadiusBottomRight, dark.CornerRadiusTopLeft, dark.CornerRadiusTopRight) =
            (Ui.C(Palette.Ink), Ui.C(Palette.Ink), 12, 12, 12, 12);
        (dark.ContentMarginLeft, dark.ContentMarginRight, dark.ContentMarginTop, dark.ContentMarginBottom) = (16, 16, 14, 14);
        HoverCard.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", dark);
        HoverCard.MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        HoverCard.Visible = false;
        AddChild(HoverCard);

        Refresh();
        if (Focus is { } focus && MapLabels.Find(Map, focus) is { } point) Host.Diorama.Focus(point, MapLabels.ZoomFor(focus));
    }

    /// <summary>Shows the map as it was at the end of a year (today's map for the current year).</summary>
    public void ShowYear(int year)
    {
        Year = Math.Clamp(year, 1, Now);
        Map = Year == Now ? Host.Session!.Map() : Host.Session!.Map(Year);
        Host.Diorama.Show(Map, Year == Now ? null : Year);
        if (Math.Abs(History.Value - Year) > 0.5) History.SetValueNoSignal(Year);
        Refresh();
    }

    public void SetLayer(MapLayers layer, bool on)
    {
        Host.Diorama.SetLayers(on ? Host.Diorama.Layers | layer : Host.Diorama.Layers & ~layer);
        RebuildTags();
    }

    private void Refresh()
    {
        _kicker.Text = $"{Map.WorldName} · {_zoom} view".ToUpperInvariant();
        _summary.Text = Year != Now ? $"Year {Map.Year}. {Map.Summary}" : Map.Summary;
        _yearLabel.Text = Year == Now ? $"Year {Now}" : $"Year {Year} of {Now}";
        Ui.Clear(_legend);
        foreach (var entry in Map.Legend)
        {
            var row = Ui.Row("Entry", 8);
            row.AddChild(Ui.Dot(entry.Colour switch { "conflict" => Palette.Conflict, "event" => Palette.Ink, var name => Palette.Heraldic(name) }, 10));
            var label = Ui.Body(entry.Label, "Label", Palette.Body, 12);
            label.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
            row.AddChild(label);
            _legend.AddChild(row);
        }
        RebuildTags();
    }

    /// <summary>The name tags for the settlements this zoom and these layers show.</summary>
    public void RebuildTags()
    {
        Ui.Clear(TagLayer);
        HoverCard.Visible = false;
        foreach (var settlement in Map.Settlements.Where(s => MapLabels.Shows(s, _zoom, Host.Diorama.Layers)))
            TagLayer.AddChild(Tag(settlement));
    }

    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (Host.Diorama.Rig.NearestZoom != _zoom)
        {
            _zoom = Host.Diorama.Rig.NearestZoom;
            Refresh();
        }
        var grid = Host.Geography?.Terrain;
        if (grid is null) return;
        var tags = TagLayer.GetChildren().OfType<SettlementTag>().ToList();
        var boxes = new List<LabelBox>(tags.Count);
        foreach (var tag in tags)
        {
            var at = Client.Projection.ToBlock(grid, tag.Settlement.Position!, 0.02f);
            tag.Position = Host.Diorama.ScreenOf(at) - new Vector2(11, 24);
            var behind = Host.Diorama.Camera.IsPositionBehind(Diorama.ToGodot(at));
            boxes.Add(behind ? new LabelBox(-1e5f, -1e5f, 0, 0, 0) : new LabelBox(tag.Position.X, tag.Position.Y, tag.Size.X, tag.Size.Y, Priority(tag.Settlement)));
        }
        var visible = Declutter.Visible(boxes, GetViewportRect().Size.X, GetViewportRect().Size.Y, Reserved(this, "Card", "Intro", "Legend"));
        for (var i = 0; i < tags.Count; i++) tags[i].Visible = visible[i];
    }

    /// <summary>News first, then cities, towns, villages: the order in which tags claim screen space.</summary>
    public static int Priority(MapSettlement settlement) => settlement.Marker switch
    {
        PinMarker.Conflict => 10,
        PinMarker.RecentEvent => 9,
        _ => Enum.TryParse<FantasyWorld.Sim.Model.SettlementTier>(settlement.Tier, out var tier) ? (int)tier : 0,
    };

    /// <summary>Screen boxes of a screen's own panels, which labels must not cover.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<LabelBox> Reserved(Control screen, params string[] panels) =>
        panels.Select(p => screen.GetNodeOrNull<Control>(p)).Where(c => c is { Visible: true })
            .Select(c => c!.GetGlobalRect()).Select(r => new LabelBox(r.Position.X, r.Position.Y, r.Size.X, r.Size.Y, int.MaxValue));

    private SettlementTag Tag(MapSettlement settlement)
    {
        var tag = new SettlementTag { Name = $"Tag{settlement.Ref.Id}", Settlement = settlement, MouseDefaultCursorShape = CursorShape.PointingHand };
        tag.AddThemeConstantOverride("separation", 6);
        var marker = Host.Diorama.Markers.FirstOrDefault(m => m.Ref == settlement.Ref);
        var colour = settlement.Marker switch
        {
            PinMarker.Conflict => Palette.Conflict,
            PinMarker.RecentEvent => Palette.Ink,
            _ => marker?.Colour ?? Palette.Ink,
        };
        var dot = Ui.Capsule("Dot", Ui.C(colour), Ui.C(Palette.Card), 0, 0);
        dot.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(18, 18);
        dot.SizeFlagsVertical = SizeFlags.ShrinkCenter;
        var pill = Ui.Capsule("Name", Ui.C(Palette.Card), Ui.C(settlement.Marker == PinMarker.Conflict ? Palette.Ink : Palette.Border));
        var name = Ui.Body(settlement.Name, "Text", Palette.Ink, 12);
        name.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        pill.AddChild(name);
        tag.AddChild(dot);
        tag.AddChild(pill);
        foreach (var part in new Control[] { dot, pill }) part.MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore;
        tag.MouseEntered += () => ShowCard(tag);
        tag.MouseExited += () => HoverCard.Visible = false;
        tag.GuiInput += input =>
        {
            if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true }) Host.Diorama.Focus(settlement.Position!, Zoom.Town);
        };
        return tag;
    }

    /// <summary>The settlement's dark card above its tag: "Carrow Ford · Town", who holds it, people and events.</summary>
    public void ShowCard(SettlementTag tag)
    {
        Ui.Clear(HoverCard);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 6);
        var kicker = Ui.Kicker(tag.Settlement.Card.Kicker);
        kicker.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", new Color(0.73f, 0.72f, 0.69f));
        column.AddChild(kicker);
        var title = Ui.Title(tag.Settlement.Card.Title, 22, italic: false);
        title.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", Ui.C(Palette.Card));
        column.AddChild(title);
        if (tag.Settlement.Card.Line.Length > 0)
            column.AddChild(Ui.Body(tag.Settlement.Card.Line, "Line", new System.Numerics.Vector3(0.83f, 0.82f, 0.8f), 12));
        HoverCard.AddChild(column);
        HoverCard.Position = tag.Position + new Vector2(30, -110);
        HoverCard.Visible = true;
    }

    private PanelContainer ControlCard()
    {
        var card = Ui.Place(Ui.Card("Card", 280), 1, 0, -40, 40);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 18);
        card.AddChild(column);

        var zoom = Ui.Column("Zoom", 8);
        zoom.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("Zoom"));
        var presets = Ui.Row("Presets", 6);
        foreach (var preset in Enum.GetValues<Zoom>())
        {
            var button = Ui.Button(preset.ToString(), preset.ToString());
            button.Pressed += () => Host.Diorama.Rig.Preset(preset);
            presets.AddChild(button);
        }
        zoom.AddChild(presets);
        column.AddChild(zoom);

        var layers = Ui.Column("Layers", 4);
        layers.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("Layers"));
        foreach (var (layer, label) in new[]
                 {
                     (MapLayers.Borders, "Borders"), (MapLayers.Settlements, "Settlements"), (MapLayers.Events, "Events"),
                     (MapLayers.Structures, "Landmarks & labyrinths"), (MapLayers.TradeRoutes, "Trade routes"),
                 })
        {
            var box = new CheckBox { Name = layer.ToString(), Text = label, ButtonPressed = Host.Diorama.Layers.HasFlag(layer) };
            box.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", 12);
            // Trade routes are not modelled yet (sub-project 3); the layer is shown but cannot be turned on.
            box.Disabled = layer == MapLayers.TradeRoutes;
            box.TooltipText = box.Disabled ? "Trade routes arrive with a later update." : "";
            box.Toggled += on => SetLayer(layer, on);
            layers.AddChild(box);
        }
        column.AddChild(layers);

        var history = Ui.Column("HistoryBlock", 8);
        var header = Ui.Row("Header", 0);
        var historyKicker = Ui.Kicker("History");
        historyKicker.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        header.AddChild(historyKicker);
        _yearLabel = Ui.Kicker("", "Year");
        header.AddChild(_yearLabel);
        history.AddChild(header);
        History = new HSlider { Name = "History", MinValue = 1, MaxValue = Math.Max(2, Now), Step = 1, Value = Year, CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(0, 18) };
        History.ValueChanged += value => ShowYear((int)value);
        history.AddChild(History);
        var ends = Ui.Row("Ends", 0);
        var first = Ui.Line("Year 1", "First", Palette.Muted, 12);
        first.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        ends.AddChild(first);
        ends.AddChild(Ui.Line("Now", "Now", Palette.Muted, 12));
        history.AddChild(ends);
        column.AddChild(history);

        var actions = Ui.Row("Actions", 6);
        var reset = Ui.Button("Reset view", "Reset");
        reset.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        reset.Pressed += () =>
        {
            Host.Diorama.Rig.Reset();
            ShowYear(Now);
        };
        var snapshot = Ui.Button("Snapshot", "Snapshot");
        snapshot.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        snapshot.Pressed += () => Snapshot();
        actions.AddChild(reset);
        actions.AddChild(snapshot);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Ruled("ActionsRow", actions));
        return card;
    }

    /// <summary>Saves what the screen shows to user://snapshots; returns the file, or null without a real display.</summary>
    public string? Snapshot()
    {
        var image = GetViewport().GetTexture()?.GetImage();
        if (image is null || image.IsEmpty()) return null;
        var folder = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "snapshots");
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
        var file = System.IO.Path.Combine(folder, $"{Map.WorldName.ToLowerInvariant().Replace(' ', '-')}-year-{Year}-{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}.png");
        image.SavePng(file);
        Host.Status.Text = $"Snapshot saved · {System.IO.Path.GetFileName(file)}";
        return file;
    }
}
```

`game/scripts/screens/WhyCanvas.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>Draws the chain's connectors beneath the cards, or (as a second layer) the root cards' dashed outlines above them.</summary>
public partial class WhyCanvas : Control
{
    public WhyView View { get; init; } = null!;

    /// <summary>Draw the root cards' dashed outlines (above the cards) instead of the connectors (beneath them).</summary>
    public bool Outlines { get; init; }

    public override void _Draw()
    {
        var ink = Ui.C(Palette.Ink);
        if (!Outlines)
        {
            foreach (var connector in View.Graph.Connectors)
                for (var i = 0; i + 1 < connector.Points.Count; i++)
                    DrawLine(ToV(connector.Points[i]), ToV(connector.Points[i + 1]), ink, 1.2f, true);
            return;
        }
        foreach (var box in View.Graph.Cards.Where(b => b.Card.IsRoot && b.Layer > 0))
        {
            var rect = new Rect2(box.X, box.Y, box.Width, WhyLayout.CardHeight);
            var corners = new[] { rect.Position, rect.Position + new Vector2(rect.Size.X, 0), rect.End, rect.Position + new Vector2(0, rect.Size.Y), rect.Position };
            for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) DrawDashedLine(corners[i], corners[i + 1], ink, 1.5f, 6, true);
        }
    }

    private static Vector2 ToV(System.Numerics.Vector2 v) => new(v.X, v.Y);
}
```

`game/scripts/screens/WhyView.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game;

/// <summary>
/// The *Why chain* (sub-project 4 §4): the outcome in a dark card, its causes in layers below joined by "because"
/// connectors, root causes in dashed cards, and a side panel of the people, places and systems involved with Show on
/// map and Trace further (what this event went on to cause). Clicking any card traces that event in turn.
/// </summary>
public partial class WhyView : Control
{
    public const float GraphLeft = 380;
    public const float GraphWidth = 680;

    public Main Host { get; init; } = null!;
    public WhyScreen Why { get; init; } = null!;

    public WhyGraph Graph { get; private set; } = null!;
    public Control Canvas { get; private set; } = null!;
    public bool ShowingConsequences { get; private set; }

    public override void _Ready()
    {
        Name = "WhyScreen";
        SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        AddChild(Ui.Page());

        var intro = Ui.Place(Ui.Column("Intro", 10), 0, 0, 56, 40);
        intro.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(300, 0);
        intro.AddChild(Ui.BackLink(() => Host.Router.Back()));
        var kicker = Ui.Kicker("Why did this happen?");
        intro.AddChild(kicker);
        var length = Why.Outcome.Title.Text.Length;
        var title = Ui.Title(Why.Outcome.Title.Text, length > 100 ? 28 : length > 60 ? 32 : 44);
        title.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(300, 0);
        intro.AddChild(title);
        intro.AddChild(Ui.Body(Why.Summary, "Summary", Palette.Muted));
        AddChild(intro);

        var scroll = Ui.Place(new ScrollContainer { Name = "Scroll", HorizontalScrollMode = ScrollContainer.ScrollMode.Disabled }, 0, 0, GraphLeft, 70);
        scroll.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(GraphWidth + 16, 620);
        Canvas = new WhyCanvas { Name = "Graph", View = this };
        scroll.AddChild(Canvas);
        AddChild(scroll);
        ShowCauses();

        AddChild(Panel());
        var footer = Ui.Place(Ui.Row("Footer", 6), 0, 1, GraphLeft, -64);
        var lead = Ui.Title("Dashed cards are root causes.", 15);
        lead.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
        footer.AddChild(lead);
        footer.AddChild(Ui.Line("Click any card to trace it further back.", "Hint", Palette.Muted, 12));
        AddChild(footer);
    }

    /// <summary>The causes, laid out as the design's layered chain.</summary>
    public void ShowCauses()
    {
        ShowingConsequences = false;
        Graph = WhyLayout.Layout(Why, GraphWidth);
        Fill(Graph);
    }

    /// <summary>Trace further: the outcome on top and what it caused below, joined by "led to".</summary>
    public void ShowConsequences()
    {
        ShowingConsequences = true;
        var forward = new WhyScreen(Why.Outcome, Why.Consequences.Count > 0 ? new[] { Why.Consequences } : Array.Empty<IReadOnlyList<EventCard>>(),
            Why.Consequences.Select(c => new CauseEdge(c.EventId, Why.Outcome.EventId, Sim.Events.CauseKind.Trigger, "led to")).ToList(),
            Why.Summary, Why.People, Why.Places, Why.Systems, Array.Empty<EventCard>());
        Graph = WhyLayout.Layout(forward, GraphWidth);
        Fill(Graph);
    }

    private void Fill(WhyGraph graph)
    {
        Ui.Clear(Canvas);
        Canvas.CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(GraphWidth, graph.Height + 24);
        foreach (var box in graph.Cards) Canvas.AddChild(Card(box));
        // Root cards' dashed outlines are drawn above the cards.
        var outlines = new WhyCanvas { Name = "Outlines", View = this, Outlines = true, MouseFilter = MouseFilterEnum.Ignore };
        outlines.SetAnchorsAndOffsetsPreset(LayoutPreset.FullRect);
        Canvas.AddChild(outlines);
        foreach (var label in graph.Connectors.GroupBy(c => c.To).Select(g => g.First()))
        {
            var word = Ui.Title(label.Label, 15);
            word.AutowrapMode = TextServer.AutowrapMode.Off;
            word.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", Ui.C(Palette.Muted));
            word.Position = new Vector2(label.Points[0].X + 10, label.Points[0].Y + 12);
            Canvas.AddChild(word);
        }
        Canvas.QueueRedraw();
    }

    private PanelContainer Card(CardBox box)
    {
        var card = box.Card;
        var outcome = box.Layer == 0;
        var panel = new PanelContainer
        {
            Name = $"Card{card.EventId}", Position = new Vector2(box.X, box.Y), Size = new Vector2(box.Width, WhyLayout.CardHeight),
            CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(box.Width, WhyLayout.CardHeight), MouseDefaultCursorShape = CursorShape.PointingHand,
        };
        panel.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("panel", new StyleBoxFlat
        {
            BgColor = Ui.C(outcome ? Palette.Ink : Palette.Card),
            // Roots get no solid border: the canvas draws them a dashed one.
            BorderColor = Ui.C(card.IsRoot && !outcome ? Palette.Card : outcome ? Palette.Ink : Palette.Border),
            BorderWidthBottom = 1, BorderWidthTop = 1, BorderWidthLeft = 1, BorderWidthRight = 1,
            CornerRadiusBottomLeft = 12, CornerRadiusBottomRight = 12, CornerRadiusTopLeft = 12, CornerRadiusTopRight = 12,
            ContentMarginLeft = 18, ContentMarginRight = 18, ContentMarginTop = 14, ContentMarginBottom = 14,
        });
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 6);
        var head = Ui.Row("Head", 8);
        if (!outcome) head.AddChild(Ui.Dot(Palette.For(card.Category)));
        var kicker = outcome ? $"Year {card.Year} · Outcome" : card.IsRoot ? $"Year {card.Year} · Root cause" : $"Year {card.Year} · {Categories.Label(card.Category)}";
        var label = Ui.Kicker(kicker);
        if (outcome) label.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", new Color(0.73f, 0.72f, 0.69f));
        head.AddChild(label);
        column.AddChild(head);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Linked(card.Title, "Title", Host.Router.Open, outcome ? 20 : 15, outcome ? Palette.Card : Palette.Ink, serif: outcome));
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(card.Note) && !outcome)
        {
            var note = Ui.Body(card.Note!, "Note", Palette.Muted, 12);
            note.TextOverrunBehavior = TextServer.OverrunBehavior.TrimEllipsis;
            note.MaxLinesVisible = 1;
            column.AddChild(note);
        }
        panel.AddChild(column);
        panel.ClipContents = true;
        panel.GuiInput += input =>
        {
            if (input is InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex: MouseButton.Left, Pressed: true } && card.EventId != Why.Outcome.EventId)
                Host.Router.Explain(card.EventId);
        };
        return panel;
    }

    private PanelContainer Panel()
    {
        var card = Ui.Place(Ui.Card("Chain", 300), 1, 0, -40, 40);
        var column = Ui.Column("Column", 16);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Kicker("In this chain"));
        column.AddChild(Mentions("People", Why.People));
        column.AddChild(Mentions("Places", Why.Places));
        var systems = Ui.Column("Systems", 8);
        systems.AddChild(Ui.Body("Systems", "Label", Palette.Muted, 11));
        var chips = new HFlowContainer { Name = "Chips" };
        chips.AddThemeConstantOverride("h_separation", 6);
        chips.AddThemeConstantOverride("v_separation", 6);
        foreach (var system in Why.Systems) chips.AddChild(Ui.Chip(Categories.Label(system), name: system.ToString(), dot: Palette.For(system)));
        systems.AddChild(chips);
        column.AddChild(systems);
        var actions = Ui.Row("Actions", 6);
        var map = Ui.Button("Show on map", "ShowOnMap", primary: true);
        map.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        map.Disabled = Why.Places.Count == 0;
        map.Pressed += () => Host.Router.Open(Why.Places[0].Ref);
        var further = Ui.Button("Trace further", "TraceFurther");
        further.SizeFlagsHorizontal = SizeFlags.ExpandFill;
        further.Pressed += () =>
        {
            if (ShowingConsequences) ShowCauses();
            else ShowConsequences();
            further.Text = ShowingConsequences ? "Trace back" : "Trace further";
        };
        actions.AddChild(map);
        actions.AddChild(further);
        column.AddChild(Ui.Ruled("ActionsRow", actions));
        card.AddChild(column);
        return card;
    }

    private Control Mentions(string label, IReadOnlyList<Mention> mentions)
    {
        var block = Ui.Column(label, 4);
        block.AddChild(Ui.Body(label, "Label", Palette.Muted, 11));
        if (mentions.Count == 0) block.AddChild(Ui.Body("None recorded", "None", Palette.Muted, 13));
        var shown = mentions.Take(8).ToList();
        var text = string.Join(", ", shown.Select(m => m.Name)) + (mentions.Count > shown.Count ? $" and {mentions.Count - shown.Count} more" : "");
        var links = new List<Sim.Views.TextLink>();
        var at = 0;
        foreach (var m in shown)
        {
            links.Add(new Sim.Views.TextLink(at, m.Name.Length, m.Ref));
            at += m.Name.Length + 2;
        }
        if (mentions.Count > 0) block.AddChild(Ui.Linked(new LinkedText(text, links), "Names", Host.Router.Open, 13, Palette.Ink));
        return block;
    }
}
```

Replace `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System;
using System.Linq;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Runs the real scenes headless against a fresh tiny world and checks what they show (sub-project 4 §7). Exits 0 when
/// every check passes, 1 otherwise; CI runs it with `godot --headless --path game res://tests/HeadlessChecks.tscn`.
/// </summary>
public partial class HeadlessChecks : Node
{
    private readonly List<string> _failures = new();
    private int _checks;

    public override void _Ready() => _ = RunAsync();

    private async Task RunAsync()
    {
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "checks", $"check-{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.fw.db");
        try
        {
            var session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, 3, WorldSize.Tiny, () => System.DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, prehistoryYears: 8));
            var main = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://scenes/Main.tscn").Instantiate<Main>();
            main.Use(session);
            AddChild(main);
            await Until(() => main.CaughtUp, "the world catches up");
            await RunChecks(main);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Fail($"unexpected {e}");
        }
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "", ".bak1", ".bak2", ".bak3", "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path + suffix)) System.IO.File.Delete(path + suffix);
        GD.Print(_failures.Count == 0 ? $"HEADLESS CHECKS PASSED ({_checks})" : $"HEADLESS CHECKS FAILED: {string.Join("; ", _failures)}");
        GetTree().Quit(_failures.Count == 0 ? 0 : 1);
    }

    /// <summary>The checks for each screen; later tasks add theirs.</summary>
    private async Task RunChecks(Main main)
    {
        Check(main.Status.Text.StartsWith("Caught up · "), $"status reads caught up (\"{main.Status.Text}\")");
        await Frames(2);
        await DioramaChecks(main);
        await DigestChecks(main);
        await MapChecks(main);
        await PageChecks(main);
    }

    private async Task PageChecks(Main main)
    {
        var session = main.Session!;
        var person = session.Read((w, l) => l.All.Reverse().SelectMany(e => e.Actors.Values).First(a => a.Kind == "char" && w.Characters.ContainsKey(a.Id)).Id);
        main.Router.Open(new FantasyWorld.Sim.Events.EntityRef("char", person));
        await Frames(3);
        var character = main.Current as CharacterView;
        Check(character is not null && !main.Diorama.Visible, "a character link opens their page over the diorama");
        if (character is null) return;
        var screen = character.Character;
        Check(character.GetNode<Label>("Left/Who/Title").Text == $"{screen.Name}.", "the page is titled with their name");
        Check(character.GetNode("Left/Chips").GetChildCount() == screen.Traits.Count + (screen.Ambition.Length > 0 ? 1 : 0), "traits and ambition are chips");
        Check(character.Timeline.GetChildren().Count(c => c.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Event") || c.Name == "Highlight") == screen.Timeline.Count, "every deed is on the timeline");
        Check(character.GetNode("Ties/Column/List").GetChildCount() == Math.Max(1, screen.Ties.Count), "ties are listed");
        await Shot("character");
        var followed = session.IsFollowed(screen.Ref);
        character.ToggleFollow();
        Check(session.IsFollowed(screen.Ref) != followed && character.FollowButton.Text == (followed ? "Follow" : "Following"), "Follow follows");
        character.ToggleFollow();

        character.GetNode<Button>("Ties/Column/Actions/Family").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Frames(2);
        var shown = (main.Current as FamilyView)?.FindChildren("Person*", "", true, false).Count ?? -1;
        Check(shown == session.Family(person).Nodes.Count, $"the family tree shows everyone ({shown} of {session.Family(person).Nodes.Count})");
        await Shot("family");
        main.Router.Back();
        await Frames(2);
        Check(main.Current is CharacterView, "Back returns to the character");

        var eventId = session.Read((w, l) => l.All.Last(e => e.Causes.Count > 0 && l.TryGet(e.Causes[0].EventId, out _)).Id);
        main.Router.Explain(eventId);
        await Frames(3);
        var why = main.Current as WhyView;
        Check(why is not null, "a why chain opens");
        if (why is null) return;
        Check(why.Graph.Cards.Count == 1 + why.Why.Layers.Sum(l => l.Count) && why.Canvas.FindChildren("Card*", "", false, false).Count == why.Graph.Cards.Count,
            "every cause is a card");
        Check(why.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Summary").Text == why.Why.Summary, "the chain is summarised");
        await Shot("why");
        var cause = why.Graph.Cards[1].Card.EventId;
        why.Canvas.GetNode<PanelContainer>($"Card{cause}").EmitSignal(Control.SignalName.GuiInput, new InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = true });
        await Frames(2);
        Check(main.Router.Current == new Screen(ScreenKind.Why, cause), "clicking a cause traces it further back");
        main.Router.Back();
        await Frames(2);
        var again = (WhyView)main.Current!;
        again.GetNode<Button>("Chain/Column/ActionsRow/Actions/TraceFurther").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(again.ShowingConsequences && again.Graph.Cards.Count == 1 + again.Why.Consequences.Count, "trace further shows what it led to");
        main.Router.Home();
        await Frames(2);
        Check(main.Diorama.Visible && main.Current is DigestView, "home is the digest over the diorama");
    }

    private async Task MapChecks(Main main)
    {
        main.Diorama.Rig.Reset();
        main.Router.Go(new Screen(ScreenKind.Map));
        await Frames(30);
        var view = main.Current as MapHudView;
        Check(view is not null, "the map screen opens");
        if (view is null) return;
        var map = view.Map;
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Title").Text == $"{map.WorldName}.", "the map is titled with the world's name");
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Summary").Text == map.Summary, "and summarised");
        var tagged = map.Settlements.Count(s => MapLabels.Shows(s, view.Host.Diorama.Rig.NearestZoom, main.Diorama.Layers));
        Check(view.TagLayer.GetChildCount() == tagged, $"settlements big enough for the zoom are named ({tagged})");
        Check(view.GetNode("Legend").GetChildCount() == map.Legend.Count, "the legend lists the realms");
        await Shot("map");

        var tags = view.TagLayer.GetChildren().OfType<SettlementTag>().ToList();
        if (tags.Count > 0)
        {
            view.ShowCard(tags[0]);
            Check(view.HoverCard.Visible && view.HoverCard.FindChildren("Title", "Label", true, false).OfType<Label>().Single().Text == tags[0].Settlement.Card.Title,
                "hovering a settlement shows who holds it");
            await Shot("map-card");
        }

        var borders = view.GetNode<CheckBox>("Card/Column/Layers/Borders");
        borders.ButtonPressed = false;
        Check(!main.Diorama.BorderLines.Visible && !main.Diorama.Layers.HasFlag(MapLayers.Borders), "the borders layer can be hidden");
        borders.ButtonPressed = true;
        Check(view.GetNode<CheckBox>("Card/Column/Layers/TradeRoutes").Disabled, "trade routes are not modelled yet");
        view.GetNode<CheckBox>("Card/Column/Layers/Settlements").ButtonPressed = false;
        Check(!main.Diorama.SettlementLayer.Visible && view.TagLayer.GetChildren().OfType<SettlementTag>().All(t => t.Settlement.Marker is PinMarker.Conflict or PinMarker.RecentEvent),
            "hiding settlements leaves only places with news");
        view.GetNode<CheckBox>("Card/Column/Layers/Settlements").ButtonPressed = true;

        view.History.Value = 2;
        await Frames(2);
        Check(view.Year == 2 && view.Map.Historical && view.Map.Year == 2 && main.Diorama.ShownYear == 2, "the history slider shows the map of Year 2");
        await Shot("map-year-2");
        view.GetNode<Button>("Card/Column/ActionsRow/Actions/Reset").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(view.Year == view.Now && main.Diorama.ShownYear is null && view.History.Value == view.Now, "reset view returns to today");

        view.GetNode<Button>("Card/Column/Zoom/Presets/Region").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Frames(90);
        Check(main.Diorama.Rig.NearestZoom == Zoom.Region && view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Kicker").Text.EndsWith("REGION VIEW"), "the zoom presets descend");

        view.History.Value = 2;
        var settlement = map.Settlements.First(s => s.Position is not null);
        main.Router.Open(settlement.Ref);
        await Frames(2);
        Check(main.Current is MapHudView { Focus: { } focus } && focus == settlement.Ref, "a linked place opens the map");
        Check(main.Diorama.Rig.GoalDistance == OrbitRig.DistanceFor(Zoom.Town)
              && Diorama.ToGodot(main.Diorama.Rig.GoalTarget).DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(FantasyWorld.Client.Projection.ToBlock(main.Geography!.Terrain, settlement.Position!, 0))) < 0.01f,
            "and flies to it");
        main.Router.Home();
        await Frames(2);
        Check(main.Diorama.ShownYear is null, "leaving the map returns the diorama to today");
    }

    private async Task DigestChecks(Main main)
    {
        main.Router.Home();
        main.Diorama.Rig.Reset();
        await Frames(60);
        var view = main.Current as DigestView;
        Check(view is not null, "the digest is the first screen");
        if (view is null) return;
        var digest = main.Digest!;
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Title").Text == digest.Title, "the digest shows the year");
        Check(view.GetNode<Label>("Intro/Subtitle").Text == digest.Subtitle, "and the time away");
        Check(main.Session!.Digest().Items.Count == 0 && main.Session.Digest().Headline is null, "showing the digest marks it seen");
        Check(digest.Headline is null || view.Content.GetNode<RichTextLabel>("Headline/HeadlineTitle").GetParsedText() == digest.Headline.Title.Text,
            "the headline is shown");
        Check(view.Content.GetNodeOrNull("Items")?.GetChildCount() == digest.Items.Count, $"every item is listed ({digest.Items.Count})");
        Check(view.PinLayer.GetChildCount() == digest.Pins.Count && main.Diorama.Pins.Count == digest.Pins.Count, $"one pin per place ({digest.Pins.Count})");
        await Shot("digest");

        if (digest.Pins.Count > 0)
        {
            var pin = view.PinLayer.GetChild<PinTag>(0);
            var expected = main.Diorama.ScreenOf(main.Diorama.Pins[0].Position) - new Godot.Vector2(11, 11);
            Check(pin.Position.DistanceTo(expected) < 1, "pins follow their places on screen");
            view.FlyTo(digest.Pins[0].EventId);
            await Frames(2);
            Check(main.Diorama.Rig.GoalDistance == OrbitRig.DistanceFor(Zoom.Region), "an item flies the camera to its place");
        }

        view.ShowChronicle(true);
        await Frames(2);
        Check(view.Content.GetNodeOrNull("Chronicle") is not null && view.ShowingChronicle, "the toggle shows the full chronicle");
        await Shot("digest-chronicle");
        view.ShowChronicle(false);

        if (digest.Headline is { CanExplain: true } headline)
        {
            view.Content.GetNode<Button>("Headline/Why").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Check(main.Router.Current == new Screen(ScreenKind.Why, headline.EventId), "\"Why did this happen?\" opens the why chain");
            main.Router.Back();
            await Frames(2);
            Check(main.Current is DigestView, "Back returns to the digest");
        }
    }

    private async Task DioramaChecks(Main main)
    {
        var diorama = main.Diorama;
        var map = main.Session!.Map();
        Check(diorama.Block.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } block && block.GetSurfaceCount() == 2, "the block has a top and walls");
        Check(diorama.GetNodeOrNull<MeshInstance3D>("Water")?.Mesh is not null, "the sea is drawn");
        var settlements = map.Settlements.Count(s => s.Position is not null);
        Check(diorama.Markers.Count(m => m.Shape == MarkerShape.Settlement) == settlements
              && diorama.SettlementLayer.GetNode<MultiMeshInstance3D>("Roofs").Multimesh.InstanceCount >= 3 * settlements,
            "every settlement is a cluster of houses");
        Check(diorama.StructureLayer.GetChildCount() == map.Structures.Count(s => s.Position is not null), "every landmark and labyrinth stands on the block");
        Check(diorama.TreeCount > 100, $"woods are planted ({diorama.TreeCount} trees)");
        Check(map.Borders.Count == 0 || diorama.BorderLines.Mesh is ArrayMesh { } lines && lines.GetSurfaceCount() == 2, "realm borders are drawn, ink over a halo");

        diorama.Rig.Reset();
        await Frames(90);
        await Shot("diorama");
        Check(diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Eye)) < 0.01f, "the camera follows the rig");
        var target = diorama.Markers[0];
        var onScreen = diorama.ScreenOf(target.Position);
        Check(diorama.Pick(onScreen) is { } picked && (picked == target.Ref || diorama.Markers.Any(m => m.Ref == picked && diorama.ScreenOf(m.Position).DistanceTo(onScreen) < 1)),
            "clicking a marker picks it");
        Check(diorama.Pick(new Godot.Vector2(-500, -500)) is null, "clicking empty space picks nothing");

        var settlement = map.Settlements.First(s => s.Position is not null);
        diorama.Focus(settlement.Position!, Zoom.Town);
        await Frames(120);
        await Shot("diorama-town");
        Check(diorama.Rig.NearestZoom == Zoom.Town && diorama.Camera.Position.DistanceTo(Diorama.ToGodot(diorama.Rig.Target)) < 1f, "focusing descends to the town");
    }

    /// <summary>With `-- --shots &lt;dir&gt;` (and a real display), saves what the screen shows, for visual review.</summary>
    private async Task Shot(string name)
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        var at = System.Array.IndexOf(args, "--shots");
        if (at < 0 || at + 1 >= args.Length || DisplayServer.GetName() == "headless") return;
        await Frames(4);
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(args[at + 1]);
        GetViewport().GetTexture().GetImage().SavePng(System.IO.Path.Combine(args[at + 1], name + ".png"));
    }

    private void Check(bool ok, string what)
    {
        _checks++;
        if (!ok) Fail(what);
    }

    private void Fail(string what)
    {
        _failures.Add(what);
        GD.PrintErr("FAIL: " + what);
    }

    private async Task Frames(int n)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
    }

    private async Task Until(System.Func<bool> condition, string what, int maxFrames = 600)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < maxFrames && !condition(); i++) await Frames(1);
        Check(condition(), what);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;
using Godot;

namespace FantasyWorld.Game.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Renders a full-size world from the angles a player sees, for visual review and the README:
/// `godot --path game res://tests/Showcase.tscn -- --shots &lt;dir&gt; [--seed n] [--years n]` (needs a display).
/// </summary>
public partial class Showcase : Node
{
    public override void _Ready() => _ = RunAsync();

    private async Task RunAsync()
    {
        var args = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        string? Arg(string name) => System.Array.IndexOf(args, name) is var i and >= 0 && i + 1 < args.Length ? args[i + 1] : null;
        var dir = Arg("--shots") ?? System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "showcase");
        var seed = ulong.Parse(Arg("--seed") ?? "7");
        var years = int.Parse(Arg("--years") ?? "40");
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(OS.GetUserDataDir(), "showcase", $"showcase-{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.fw.db");
        var session = await Task.Run(() => Session.Create(path, seed, WorldSize.Medium, () => System.DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, prehistoryYears: years));
        var main = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://scenes/Main.tscn").Instantiate<Main>();
        main.Use(session);
        AddChild(main);
        while (!main.CaughtUp) await Frames(1);
        System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);

        await Shot(main, dir, "1-digest");
        main.Router.Go(new Screen(ScreenKind.Map));
        await Shot(main, dir, "2-map");
        var city = main.Diorama.Markers.Where(m => m.Shape == MarkerShape.Settlement).MaxBy(m => m.Size)!;
        main.Diorama.Focus(FantasyWorld.Client.Projection.ToMap(city.Position), Zoom.Region);
        await Shot(main, dir, "3-region");
        main.Diorama.Rig.Preset(Zoom.Town);
        await Shot(main, dir, "4-town");
        main.Diorama.Rig.Reset();
        main.Diorama.Rig.Drag(-260, -90);
        await Shot(main, dir, "5-low-angle");

        var notable = session.Read((w, l) => w.Characters.Values.Where(c => c.IsActive).MaxBy(c => l.Involving(c.Ref).Count())!.Id);
        main.Router.Open(new FantasyWorld.Sim.Events.EntityRef("char", notable));
        await Shot(main, dir, "6-character", 10);
        main.Router.Go(new Screen(ScreenKind.Family, notable));
        await Shot(main, dir, "7-family", 10);
        var war = session.Read((w, l) => l.All.Last(e => e.Type == "WarDeclared").Id);
        main.Router.Explain(war);
        await Shot(main, dir, "8-why", 10);

        main.QueueFree();
        await Frames(2);
        foreach (var suffix in new[] { "", ".bak1", ".bak2", ".bak3", "-wal", "-shm" })
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path + suffix)) System.IO.File.Delete(path + suffix);
        GD.Print($"SHOWCASE SAVED TO {dir}");
        GetTree().Quit();
    }

    private async Task Shot(Main main, string dir, string name, int frames = 150)
    {
        await Frames(frames);
        GetViewport().GetTexture().GetImage().SavePng(System.IO.Path.Combine(dir, name + ".png"));
    }

    private async Task Frames(int n)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
    }
}
```
