Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Narration/TemplateNarrator.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Chronicle;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;

/// <summary>Turns events into prose (spec §8.7).</summary>
public interface INarrator
{
    /// <summary>One event as a sentence or two.</summary>
    string Narrate(WorldEvent ev);

    /// <summary>A why-chain as a short passage: the event, then what led to it.</summary>
    string NarrateChain(ChainNode chain);

    /// <summary>The morning digest: what happened while the reader was away.</summary>
    string Digest(DigestView digest);
}

/// <summary>
/// Per-event-type templates with connectives ("because", "which followed") — the offline narrator and the test
/// baseline. Placeholders: <c>{role}</c> names an actor, <c>{@place}</c> the place, <c>{=key}</c> a payload value.
/// A sentence whose placeholder is missing falls back to a generic line, so new event types always read.
/// </summary>
public sealed partial class TemplateNarrator(World world) : INarrator
{
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Templates = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        ["AllianceBroken"] = "The alliance between {a} and {b} was broken.",
        ["AllianceFormed"] = "{a} and {b} swore an alliance.",
        ["AllyJoinedWar"] = "{ally} took up arms in {war}.",
        ["ArmyRaised"] = "{realm} raised {army}, {=soldiers} strong, for {war}.",
        ["ArmyRouted"] = "{army} of {realm} broke and fled at {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactClaimed"] = "{claimant} claimed {artifact} from the treasury of {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactCurseRevealed"] = "The curse of {artifact} showed itself in {holder}: {=curse}.",
        ["ArtifactForged"] = "{maker} forged {artifact} at {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactFound"] = "{delver} found {artifact} in the depths of {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactInherited"] = "{heir} inherited {artifact}.",
        ["ArtifactStolen"] = "{thief} stole {artifact} from {victim}.",
        ["BattleFought"] = "At {@place}, {victor} defeated {vanquished} in a battle of {war}.",
        ["Knighted"] = "{liege} knighted {knight} in the service of {realm}.",
        ["DistinguishedInBattle"] = "{character} won glory in the battle at {@place}.",
        ["WoundedInBattle"] = "{character} was {=wound} in the battle at {@place}.",
        ["CapitalMoved"] = "{realm} moved its seat to {@place}.",
        ["CharacterBorn"] = "{child} was born at {@place} to {mother} and {father}.",
        ["CharacterDied"] = "{deceased} died of {=cause} at {=age}.",
        ["ComingOfAge"] = "{character} came of age, dreaming of {=ambition}.",
        ["ChronicleCompacted"] = "the chronicle of {=period} was condensed ({=events} minor events: {=most common}).",
        ["CouncilAppointed"] = "{member} became {=seat} of {realm}.",
        ["DelveFailed"] = "{delver} sought {artifact} in {@place} and failed.",
        ["DuelFought"] = "{challenger} fought {defender} {=stakes} at {@place}.",
        ["EraBegan"] = "{=era} began.",
        ["FoodShortageBegan"] = "Food ran short in {@place}.",
        ["FriendshipFormed"] = "{a} and {b} became friends.",
        ["HolyWarProclaimed"] = "{a} proclaimed a holy war against {b} in the name of {=faith}.",
        ["LabyrinthOpened"] = "{structure} opened in {@place}.",
        ["LandmarkBegun"] = "{builder} began raising {structure} at {@place}.",
        ["LandmarkCompleted"] = "{structure} was completed at {@place}.",
        ["IndependenceDeclared"] = "{realm} threw off the rule of {former liege}.",
        ["MarriageWed"] = "{spouse} and {partner} were wed at {@place}.",
        ["MonsterHuntFailed"] = "{hunter} hunted the monsters of {@place} and was driven back.",
        ["MonstersSlain"] = "{hunter} slew the monsters of {@place}.",
        ["NewLordRose"] = "{lord} rose to rule {@place}.",
        ["PeaceSigned"] = "{war} ended: {victor} over {vanquished} ({=terms}).",
        ["PlagueOutbreak"] = "Plague broke out in {@place}.",
        ["RealmFell"] = "{realm} fell.",
        ["RegionCeded"] = "{@place} passed from {from} to {to}.",
        ["RegionLiberated"] = "{realm} freed {@place}.",
        ["RegionOccupied"] = "{army} of {realm} took {@place}.",
        ["RevoltBegan"] = "{@place} rose in revolt.",
        ["RivalryBegan"] = "{a} and {b} became rivals over {=over}.",
        ["RivalryDeclared"] = "{a} and {b} declared each other rivals.",
        ["SettlementAbandoned"] = "{@place} was abandoned.",
        ["SettlementSacked"] = "{commander} sacked {@place}; {=dead} died.",
        ["SiegeBegan"] = "{army} of {realm} laid siege to {@place}.",
        ["SiegeEnded"] = "The siege of {@place} ended: it {=outcome}.",
        ["StructureRuined"] = "{@place} fell into ruin.",
        ["SuccessionOccurred"] = "{heir} succeeded {predecessor} at {@place}.",
        ["TheftFoiled"] = "{victim} caught {thief} reaching for {artifact}.",
        ["WarDeclared"] = "{attacker} declared war on {defender} for {=casus belli}: {war}.",
    };

    private readonly Func<EntityRef, string?> _nameOf = Names.Of(world);

    /// <summary>The event as one sentence, without date or annotations (headlines, lists).</summary>
    public string Sentence(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var sentence = Templates.TryGetValue(ev.Type, out var template) && Fill(template, ev) is { } filled ? filled : Generic(ev);
        return char.ToUpperInvariant(sentence[0]) + sentence[1..];
    }

    public string Narrate(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var sentence = Templates.TryGetValue(ev.Type, out var template) && Fill(template, ev) is { } filled
            ? template.StartsWith('{') ? filled : LowerFirst(filled)
            : LowerFirst(Generic(ev));
        var text = new StringBuilder($"In year {ev.Date.Year}, {sentence}");
        if (ev.Payload.TryGetValue("motive", out var motive)) text.Append($" ({motive})");
        if (ev.Payload.TryGetValue("account", out var account)) text.Append($" {account}");
        return text.ToString();
    }

    public string NarrateChain(ChainNode chain)
    {
        var text = new StringBuilder(Narrate(chain.Event));
        Causes(text, chain, first: true);
        return text.ToString();
    }

    public string Digest(DigestView digest)
    {
        if (digest.EventCount == 0) return "Nothing of note has happened since you last looked.";
        var text = new StringBuilder($"From {digest.From} to {digest.To}, {digest.EventCount} events were recorded. The chronicle remembers:");
        foreach (var group in digest.Groups)
        {
            if (group.Label.Length > 0) text.Append($"\n{group.Label}:");
            foreach (var entry in group.Entries) text.Append($"\n- {Narrate(entry.Event)} [#{entry.Event.Id}]");
        }
        return text.ToString();
    }

    private void Causes(StringBuilder text, ChainNode node, bool first)
    {
        foreach (var cause in node.Causes)
        {
            var connective = first ? "This happened because" : "That followed from what came before:";
            text.Append($" {connective} {LowerFirst(Narrate(cause.Event))}");
            Causes(text, cause, first: false);
        }
    }

    private string? Fill(string template, WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var missing = false;
        var result = Placeholder().Replace(template, m =>
        {
            var key = m.Groups[1].Value;
            string? value = key switch
            {
                "@place" => ev.Place is { } place ? _nameOf(place) : null,
                _ when key.StartsWith('=') => ev.Payload.GetValueOrDefault(key[1..]),
                _ => ev.Actors.TryGetValue(key, out var actor) ? _nameOf(actor) : null,
            };
            missing |= value is null;
            return value ?? "";
        });
        return missing ? null : result;
    }

    private string Generic(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var who = ev.Actors.Select(a => _nameOf(a.Value)).Where(n => n is not null).ToList();
        var text = Briefs.Spaced(ev.Type);
        if (who.Count > 0) text += $" involving {string.Join(" and ", who)}";
        if (ev.Place is { } place && _nameOf(place) is { } where) text += $" at {where}";
        return char.ToUpperInvariant(text[0]) + text[1..] + ".";
    }

    /// <summary>Sentences that open with a name keep their capital; others continue the "In year N," clause.</summary>
    private static string LowerFirst(string sentence) =>
        sentence.Length > 0 ? char.ToLowerInvariant(sentence[0]) + sentence[1..] : sentence;

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\{([^}]+)\}")]
    private static partial Regex Placeholder();
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Systems/MilitarySystem.Battles.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Globalization;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Agency;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Geo;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;

/// <summary>
/// Battles (spec §6 step 4): where hostile armies meet, the largest of each side fight. Strength comes
/// from troops, the commander's martial skill, morale and supply, with defenders favoured on their own
/// ground and in rough terrain; a seeded roll decides. Losers suffer heavier casualties and retreat (or
/// break and disband); every commander and knight present faces a fate chosen by the arbiter.
/// </summary>
public sealed partial class MilitarySystem
{
    partial void FightBattles(SimContext ctx)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        foreach (var war in world.Wars.Values.Where(w => !w.Ended).ToList())
            foreach (var regionId in Active(world).Where(a => a.WarId == war.Id).Select(a => a.RegionId).Distinct().OrderBy(id => id).ToList())
            {
                var here = Active(world).Where(a => a.WarId == war.Id && a.RegionId == regionId).ToList();
                var attacker = here.Where(a => war.IsAttacker(a.RealmId)).OrderByDescending(a => a.Soldiers).ThenBy(a => a.Id).FirstOrDefault();
                var defender = here.Where(a => !war.IsAttacker(a.RealmId)).OrderByDescending(a => a.Soldiers).ThenBy(a => a.Id).FirstOrDefault();
                if (attacker is not null && defender is not null) Battle(ctx, war, attacker, defender, world.Regions[regionId]);
            }
    }

    /// <summary>Fighting strength: troops weighted by quality, scaled by commander, morale and supply.</summary>
    public static double Strength(World world, Army army, Region region)
    {
        var magic = region.LeyPower / 50.0;
        var troops = army.Levies + army.MenAtArms * 3.0 + army.Knights.Count * 25 + army.Mages * 30 * magic;
        var martial = world.Characters.TryGetValue(army.CommanderId, out var c) ? c.Attributes.Martial : 5;
        return troops * (0.6 + martial / 25.0) * (army.Morale / 100 + 0.2) * (0.5 + army.Supply / 200);
    }

    private void Battle(SimContext ctx, War war, Army attacker, Army defender, Region region)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        var a = Strength(world, attacker, region);
        var d = Strength(world, defender, region);
        if (Friendly(world, war, world.Realms[defender.RealmId], region)) d *= 1.2;
        if (region.Biome is Biome.Hills or Biome.Mountain or Biome.Forest or Biome.Swamp or Biome.Jungle) d *= 1.15;
        var attackerWins = ctx.Rng.Chance(a / Math.Max(1, a + d));
        var (winner, loser) = attackerWins ? (attacker, defender) : (defender, attacker);
        var winnerLosses = Casualties(winner, ctx.Rng.NextInt(5, 16) / 100.0);
        var loserLosses = Casualties(loser, ctx.Rng.NextInt(25, 41) / 100.0);

        var actors = new Dictionary<string, EntityRef>
        {
            ["victor"] = world.Realms[winner.RealmId].Ref,
            ["vanquished"] = world.Realms[loser.RealmId].Ref,
            ["war"] = war.Ref,
        };
        if (world.Characters.TryGetValue(winner.CommanderId, out var victorious)) actors["victor commander"] = victorious.Ref;
        if (world.Characters.TryGetValue(loser.CommanderId, out var defeated)) actors["vanquished commander"] = defeated.Ref;
        // The knights who rode with each host are named on the battle, so it is part of their lives too.
        foreach (var (army, side) in new[] { (winner, "victor"), (loser, "vanquished") })
        {
            var n = 0;
            foreach (var knight in army.Knights.Where(id => world.Characters.TryGetValue(id, out var k) && k.IsActive).Take(3))
                actors[$"{side} knight {++n}"] = world.Characters[knight].Ref;
        }
        var previous = ctx.Log.Involving(war.Ref).LastOrDefault(e => e.Type is "BattleFought" or "SiegeEnded");
        var causes = new List<Cause> { new(war.DeclaredEventId, CauseKind.Enabler, 0.5) };
        if (previous is not null) causes.Add(new Cause(previous.Id, CauseKind.Enabler, 0.4));
        var battle = ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("BattleFought", Actors: actors, Place: region.Ref,
            Payload: new Dictionary<string, string>
            {
                ["victor losses"] = winnerLosses.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                ["vanquished losses"] = loserLosses.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            },
            Causes: causes, Importance: Math.Clamp(30 + (winnerLosses + loserLosses) / 100, 30, 75), Tags: new[] { "War" }));

        war.Battles++;
        var share = (winnerLosses + loserLosses) / (double)Math.Max(1, attacker.Soldiers + defender.Soldiers + winnerLosses + loserLosses);
        war.BattleScore = Math.Clamp(war.BattleScore + (attackerWins ? 1 : -1) * (5 + 20 * share), -60, 60);
        winner.Morale = Math.Min(100, winner.Morale + 10);
        loser.Morale = Math.Max(0, loser.Morale - 25);
        Statecraft.Remember(world.Realms[loser.RealmId], battle.Id, winner.RealmId, -40, 60);

        foreach (var (army, won, foe) in new[] { (winner, true, loser), (loser, false, winner) })
            foreach (var id in army.Knights.Append(army.CommanderId).Where(id => id != 0).ToList())
                if (world.Characters.TryGetValue(id, out var c) && c.IsActive)
                    FaceBattle(ctx, c, won, battle, world.Characters.GetValueOrDefault(foe.CommanderId));

        // The day's hero: the most martial of the victors still standing.
        if (winner.Knights.Append(winner.CommanderId).Where(id => world.Characters.TryGetValue(id, out var h) && h.IsActive)
                .Select(id => world.Characters[id]).OrderByDescending(h => h.Attributes.Martial).ThenBy(h => h.Id).FirstOrDefault() is { } hero)
        {
            hero.Renown = Math.Min(100, hero.Renown + 4);
            ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("DistinguishedInBattle", Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["character"] = hero.Ref, ["war"] = war.Ref },
                Place: region.Ref, Causes: new[] { new Cause(battle.Id, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) },
                Importance: Fates.Importance(hero, 28), Tags: new[] { "War", "Characters" }));
        }

        if (loser.Morale < 20 || loser.Soldiers < 50)
        {
            ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("ArmyRouted", Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["army"] = loser.Ref, ["realm"] = world.Realms[loser.RealmId].Ref },
                Place: region.Ref, Causes: new[] { new Cause(battle.Id, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) }, Importance: 35, Tags: new[] { "War" }));
            Statecraft.Disband(world, loser);
        }
        else
        {
            loser.Objective = ArmyObjective.Return;
            loser.TargetRegionId = 0;
        }
    }

    private static int Casualties(Army army, double share)
    {
        var levies = (int)(army.Levies * share);
        var menAtArms = (int)(army.MenAtArms * share);
        var mages = (int)(army.Mages * share);
        army.Levies -= levies;
        army.MenAtArms -= menAtArms;
        army.Mages -= mages;
        return levies + menAtArms + mages;
    }

    /// <summary>The legal fates of a notable in battle; losers are far likelier to fall.</summary>
    public static Encounter BattleFate(Character c, bool won)
    {
        var (dead, maimed, wounded) = won ? (0.03, 0.03, 0.12) : (0.12, 0.08, 0.2);
        return new Encounter("battle", c.Id, 0, 1, 1, true, new[]
        {
            new EncounterOutcome("unharmed", 1 - dead - maimed - wounded, won, Fate.Unharmed, Fate.Unharmed),
            new EncounterOutcome("wounded", wounded, won, Fate.Wounded, Fate.Unharmed),
            new EncounterOutcome("maimed", maimed, won, Fate.Maimed, Fate.Unharmed),
            new EncounterOutcome("slain", dead, won, Fate.Dead, Fate.Unharmed),
        });
    }

    private void FaceBattle(SimContext ctx, Character c, bool won, WorldEvent battle, Character? enemyCommander)
    {
        var fate = _arbiter.Resolve(BattleFate(c, won), ctx.Rng).AttackerFate;
        if (fate == Fate.Dead)
        {
            Fates.Die(ctx, c, "battle", new[] { new Cause(battle.Id, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) }, enemyCommander is { IsActive: true } ? enemyCommander : null);
            return;
        }
        Fates.Injure(c, fate, ctx.Rng);
        if (fate is Fate.Wounded or Fate.Maimed)
            ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("WoundedInBattle", Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["character"] = c.Ref },
                Place: battle.Place, Causes: new[] { new Cause(battle.Id, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) },
                Payload: new Dictionary<string, string> { ["wound"] = fate == Fate.Maimed ? "maimed" : "wounded" },
                Importance: Fates.Importance(c, fate == Fate.Maimed ? 26 : 20), Tags: new[] { "War", "Characters" }));
        if (!won) return;
        c.Renown = Math.Min(100, c.Renown + 6);
        if (c.Renown >= 50 && !c.Has(Traits.Renowned))
        {
            c.Traits.Add(Traits.Renowned);
            c.Traits.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        }
    }
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Systems/PromotionSystem.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;

/// <summary>
/// Populace promotion (spec §3): when a story needs a person, one steps out of the aggregate
/// population. Empty seats get a new lord (citing the death that emptied them), revolts get a
/// leader, monster-ridden regions get a hero, leaderless villages get an elder.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PromotionSystem : ISimSystem
{
    public const double HeroPerMonth = 0.2;
    public const double ElderPerMonth = 0.05;

    public string Name => "promotion";

    public void OnMonthlyTick(SimContext ctx)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        foreach (var ruin in world.Settlements.Values.Where(s => s.Abandoned))
            Evacuate(ctx, ruin);
        var residents = world.ActiveCharacters.ToLookup(c => c.SettlementId);
        // A realm's capital always gets a ruler, even after it has shrunk to a village.
        var capitals = world.Realms.Values.Where(r => !r.Destroyed).Select(r => r.CapitalId).ToHashSet();
        foreach (var s in world.Settlements.Values.Where(s => !s.Abandoned).ToList())
        {
            if ((s.Tier >= SettlementTier.Town || capitals.Contains(s.Id)) && s.LordId == 0) RaiseLord(ctx, s, residents[s.Id]);
            if (s.Conditions.TryGetValue(Conditions.Revolt, out var revolt) && !residents[s.Id].Any(c => c.Role == Role.Rebel))
                RaiseRebel(ctx, s, revolt);
            if (s.Tier == SettlementTier.Village && !residents[s.Id].Any() && ctx.Rng.Chance(ElderPerMonth))
                Promote(ctx, s, Role.Elder, "ElderChosen", 5, Array.Empty<Cause>());
        }

        foreach (var region in world.Regions.Values)
        {
            if (!region.Conditions.TryGetValue(Conditions.Dangerous, out var danger)) continue;
            var settlements = world.LivingSettlements(region).ToList();
            if (settlements.Count == 0 || settlements.Any(s => residents[s.Id].Any(c => c.Role is Role.Adventurer or Role.Knight)))
                continue;
            if (!ctx.Rng.Chance(HeroPerMonth)) continue;
            var home = settlements.OrderByDescending(s => s.Population).ThenBy(s => s.Id).First();
            var (hero, _) = Promote(ctx, home, Role.Adventurer, "HeroRose", 25, new[] { new Cause(danger, CauseKind.Trigger, 0.9) });
            hero.Ambition = Ambitions.Renown;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Settlements are abandoned in the settlements' yearly tick; promotion runs after it and evacuates the same day.</summary>
    public void OnYearlyTick(SimContext ctx)
    {
        foreach (var ruin in ctx.World.Settlements.Values.Where(s => s.Abandoned))
            Evacuate(ctx, ruin);
        foreach (var realm in ctx.World.Realms.Values.Where(r => !r.Destroyed).OrderBy(r => r.Id).ToList())
            Knight(ctx, realm);
    }

    public const int MaxKnightsPerYear = 2;
    public const int MinKnightMartial = 8;

    /// <summary>
    /// Realms keep a household of knights, about one for every two settlements (at most eight): each year the ruler
    /// knights up to two of the most martial adults of the realm's land who hold no other office. Knights ride with
    /// the realm's host in war.
    /// </summary>
    private static void Knight(SimContext ctx, Realm realm)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        var territory = Realms.Territory(world, realm).Select(r => r.Id).ToHashSet();
        var settlements = world.Settlements.Values.Count(s => !s.Abandoned && territory.Contains(s.RegionId));
        var wanted = Math.Clamp(settlements / 2, 1, 8);
        var people = world.ActiveCharacters.Where(c => territory.Contains(world.Settlements[c.SettlementId].RegionId)).ToList();
        var missing = Math.Min(MaxKnightsPerYear, wanted - people.Count(c => c.Role == Role.Knight));
        if (missing <= 0) return;
        var ruler = Realms.RulerOf(world, realm);
        foreach (var candidate in people
                     .Where(c => c.Role is Role.None or Role.Adventurer && c.Stage(ctx.Date) == LifeStage.Adult && c.Attributes.Martial >= MinKnightMartial
                                 && c.Id != ruler?.Id)
                     .OrderByDescending(c => c.Attributes.Martial + c.Renown / 10).ThenBy(c => c.Id).Take(missing).ToList())
        {
            candidate.Role = Role.Knight;
            var actors = new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["knight"] = candidate.Ref, ["realm"] = realm.Ref };
            if (ruler is not null) actors["liege"] = ruler.Ref;
            ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("Knighted", Actors: actors, Place: world.Settlements[candidate.SettlementId].Ref,
                Importance: Fates.Importance(candidate, 22), Tags: new[] { "Characters" }));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The ablest adult already living there takes the seat; failing that, someone from the populace.</summary>
    private static void RaiseLord(SimContext ctx, Settlement seat, IEnumerable<Character> residents)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        var causes = seat.Conditions.TryGetValue(Fates.VacantSeat, out var death)
            ? new[] { new Cause(death, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) }
            : Array.Empty<Cause>();
        var candidate = residents
            .Where(c => c.Stage(ctx.Date) != LifeStage.Child && c.Role is not (Role.Lord or Role.Rebel))
            .OrderByDescending(c => c.Attributes.Stewardship + c.Attributes.Diplomacy + c.Renown / 5).ThenBy(c => c.Id)
            .FirstOrDefault();
        if (candidate is null)
        {
            (candidate, _) = Promote(ctx, seat, Role.Lord, "NewLordRose", Importance(seat), causes);
        }
        else
        {
            candidate.Role = Role.Lord;
            ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("NewLordRose",
                Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["lord"] = candidate.Ref },
                Place: seat.Ref, Causes: causes, Importance: Importance(seat), Tags: new[] { "Characters", "Politics" }));
        }
        seat.LordId = candidate.Id;
        seat.Conditions.Remove(Fates.VacantSeat);
        world.Houses[candidate.HouseId].SeatSettlementId = seat.Id;
    }

    private static void RaiseRebel(SimContext ctx, Settlement s, long revolt)
    {
        var (rebel, rose) = Promote(ctx, s, Role.Rebel, "RebelLeaderRose", 30, new[] { new Cause(revolt, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) });
        rebel.Renown = 10;
        rebel.Ambition = Ambitions.Renown;
        if (s.LordId == 0) return;
        var lord = ctx.World.Characters[s.LordId];
        Fates.Remember(rebel, rose.Id, lord.Id, -50, 60);
        Fates.Remember(lord, rose.Id, rebel.Id, -50, 60);
    }

    /// <summary>A new notable from the settlement's populace (one adult fewer in the aggregate), with a new house.</summary>
    private static (Character Character, WorldEvent Rose) Promote(SimContext ctx, Settlement s, Role role, string eventType, int importance, IReadOnlyList<Cause> causes)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        var region = world.Regions[s.RegionId];
        var house = CharacterFactory.NewHouse(world, ctx.Rng, region.CultureId, s.Id);
        var born = ctx.Date.Day - (long)ctx.Rng.NextInt(18, 46) * WorldDate.DaysPerYear - ctx.Rng.NextInt(0, 360);
        var c = CharacterFactory.Create(world, ctx.Rng, s.Id, ctx.Rng.Chance(0.5) ? Sex.Female : Sex.Male, born, house.Id, role);
        if (s.Adults > 0) s.Adults--;
        var rose = ctx.Emit(new EventDraft(eventType,
            Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { [role == Role.Lord ? "lord" : "character"] = c.Ref },
            Place: s.Ref, Causes: causes, Importance: importance, Tags: new[] { "Characters" }));
        return (c, rose);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Notables leave a settlement that has been abandoned for the largest living settlement in the
    /// region (else a neighbouring region, else anywhere); its lord loses the dead seat.
    /// </summary>
    private static void Evacuate(SimContext ctx, Settlement ruin)
    {
        var world = ctx.World;
        var people = world.ActiveCharacters.Where(c => c.SettlementId == ruin.Id).ToList();
        if (people.Count == 0 && ruin.LordId == 0) return;
        var region = world.Regions[ruin.RegionId];
        var regions = new[] { region.Id }.Concat(world.Geography?.Graph.Neighbours(region.Id) ?? Array.Empty<long>())
            .Select(id => world.Regions[id]).Concat(world.Regions.Values);
        var refuge = regions.Select(r => world.LivingSettlements(r).OrderByDescending(s => s.Population).ThenBy(s => s.Id).FirstOrDefault())
            .FirstOrDefault(s => s is not null);
        if (refuge is null) return;

        var abandonment = ctx.Log.Involving(ruin.Ref).LastOrDefault(e => e.Type == "SettlementAbandoned");
        if (ruin.LordId != 0)
        {
            world.Characters[ruin.LordId].Role = Role.None;
            ruin.LordId = 0;
        }
        foreach (var c in people) c.SettlementId = refuge.Id;
        ctx.Emit(new EventDraft("NotablesFled",
            Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef> { ["from"] = ruin.Ref },
            Place: refuge.Ref,
            Payload: new Dictionary<string, string> { ["people"] = people.Count.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) },
            Causes: abandonment is not null ? new[] { new Cause(abandonment.Id, CauseKind.Trigger, 1.0) } : null,
            Importance: 15, Tags: new[] { "Characters" }));
    }

    private static int Importance(Settlement seat) => seat.Tier == SettlementTier.City ? 45 : 30;
}
```

---

### Task 13: War on the map

War drawn the way a strategy map draws it: a broad campaign arrow along each host's road to its target that draws itself in and then flows (attackers red, defenders blue); a banner in the realm's colours that waves on its pole and marches from region to region as days pass, with a tag naming the host, its strength, its commander and its knights; fronts where the warring sides' lands meet, with teeth facing the attacker; occupied land hatched and outlined in the occupier's side colour; and crossed swords over this year's battlefields. Clicking a banner opens its commander, clicking crossed swords the battle's why chain; the map's *Armies & fronts* layer toggles it all. The map view model gains the hosts (with route, leaders and strength) and the year's battles; a film scene records a march for review.

**Files:**
- Create: `game/scripts/WarLayer.cs`, `game/scripts/screens/ArmyTag.cs`, `game/tests/WarMovie.cs`, `game/tests/WarMovie.tscn`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/WarLayout.cs`
- Modify: `README.md`, `game/scripts/Diorama.cs`, `game/scripts/Main.cs`, `game/scripts/screens/MapHudView.cs`, `game/tests/HeadlessChecks.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/BlockMesh.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/MapLabels.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Client/Markers.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Views/MapScreen.cs`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Client/WarLayoutTests.cs`
- Test (modify): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Views/Golden/map.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–12; `Army`, `War` (Plan 4).
- Produces: `record MapArmy`, `MapBattle`; `MapScreen.Armies`, `MapScreen.Battles`, `MapScreen.BattleDays`; `MeshData.Uvs`; `record Arrow`, `Front`; `static class WarLayout { Attacker; Defender; SideColour; Colour; Arrows; ArrowMesh; Fronts; Occupation; OccupationOutline }`; `MapLayers.Armies`; `partial class WarLayer : Node3D { Show(map, geography); BannerAt; BannerTop; ScaleFor; Armies; RecentBattles }`; `ArmyTag`; `Main` routes picked banners and battles; `game/tests/WarMovie.tscn`; README war section.

```csharp
using System.Numerics;
using FantasyWorld.Client;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Core;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Views;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Client;

public class WarLayoutTests
{
    /// <summary>A tiny world where realm A's host marches across the border into B's land, which A partly occupies.</summary>
    private static (World World, EventLog Log, Army Army, Region Target) AtWar()
    {
        var world = WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(3, WorldSize.Tiny), DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var graph = world.Geography!.Graph;
        var link = graph.Links.First(l => world.Regions[l.A].OwnerRealmId != 0 && world.Regions[l.B].OwnerRealmId != 0
                                          && world.Regions[l.A].OwnerRealmId != world.Regions[l.B].OwnerRealmId);
        var (home, target) = (world.Regions[link.A], world.Regions[link.B]);
        var (a, b) = (world.Realms[home.OwnerRealmId], world.Realms[target.OwnerRealmId]);
        var log = new EventLog();
        var war = new War { Id = world.Ids.Take(War.Kind), Name = "The Border War", AttackerId = a.Id, DefenderId = b.Id, Attackers = { a.Id }, Defenders = { b.Id }, StartDay = 0 };
        world.Wars[war.Id] = war;
        var people = world.Characters.Values.Where(c => c.IsActive).Take(2).ToList();
        var army = new Army
        {
            Id = world.Ids.Take(Army.Kind), Name = $"The Host of {a.Name}", RealmId = a.Id, WarId = war.Id, CommanderId = people[0].Id,
            Knights = { people[1].Id }, Levies = 900, MenAtArms = 100, RegionId = home.Id, Path = { target.Id }, TargetRegionId = target.Id,
            Objective = ArmyObjective.Siege,
        };
        world.Armies[army.Id] = army;
        target.OccupierRealmId = a.Id;
        log.Append(world.Date, new EventDraft("BattleFought", Actors: new Dictionary<string, EntityRef>
            { ["victor"] = a.Ref, ["vanquished"] = b.Ref, ["war"] = war.Ref }, Place: target.Ref));
        return (world, log, army, target);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheMap_ShowsHostsWithTheirRoad_Leaders_AndRecentBattles()
    {
        var (world, log, army, target) = AtWar();
        var map = MapScreen.Build(world, log);
        var host = Assert.Single(map.Armies!);
        Assert.Equal(army.Ref, host.Ref);
        Assert.Equal(2, host.Route.Count);
        Assert.Equal(MapPoint.Of(world, target.Ref), host.Route[^1]);
        Assert.Equal(world.Characters[army.CommanderId].Ref, host.Commander!.Ref);
        Assert.Single(host.Knights);
        Assert.Equal("attacker", host.Side);
        Assert.Equal(1000 + 1, host.Soldiers);
        Assert.Single(map.Battles!);
        Assert.Empty(MapScreen.Build(world, log, new WorldDate(-1)).Armies!);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Arrows_RunFromTheHostToItsTarget_WideningToAHead()
    {
        var (world, log, _, target) = AtWar();
        var map = MapScreen.Build(world, log);
        var arrow = Assert.Single(WarLayout.Arrows(map, world.Geography!));
        var goal = Projection.ToBlock(world.Geography!.Terrain, MapPoint.Of(world, target.Ref)!, 0);
        Assert.True(Vector2.Distance(new(arrow.Head.X, arrow.Head.Z), new(goal.X, goal.Z)) < 0.01f);
        Assert.All(arrow.Mesh.Positions, p => Assert.True(p.Y > 0));
        Assert.Equal(arrow.Mesh.Positions.Length, arrow.Mesh.Uvs!.Length);
        Assert.Equal(1, arrow.Mesh.Uvs.Max(u => u.Y), 3);
        Assert.All(arrow.Mesh.Indices, i => Assert.InRange(i, 0, arrow.Mesh.Positions.Length - 1));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheFront_RunsBetweenTheSides_AndOccupiedLandIsHatched()
    {
        var (world, log, _, target) = AtWar();
        var map = MapScreen.Build(world, log);
        var fronts = WarLayout.Fronts(map, world.Geography!);
        Assert.NotEmpty(fronts);
        Assert.Contains(fronts, f => f.Teeth.Triangles > 0);
        var hatch = WarLayout.Occupation(map, world.Geography!)!;
        var tiles = world.Geography!.RegionOf.Count(r => r == target.Id);
        Assert.Equal(tiles * 2, hatch.Triangles);
        Assert.NotEmpty(WarLayout.OccupationOutline(map, world.Geography!));
    }
}
```
